// engines/injector.js
// Injector script para interceptar window.fetch y detectar orderForm
// ---------------------------------------------
// AVISO: Este script es una plantilla. Por seguridad la ejecución
// automática del payload de compra está desactivada por defecto.
// Para activar la compra automática, configura `window.__BOT_AUTO_PURCHASE = true`
// y provee `window.__BOT_PURCHASE_PAYLOAD` con el JSON del payload.

(function() {
    console.log("⚡ [BOT] Modo Dios Activado: Hooking fetch...");

    const originalFetch = window.fetch;
    const nativeString = originalFetch.toString();

    // Configuración por defecto (puedes sobrescribirlas desde Python inyectando valores)
    window.__BOT_AUTO_PURCHASE = false; // cambiar a true para permitir el POST desde el browser
    window.__BOT_PURCHASE_PAYLOAD = null; // objeto JSON con payment/shipping si lo deseas

    let hasFired = false;

    window.fetch = async function(...args) {
        const response = await originalFetch(...args);
        const clone = response.clone();
        const url = args[0] && args[0].toString ? args[0].toString() : String(args[0]);

        try {
            if (url.includes('/api/checkout/pub/orderForm') && !hasFired) {
                clone.json().then(data => {
                    const items = data.items || [];
                    const totalizers = data.totalizers || [];
                    const priceCheck = totalizers.find(t => t.id === 'Items' && t.value > 0);

                    if (items.length > 0 && priceCheck) {
                        const eventDetail = { orderFormId: data.orderFormId, price: priceCheck.value, items };
                        console.log('🔥 [BOT] SEÑAL DETECTADA. PRECIO: ' + priceCheck.value);
                        console.log('🚀 [BOT_EVENT] ' + JSON.stringify(eventDetail));
                        hasFired = true;

                        // Sólo dispara compra si está habilitado explícitamente
                        if (window.__BOT_AUTO_PURCHASE && window.__BOT_PURCHASE_PAYLOAD) {
                            triggerPurchase(data.orderFormId, window.__BOT_PURCHASE_PAYLOAD);
                        }
                    }
                }).catch(()=>{});
            }
        } catch (e) {
            // No bloquear la navegación por errores del hook
            console.error('❌ [BOT] Error en hook fetch:', e);
        }

        return response;
    };

    // Engaño para que fetch.toString() parezca nativo
    window.fetch.toString = function() { return nativeString; };

    async function triggerPurchase(orderFormId, payload) {
        console.time('CheckoutTime');
        try {
            const purchaseReq = await originalFetch(`/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/${orderFormId}/transaction`, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: JSON.stringify(payload),
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
            });

            const result = await purchaseReq.json();
            console.log('🚀 [BOT] RESULTADO COMPRA:', result);
            console.timeEnd('CheckoutTime');

            if (result && result.redirectUrl) {
                window.location.href = result.redirectUrl;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('❌ [BOT] FALLO CRÍTICO:', e);
        }
    }

})();

FILE: engines\manual_login.py

import subprocess
import os
import time
import logging

log = logging.getLogger("manual_login")


def open_chrome_for_login(account_name: str, profile_dir: str) -> bool:
    """
    🔑 ABRE CHROME PARA MANUAL LOGIN - FUNCIONA EN THREADS
    
    ✅ CAMBIO CRÍTICO: No usar asyncio.run() en threads (causa deadlock en Windows)
    ✅ Usar subprocess.Popen() pero ESPERAR A QUE SE CIERRE
    ✅ Usa psutil para esperar que el proceso termine
    
    Args:
        account_name: Nombre de la cuenta (ej: "cuenta2")
        profile_dir: Ruta al perfil (relativa o absoluta, se convierte a absoluta)
    
    Returns:
        True si LOGIN completó correctamente, False si error
    """
    
    # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
    # PASO 1: RUTA ABSOLUTA
    # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
    abs_profile_path = os.path.abspath(profile_dir)
    
    print(f"\n[LOGIN {account_name}] 🔧 ABRIENDO CHROME")
    print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 📂 Perfil: {abs_profile_path}")
    
    try:
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        # PASO 2: LIMPIEZA - Lockfile y zombies
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        lockfile = os.path.join(abs_profile_path, "Lockfile")
        if os.path.exists(lockfile):
            try:
                os.remove(lockfile)
                print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 🔓 Lockfile eliminado")
            except PermissionError:
                print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] ⚠️  Lockfile en uso (continuando)")
        
        # Matar zombies
        try:
            ruta_wmic = abs_profile_path.replace("\\", "\\\\")
            cmd_kill = f'wmic process where "name=\'chrome.exe\' and CommandLine like \'%{ruta_wmic}%\'" call terminate'
            subprocess.run(
                cmd_kill,
                shell=True,
                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                timeout=5
            )
            print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 🎯 Procesos zombies eliminados")
            time.sleep(0.5)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] ⚠️  Error limpieza: {e}")
        
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        # PASO 3: CREAR CARPETA si no existe
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        os.makedirs(abs_profile_path, exist_ok=True)
        
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        # PASO 4: ENCONTRAR CHROME
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        chrome_paths = [
            r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe",
            r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe",
            os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe")
        ]
        
        chrome_exe = None
        for path in chrome_paths:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                chrome_exe = path
                break
        
        if not chrome_exe:
            print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] ❌ Chrome no encontrado")
            return False
        
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 🔍 Chrome encontrado")
        
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        # PASO 5: LANZAR CHROME Y ESPERAR A QUE SE CIERRE
        # ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
        args = [
            chrome_exe,
            f"--user-data-dir={abs_profile_path}",
            "--no-first-run",
            "--no-default-browser-check",
            "--start-maximized",
            "https://www.nike.cl/login"
        ]
        
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 🚀 Lanzando Chrome...")
        
        # ✅ Popen pero esperamos a que se cierre
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            args,
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP
        )
        
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] ✅ Chrome abierto (PID: {process.pid})")
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 👉 LOGUEATE EN NIKE")
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 👉 CIERRA CHROME CUANDO TERMINES")
        
        # ✅ ESPERAR A QUE EL PROCESO TERMINE
        # Esto bloquea hasta que el usuario cierre Chrome
        return_code = process.wait()
        
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] ✅ Chrome cerrado (exit code: {return_code})")
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] 💾 Cookies guardadas en disco")
        
        log.info(f"[{account_name}] Chrome cerrado. Cookies en {abs_profile_path}")
        return True
    
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[LOGIN {account_name}] ❌ ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
        log.error(f"[{account_name}] {e}", exc_info=True)
        return False

# engines/manual_review.py
"""
Abre navegador para revisión manual del checkout sin automátizar nada.
Permite al humano:
- Revisar datos
- Confirmar dirección
- Verificar precio
- Sin correr el bot
"""
import logging
from typing import Optional
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright, Browser, BrowserContext, Page


class ManualReviewBrowser:
    """
    Abre Chrome con sesión real para revisión manual.
    
    ✅ Abre Chrome
    ✅ Inyecta cookies válidas
    ✅ Navega a checkout
    ✅ No automátiza nada
    ✅ Humano revisa
    """

    def __init__(self, log: Optional[object] = None):
        self.log = log or logging.getLogger("ManualReviewBrowser")
        self.browser: Optional[Browser] = None
        self.context: Optional[BrowserContext] = None
        self.page: Optional[Page] = None

    def open_for_review(
        self,
        session,  # requests.Session con cookies VTEX
        url: str = "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/#/payment",
        headless: bool = False
    ) -> Optional[Page]:
        """
        Abre navegador con sesión real para revisión manual (SYNC).
        
        Args:
            session: requests.Session con cookies VTEX válidas
            url: URL a navegar (default: checkout payment)
            headless: False = abrir visible (recomendado)
            
        Returns:
            Page (Playwright) para que humano lo maneje
            None si falla
        """
        try:
            self.log.info(f"🔍 Abriendo navegador para revisión manual...")
            
            # Usar sync_playwright (no async)
            playwright = sync_playwright().start()
            self.browser = playwright.chromium.launch(headless=headless)
            self.context = self.browser.new_context()
            
            # 2. Inyectar cookies reales
            self.log.info(f"   Inyectando sesión VTEX...")
            cookies_to_inject = []
            
            for cookie in session.cookies:
                # Convertir RequestsCookie → PlaywrightCookie
                pw_cookie = {
                    "name": cookie.name,
                    "value": cookie.value,
                    "domain": cookie.domain or ".nike.cl",
                    "path": cookie.path or "/",
                }
                # Agregar expiry si existe
                if hasattr(cookie, "expires") and cookie.expires:
                    pw_cookie["expires"] = cookie.expires
                
                cookies_to_inject.append(pw_cookie)
            
            if cookies_to_inject:
                self.context.add_cookies(cookies_to_inject)
                self.log.info(f"✅ Inyectadas {len(cookies_to_inject)} cookies")
            else:
                self.log.warning(f"⚠️ No hay cookies para inyectar")
            
            # 3. Crear página
            self.page = self.context.new_page()
            
            # 4. Navegar a checkout
            self.log.info(f"   Navegando a {url}...")
            self.page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle")
            self.log.info(f"✅ Página cargada")
            
            # 5. Mostrar a humano
            self.log.info(f"🟡 Revisión manual abierta → humano puede revisar")
            self.log.info(f"   NO automátizando nada")
            self.log.info(f"   Humano puede: revisar datos, confirmar dirección, etc.")
            
            return self.page
        
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error abriendo navegador: {e}")
            if self.browser:
                self.browser.close()
            return None

    async def close(self):
        """Cierra navegador cuando humano termina"""
        try:
            if self.page:
                self.page.close()
            if self.context:
                self.context.close()
            if self.browser:
                self.browser.close()
            self.log.info("✅ Navegador de revisión cerrado")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"Error cerrando navegador: {e}")


def open_manual_review_sync(session, log: Optional[object] = None) -> Optional[Page]:
    """
    Abre navegador sincrónico para revisión manual.
    
    Uso:
    ```python
    page = open_manual_review_sync(account.session)
    # Humano revisa en Chrome (página se mantiene abierta)
    ```
    """
    logger = log or logging.getLogger("ManualReview")
    reviewer = ManualReviewBrowser(log=logger)
    
    try:
        page = reviewer.open_for_review(session, headless=False)
        # NO cerrar automáticamente, dejar que humano lo cierre
        return page
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error en revisión manual: {e}")
        return None

FILE: engines\modal_handlers.py

# engines/modal_handlers.py
"""
Handlers para modales comunes en Nike checkout.
"""
import logging
from typing import Optional
from playwright.sync_api import Page, TimeoutError as PlaywrightTimeout


class CheckoutModals:
    """Maneja modales que aparecen durante el checkout de Nike."""

    def __init__(self, log: Optional[object] = None):
        self.log = log or logging.getLogger("CheckoutModals")

    def handle_confirm_data_modal(self, page: Page, timeout_sec: int = 3) -> bool:
        """
        Maneja el modal "¡CONFIRMA TUS DATOS!" que aparece en Nike CL.
        
        ✅ Este modal es NORMAL y ESPERADO.
        ✅ Debe ser aceptado SIEMPRE antes de elegir método de pago.
        
        Args:
            page: Playwright Page
            timeout_sec: Timeout en segundos
            
        Returns:
            True si modal fue encontrado y aceptado
            False si modal no apareció (también OK)
        """
        try:
            self.log.info("   🔍 Buscando modal 'Confirma tus datos'...")
            
            # Buscar el modal (puede tener varios selectores)
            modal_selectors = [
                "text=¡CONFIRMA TUS DATOS!",
                "text=Confirma tus datos",
                "text=Confirmar datos",
                "[data-testid='confirm-data-modal']",
            ]
            
            button_selectors = [
                "text=Continuar compra",
                "text=Continuar",
                "button:has-text('Continuar')",
            ]
            
            # 1. Esperar que el modal sea visible
            modal_found = False
            for selector in modal_selectors:
                try:
                    page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=timeout_sec * 1000)
                    self.log.info(f"   ✅ Modal encontrado: {selector}")
                    modal_found = True
                    break
                except PlaywrightTimeout:
                    continue
            
            if not modal_found:
                self.log.debug("   ⏭️  Modal 'Confirma tus datos' no apareció (OK)")
                return False
            
            # 2. Clickear botón "Continuar compra"
            for btn_selector in button_selectors:
                try:
                    page.click(btn_selector)
                    self.log.info(f"✅ Modal aceptado (botón: {btn_selector})")
                    
                    # Esperar a que el modal se cierre
                    page.wait_for_function(
                        """() => !document.body.innerText.includes('CONFIRMA TUS DATOS')""",
                        timeout=2000
                    )
                    return True
                except:
                    continue
            
            self.log.warning("⚠️ Modal encontrado pero no se pudo aceptar")
            return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error en handle_confirm_data_modal: {e}")
            return False

    def select_transfer_payment(self, page: Page, timeout_sec: int = 5) -> bool:
        """
        Selecciona "Transferencia con tu banco" como método de pago.
        
        Args:
            page: Playwright Page
            timeout_sec: Timeout en segundos
            
        Returns:
            True si se seleccionó exitosamente
        """
        try:
            self.log.info("   Seleccionando 'Transferencia con tu banco'...")
            
            selectors = [
                "text=Transferencia con tu banco",
                "text=Transferencia",
                "[data-testid='transfer-payment']",
                "label:has-text('Transferencia')",
            ]
            
            for selector in selectors:
                try:
                    page.click(selector)
                    self.log.info(f"✅ Método seleccionado: {selector}")
                    return True
                except:
                    continue
            
            self.log.warning("⚠️ No se pudo seleccionar 'Transferencia con tu banco'")
            return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error seleccionando método: {e}")
            return False

    def click_finalize_payment(self, page: Page, timeout_sec: int = 5) -> bool:
        """
        Clickea botón "Finalizar compra" UNA SOLA VEZ.
        
        Espera a que el botón esté habilitado antes de hacer click.
        
        Args:
            page: Playwright Page
            timeout_sec: Timeout para esperar botón habilitado
            
        Returns:
            True si se clickeó exitosamente
        """
        try:
            self.log.info("   Esperando botón 'Finalizar compra' habilitado...")
            
            # Esperar a que el botón exista y esté habilitado
            page.wait_for_function(
                """() => {
                    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('button');
                    const finalize = [...buttons].find(b => 
                        b.innerText.includes('Finalizar') && 
                        !b.disabled
                    );
                    return finalize != null;
                }""",
                timeout=timeout_sec * 1000
            )
            
            self.log.info("✅ Botón 'Finalizar compra' habilitado")
            
            # Click UNA SOLA VEZ
            page.click("button:has-text('Finalizar')")
            self.log.info("✅ Pago iniciado (botón clickeado)")
            
            return True
            
        except PlaywrightTimeout:
            self.log.error("❌ Timeout esperando botón 'Finalizar compra'")
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error clickeando 'Finalizar': {e}")
            return False

# engines/orderform_watcher.py
"""
OrderForm Watcher - Versión concreta.
Pragma: sin Over-Engineering.
Solo watch de pricing (PRICING_WAIT + PRICING_FROZEN).
"""
import time
import logging


class OrderFormWatcher:
    """Observa OrderForm para congelación de precio."""

    def __init__(self, session, log=None):
        """
        Args:
            session: requests.Session con cookies VTEX
            log: logger (opcional)
        """
        self.session = session
        self.log = log or logging.getLogger("OrderFormWatcher")

    def fetch(self) -> dict:
        """Obtiene OrderForm actual."""
        r = self.session.get("/api/checkout/pub/orderForm")
        r.raise_for_status()
        return r.json()

    def is_ready(self, of: dict) -> bool:
        """¿OrderForm está listo para congelar?"""
        try:
            # OrderFormId
            if not of.get("orderFormId"):
                self.log.debug("❌ sin orderFormId")
                return False

            # Items
            items = of.get("items", [])
            if not items:
                self.log.debug("❌ items vacío")
                return False

            # Price check
            for i, item in enumerate(items):
                price = item.get("price", 0)
                if price <= 0:
                    self.log.debug(f"❌ item[{i}] price <= 0")
                    return False

            # Total check
            totalizers = of.get("totalizers", [])
            total = sum(t.get("value", 0) for t in totalizers)
            if total <= 0:
                self.log.debug(f"❌ total <= 0")
                return False

            # Shipping check
            logistics = of.get("shippingData", {}).get("logisticsInfo", [])
            if not logistics or not logistics[0].get("selectedSla"):
                self.log.debug("❌ sin SLA seleccionado")
                return False

            # Payment systems
            if not of.get("paymentData", {}).get("paymentSystems"):
                self.log.debug("❌ sin payment systems")
                return False

            return True

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.debug(f"❌ error validando: {e}")
            return False

    def wait_until_ready(self, timeout=8) -> dict:
        """
        PRICING_WAIT → ready signal
        Espera a que OrderForm esté listo.
        
        ✅ MEJORA E4: Circuit breaker si VTEX no responde (500, 503)
        
        Bloquea si:
        - price == 0
        - total == 0
        - sin SLA
        
        Lanza:
        - TimeoutError si timeout
        - RuntimeError si VTEX está caído (circuit break)
        
        Retorna: OrderForm cuando is_ready() == True
        """
        start = time.time()
        attempt = 0
        consecutive_errors = 0

        while time.time() - start < timeout:
            attempt += 1
            try:
                # Fetch con validación de error
                r = self.session.get("/api/checkout/pub/orderForm")
                
                # CIRCUIT BREAKER: Si VTEX devuelve 5xx → fail fast
                if r.status_code >= 500:
                    self.log.error(f"❌ VTEX unavailable ({r.status_code})")
                    raise RuntimeError(f"VTEX circuit open: {r.status_code}")
                
                r.raise_for_status()
                of = r.json()
                consecutive_errors = 0  # Reset counter
                
                if self.is_ready(of):
                    elapsed = time.time() - start
                    self.log.info(f"✅ OrderForm ready en {elapsed:.1f}s (intento {attempt})")
                    return of

            except RuntimeError as e:
                # Circuit break: propagar error inmediatamente
                if "circuit open" in str(e).lower():
                    self.log.error(f"🔴 VTEX circuit break: {e}")
                    raise
                consecutive_errors += 1
                if consecutive_errors >= 3:
                    self.log.error(f"❌ 3 errores consecutivos, abortando")
                    raise
            except Exception as e:
                consecutive_errors += 1
                self.log.debug(f"⚠️ Error fetch (intento {attempt}): {e}")
                if consecutive_errors >= 3:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Demasiados errores: {e}")

            time.sleep(0.25)

        elapsed = time.time() - start
        raise TimeoutError(f"OrderForm no ready después {elapsed:.1f}s ({attempt} intentos)")

    def freeze(self, window_ms=400) -> dict:
        """
        PRICING_FROZEN → congelación confirmada
        Implementa freeze window.
        
        Captura 2 snapshots con intervalo.
        Si son iguales (pricing, SLA) → congelado.
        Si cambió → lanza RuntimeError.
        
        Args:
            window_ms: intervalo entre capturas (default 400ms)
        
        Retorna: OrderForm congelado
        Lanza: RuntimeError si no está estable
        """
        window = window_ms / 1000.0

        self.log.info(f"🔒 Capturando snapshot A...")
        snap1 = self.fetch()

        time.sleep(window)

        self.log.info(f"🔒 Capturando snapshot B (después {window_ms}ms)...")
        snap2 = self.fetch()

        # Comparar totalizers
        total1 = snap1.get("totalizers", [])
        total2 = snap2.get("totalizers", [])

        if total1 != total2:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Totalizers cambió: {total1} vs {total2}")

        # Comparar items prices
        items1 = snap1.get("items", [])
        items2 = snap2.get("items", [])

        for i, (item1, item2) in enumerate(zip(items1, items2)):
            if item1.get("price") != item2.get("price"):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"item[{i}].price cambió: {item1.get('price')} vs {item2.get('price')}"
                )

        # Comparar SLA
        sla1 = snap1.get("shippingData", {}).get("logisticsInfo", [{}])[0].get("selectedSla")
        sla2 = snap2.get("shippingData", {}).get("logisticsInfo", [{}])[0].get("selectedSla")

        if sla1 != sla2:
            raise RuntimeError(f"SLA cambió: {sla1} vs {sla2}")

        self.log.info(f"✅ Pricing congelado")
        return snap2

    def get_total(self, of: dict) -> int:
        """Retorna total en centavos (ej: 12299000 = $122.990)"""
        return sum(t.get("value", 0) for t in of.get("totalizers", []))

    def display_price(self, total_cents: int) -> str:
        """Formato para display: $122.990"""
        return f"${total_cents // 100:,}".replace(",", ".")

# engines/payment_engine.py
"""
Payment Engine - Versión concreta.
Pragma: sin Over-Engineering.
Solo inyecta payment bancario en orderForm.
"""
import logging


class PaymentEngine:
    """Inyecta payment method bancario en orderForm."""

    def __init__(self, session, log=None):
        """
        Args:
            session: requests.Session con cookies VTEX
            log: logger (opcional)
        """
        self.session = session
        self.log = log or logging.getLogger("PaymentEngine")

    def set_bank_payment(self, order_form_id: str) -> dict:
        """
        Inyecta payment method bancario.
        
        PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED:
        - Backend call (sin UI)
        - Inyecta paymentSystem = "bankInvoice" (AJUSTA según Nike CL)
        - VTEX retorna orderForm actualizado
        
        Args:
            order_form_id: orderFormId desde OrderForm
        
        Retorna: OrderForm actualizado
        Lanza: Exception si falla
        """
        self.log.info(f"🏦 Inyectando payment: bankInvoice")

        payload = {
            "payments": [
                {
                    "paymentSystem": "bankInvoice",  # 📌 AJUSTA según Nike CL
                    "value": None,
                    "installments": 1
                }
            ]
        }

        url = f"/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{order_form_id}/attachments/paymentData"
        
        try:
            r = self.session.post(url, json=payload)
            r.raise_for_status()
            of = r.json()
            self.log.info(f"✅ Payment inyectado")
            return of
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error inyectando: {e}")
            raise

# engines/playwright_engine.py
import asyncio
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Tuple

from playwright.async_api import async_playwright, BrowserContext, Page
from utils import setup_logger


class PlaywrightEngine:
    """
    Controla el navegador (Chromium) para el PASO FINAL de checkout/pago.
    Usa un perfil persistente para mantener sesión Nike.
    """
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, profile_path: str, headless: bool = False, log_level: str = "INFO",
                 injector_path: str | None = None, auto_purchase_payload: dict | None = None,
                 auto_purchase: bool = False):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.profile_path = profile_path
        self.headless = headless
        self.log = setup_logger("PlaywrightEngine", log_level)
        self._playwright = None
        self._context: BrowserContext | None = None
        # Injector options
        self._injector_path = injector_path
        self._auto_purchase_payload = auto_purchase_payload
        self._auto_purchase = auto_purchase

    async def __aenter__(self):
        self._playwright = await async_playwright().start()
        self.log.info("Lanzando Chromium persistente con Playwright...")
        self._context = await self._playwright.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
            user_data_dir=self.profile_path,
            headless=self.headless,
            args=[
                "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
                "--no-sandbox",
                "--disable-infobars"
            ]
        )

        # Añadir script de inyección si existe
        try:
            injector_file = Path(self._injector_path) if self._injector_path else Path(__file__).parent / "injector.js"
            if injector_file.exists():
                script_text = injector_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

                # Valores inyectables (auto purchase flag + payload)
                prelude = f"window.__BOT_AUTO_PURCHASE = {str(bool(self._auto_purchase)).lower()};"
                payload_json = json.dumps(self._auto_purchase_payload) if self._auto_purchase_payload is not None else "null"
                prelude += f" window.__BOT_PURCHASE_PAYLOAD = {payload_json};\n"

                await self._context.add_init_script(script=prelude + "\n" + script_text)
                self.log.info(f"Injector script añadido desde: {injector_file}")
            else:
                self.log.debug(f"No se encontró injector script en: {injector_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.warning(f"No se pudo añadir injector script: {e}")

        # Bloqueamos imágenes para ahorrar datos y acelerar
        async def route_handler(route):
            req = route.request
            if req.resource_type in ["image", "media", "font"]:
                await route.abort()
            else:
                await route.continue_()

        await self._context.route("**/*", route_handler)

        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        if self._context:
            self.log.info("Cerrando contexto Playwright...")
            await self._context.close()
        if  self._playwright:
            await self._playwright.stop()

    @property
    def context(self) -> BrowserContext:
        if not self._context:
            raise RuntimeError("Contexto Playwright no inicializado")
        return self._context

    async def open_checkout_payment(self) -> Tuple[Page, BrowserContext]:
        """
        Abre directamente la página de pago del checkout VTEX/Nike.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/checkout/#/payment"
        self.log.info(f"Abrir checkout payment: {url}")

        # Reusamos la primera página disponible o creamos una nueva
        pages = self.context.pages
        page = pages[0] if pages else await self.context.new_page()

        # Capturamos logs de consola para detectar eventos del injector
        def _console_cb(msg):
            try:
                text = msg.text() if callable(msg.text) else msg.text
            except Exception:
                text = str(msg)

            # Log general
            self.log.info(f"[PAGE] {text}")

            # Detectar eventos estructurados emitidos por el injector
            marker = '🚀 [BOT_EVENT] '
            if marker in text:
                try:
                    payload = text.split(marker, 1)[1]
                    detail = json.loads(payload)
                    self.log.info(f"[BOT_EVENT] {detail}")
                except Exception:
                    self.log.warning("No se pudo parsear BOT_EVENT JSON")

        page.on("console", _console_cb)

        await page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle")
        self.log.info("Checkout payment cargado.")
        return page, self.context

    async def ensure_logged_in(self):
        """
        Aquí podrías verificar si estás logueado, y si no, hacer login una vez.
        Por ahora solo lo dejamos como hook para el futuro.
        """
        self.log.info("Verificando sesión Nike (hook).")
        # TODO: implementar detector de login si hace falta
        return
    
    async def warm_up_pdp(self, sku: str, session_store=None, timeout: int = 30) -> bool:
        """
        Warm-up PDP - Visita página de producto para que VTEX contextualize el SKU.
        
        Usa SMART WAITS (3 señales válidas):
        1. Runtime VTEX listo
        2. Estado comercial (Apollo) cargado
        3. orderForm API respondió OK
        
        Si session_store se proporciona, actualiza cookies durante la carga.
        
        Returns: True si warm-up OK, False si timeout
        """
        self.log.info(f"🧠 Warm-up PDP: sku={sku}")
        
        pages = self.context.pages
        page = pages[0] if pages else await self.context.new_page()
        
        # Handlers de respuesta para capturar cookies y orderForm
        def _response_handler(response):
            # Capturar orderForm OK
            if "/api/checkout/pub/orderForm" in response.url and response.status == 200:
                self.log.info(f"   ✅ orderForm API respondió OK")
        
        # Listeners para actualizar cookies si tenemos session_store
        async def _route_handler(route):
            # Actualizar cookies que salen en requests
            if session_store:
                # Los headers de request incluyen cookies, pero nos interesan las responses
                pass
            await route.continue_()
        
        page.on("response", _response_handler)
        
        # PDP URL
        pdp_url = f"{self.base_url}/product/{sku}"
        self.log.info(f"   Navegando a: {pdp_url}")
        
        try:
            await page.goto(pdp_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=timeout * 1000)
            
            # 🎯 SMART WAIT #1: Runtime VTEX listo
            self.log.info(f"   Esperando __RUNTIME__ de VTEX...")
            try:
                await page.wait_for_function(
                    "() => window.__RUNTIME__ && window.__RUNTIME__.account && window.__RUNTIME__.workspace",
                    timeout=timeout * 1000
                )
                self.log.info(f"      ✅ Runtime VTEX OK")
            except Exception as e:
                self.log.warning(f"      ⚠️  Runtime VTEX timeout: {e}")
            
            # 🎯 SMART WAIT #2: Estado comercial (Apollo) cargado
            self.log.info(f"   Esperando Apollo state...")
            try:
                await page.wait_for_function(
                    "() => window.__APOLLO_STATE__ && Object.keys(window.__APOLLO_STATE__).length > 10",
                    timeout=timeout * 1000
                )
                self.log.info(f"      ✅ Apollo state OK")
            except Exception as e:
                self.log.warning(f"      ⚠️  Apollo state timeout: {e}")
            
            # 🎯 SMART WAIT #3: orderForm API respondió (monitoreamos responses)
            self.log.info(f"   Esperando orderForm API...")
            orderform_received = False
            
            def check_orderform(response):
                nonlocal orderform_received
                if "/api/checkout/pub/orderForm" in response.url and response.status == 200:
                    orderform_received = True
            
            # Cambiar handler
            page.remove_listener("response", _response_handler)
            page.on("response", check_orderform)
            
            # Esperar un poco a que llegue
            start = asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
            while not orderform_received and (asyncio.get_event_loop().time() - start) < timeout:
                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
            
            if orderform_received:
                self.log.info(f"      ✅ orderForm API OK")
            else:
                self.log.warning(f"      ⚠️  orderForm API no llegó en {timeout}s")
            
            # Capturar cookies actualizadas si tenemos session_store
            if session_store:
                try:
                    cookies_dict = await page.context.cookies()
                    cookie_map = {c["name"]: c["value"] for c in cookies_dict}
                    session_store.update_cookies(cookie_map)
                    self.log.info(f"   ✅ Cookies actualizadas en SessionStore ({len(cookie_map)} items)")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.log.warning(f"   ⚠️  Error actualizando cookies: {e}")
            
            self.log.info(f"   ✅ PDP warm-up OK")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"   ❌ PDP warm-up falló: {e}")
            return False
        finally:
            page.remove_listener("response", check_orderform)

FILE: engines\playwright_engine_v2.py
======================================================================

"""
PlaywrightEngine v2 - Versión Hardened (Mejoras #5)

Responsabilidad única: Abrir Chrome con perfil persistente.
Sin asyncio, sin deadlocks, sin sorpresas.
"""
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright



class PlaywrightEngine:
    """Motor limpio para dos casos:
    1. LOGIN: Usuario loguea manualmente
    2. CHECKOUT: Bot navega con sesión pre-cargada
    """
    
    def __init__(self, profile_path: str):
        """
        Args:
            profile_path: Ruta a auth/cuentaX/profile_run (debe ser ABSOLUTA)
        """
        self.profile_path = profile_path
        
        # DEBUG: Mostrar exactamente qué path estamos usando
        print(f"[DEBUG] Using profile_run: {self.profile_path}")
        print(f"[DEBUG] Exists?: {os.path.exists(self.profile_path)}")
        
        # CRÍTICO: Crear si no existe (en caso de inicialización)
        Path(profile_path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def launch_manual_login(self) -> bool:
        """
        Abre Chrome para que el USUARIO se loguee manualmente.
        BLOQUEANTE hasta que el usuario CIERRE el navegador.
        
        ⚠️ CRÍTICO: Debe ejecutarse en THREAD SEPARADO
        (No congelar la UI mientras Chrome está abierto)
        
        Retorna: True si completó sin errores
        """
        print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🔴 Iniciando LOGIN manual...")
        
        try:
            # 🧹 PASO 1: LIMPIAR SingletonLock (Anti-Zombie)
            lock_file = os.path.join(self.profile_path, "SingletonLock")
            if os.path.exists(lock_file):
                try:
                    os.remove(lock_file)
                    print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] 🧹 SingletonLock eliminado (desbloqueado perfil)")
                except Exception as lock_err:
                    print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ⚠️ No pude borrar SingletonLock: {lock_err}")
                    print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ⚠️ (Chrome quizás sigue abierto en otra instancia)")
            
            # 🟢 PASO 2: LANZAR NAVEGADOR
            with sync_playwright() as p:
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ⚙️ Lanzando contexto persistente en {self.profile_path}")
                
                browser = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                    user_data_dir=self.profile_path,
                    headless=False,
                    viewport=None,
                    args=[
                        "--start-maximized",
                        "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
                        "--no-first-run",
                        "--no-default-browser-check"
                    ]
                )
                
                page = browser.pages[0] if browser.pages else browser.new_page()
                
                print("[PlaywrightEngine] ✅ Chrome abierto en modo persistente")
                print("[PlaywrightEngine] 👤 Navegando a Nike login...")
                
                try:
                    page.goto("https://www.nike.cl/account/login", timeout=60000)
                except Exception as goto_err:
                    print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ⚠️ Error navegando: {goto_err}")
                
                print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🔐 LOGUEATE EN NIKE Y CIERRA EL NAVEGADOR")
                print("[PlaywrightEngine] (Las cookies se guardarán automáticamente)")
                
                # ⛔ ESPERA infinita hasta que usuario cierre Chrome
                try:
                    page.wait_for_event("close", timeout=0)
                except Exception:
                    pass  # El navegador se cerró
                
                print("[PlaywrightEngine] ✅ Chrome cerrado por usuario")
                
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] 💾 Cookies guardadas en disco")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ❌ Error en LOGIN: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            return False
        
        finally:
            # CRÍTICO: Asegurar que Playwright se limpie
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🔒 Cerrando contexto de Playwright...")


    def launch_checkout_mission(self, sku_url: str) -> bool:
        r"""
        Abre Chrome para CHECKOUT (sesión ya precargada).
        
        SOLO se ejecuta cuando PRICE_LOCKED == True.
        BLOQUEANTE hasta que usuario pague o cierre manualmente.
        
        Args:
            sku_url: URL magic link con SKU (ej: https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add?sku=123...)
            
        Retorna: True si navegó correctamente (o False si error)
        
        🔴 CRÍTICO: Este método DEBE BLOQUEAR el hilo.
        """
        print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🚀 Iniciando CHECKOUT (Chrome real)...")
        print(f"[DEBUG] Using profile_run: {self.profile_path}")
        print(f"[DEBUG] Exists?: {os.path.isdir(self.profile_path)}")
        
        # ABORT si profile_run no existe
        if not os.path.isdir(self.profile_path):
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ❌ ABORT: profile_run NO existe: {self.profile_path}")
            return False
        
        print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] Magic URL: {sku_url}")

        try:
            # 🔴 CRÍTICO: NO usar with - eso cierra Chrome inmediatamente
            # Usar .start() para mantener el proceso vivo indefinidamente
            p = sync_playwright().start()
            
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] ⚙️ Lanzando navegador persistente...")
            
            browser = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=self.profile_path,
                headless=False,
                viewport=None,
                args=[
                    "--start-maximized",
                    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled"
                ]
            )
            
            page = browser.pages[0] if browser.pages else browser.new_page()
            
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🌐 Navegando a magic link...")
            page.goto(sku_url, timeout=60000)
            
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] ✅ Chrome ABIERTO. Control humano.")
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] 💳 Completa el pago en tu banco")
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] ❗ NO se cerrará automáticamente")
            
            # 🔴 BLOQUEO ABSOLUTO - Mantener Playwright y Chrome vivos indefinidamente
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] ⏳ Esperando cierre manual...")
            try:
                while True:
                    time.sleep(1)
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🛑 Cierre manual detectado (Ctrl+C)")
            
            print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🏁 Fin de sesión de checkout")
            return True
                
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ❌ Error en CHECKOUT: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            return False

    def validate_session_real(self) -> bool:
        """
        Mejora #1: Validación REAL de sesión (no solo check de archivo).
        
        Abre Chrome en headless y navega a /checkout.
        Si se redirige a /login → sesión expirada.
        Si carga → sesión válida.
        
        Retorna: True si sesión es válida
        """
        print("[PlaywrightEngine] 🔍 Validando sesión...")
        
        try:
            # Check 1: ¿Existe el directorio?
            if not Path(self.profile_path).exists():
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngine]    ❌ Perfil no existe")
                return False
            
            # Check 2: ¿Hay cookies?
            cookies_db = Path(self.profile_path) / "Default" / "Cookies"
            if not cookies_db.exists():
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngine]    ❌ Cookies SQLite no encontrado")
                return False
            
            # Check 3: VALIDACIÓN DURA - Navega en headless
            print("[PlaywrightEngine]    🔍 Navegando en headless...")
            with sync_playwright() as p:
                browser = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                    user_data_dir=self.profile_path,
                    headless=True,  # ← Rápido
                    timeout=30000
                )
                page = browser.pages[0] if browser.pages else browser.new_page()
                
                try:
                    page.goto(
                        "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/#/cart",
                        timeout=30000
                    )
                    
                    current_url = page.url.lower()
                    
                    # ¿Se redirigió a login?
                    if "login" in current_url or "/account" in current_url:
                        print("[PlaywrightEngine]    ⚠️ Sesión expirada (redirigió a login)")
                        return False
                    
                    print("[PlaywrightEngine]    ✅ Sesión válida (navegación OK)")
                    return True
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[PlaywrightEngine]    ⚠️ Error: {e}")
                    return False
                finally:
                    browser.close()
                    
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngine] ❌ Error en validación: {e}")
            return False


======================================================================
FILE: engines\playwright_engine_v3.py
======================================================================

"""
PlaywrightEngine v3 - Versión GLOTÓN (Reutilización de contexto)

Permite atacar múltiples links consecutivos con la MISMA cuenta.
Reutiliza el navegador en lugar de cerrarlo después de cada compra.

Uso:
- Estrategia 1 (Ejército): Múltiples cuentas, cada una su propio engine
- Estrategia 2 (Glotón): UNA cuenta, múltiples pestañas/contextos

Este archivo es alternativa a playwright_engine_v2.py
"""
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright


class PlaywrightEngineV3:
    """
    Motor mejorado que REUTILIZA el navegador.
    
    Perfecto para:
    - Atacar múltiples SKUs con la misma sesión
    - Ahorrar tiempo (no reinicia Chrome cada vez)
    - Mantener cookies/sesión activas
    """
    
    def __init__(self, profile_path: str):
        """
        Args:
            profile_path: Ruta a auth/cuentaX/profile_pw
        """
        self.profile_path = profile_path
        Path(profile_path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Estado del contexto persistente
        self.playwright = None
        self.browser_context = None
    
    def cleanup_singleton_lock(self):
        """Limpiar SingletonLock antes de abrir"""
        lock_file = os.path.join(self.profile_path, "SingletonLock")
        if os.path.exists(lock_file):
            try:
                os.remove(lock_file)
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] 🧹 SingletonLock eliminado")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ⚠️ No pude borrar lock: {e}")
    
    def ensure_browser_open(self):
        """
        Garantiza que el navegador esté abierto.
        Si ya está abierto, NO lo reabre.
        """
        if self.browser_context is not None:
            try:
                # Verificar que sigue vivo (hacer prueba simple)
                pages = self.browser_context.pages
                if pages:
                    print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ♻️ Reutilizando navegador existente ({len(pages)} tabs)")
                    return True
            except Exception:
                pass  # Navegador muerto, reiniciar
        
        # Abrir navegador fresco
        print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] 🟢 Abriendo navegador persistente...")
        
        self.cleanup_singleton_lock()
        
        try:
            self.playwright = sync_playwright().start()
            
            self.browser_context = self.playwright.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=self.profile_path,
                headless=False,
                viewport=None,
                args=[
                    "--start-maximized",
                    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
                    "--no-first-run",
                    "--no-default-browser-check"
                ]
            )
            
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ✅ Navegador abierto")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ❌ Error abriendo navegador: {e}")
            self.browser_context = None
            self.playwright = None
            return False
    
    def new_tab(self, url: str):
        """
        Abre una nueva pestaña en el navegador existente.
        
        Args:
            url: URL a cargar
            
        Returns:
            Page object de Playwright
        """
        if not self.ensure_browser_open():
            return None
        
        try:
            page = self.browser_context.new_page()
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] 📄 Nueva pestaña abierta")
            page.goto(url, timeout=60000)
            return page
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ❌ Error abriendo pestaña: {e}")
            return None
    
    def launch_checkout_mission(self, sku_url: str) -> bool:
        """
        Ejecuta compra en nueva pestaña (reutiliza navegador).
        
        Args:
            sku_url: URL magic link con SKU
            
        Returns:
            True si se navegó correctamente
        """
        print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] 🚀 Checkout mission: {sku_url}")
        
        page = self.new_tab(sku_url)
        if not page:
            return False
        
        try:
            # Aquí va tu lógica de checkout
            # Por ahora solo navegamos a /checkout/#/payment explícitamente
            try:
                page.goto("https://www.nike.cl/checkout/#/payment", timeout=30000)
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ✅ Navegado a checkout")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ⚠️ Error navegando a payment: {e}")
            
            # NO cerrar la página (dejarla viva para el usuario)
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ❌ Error en checkout: {e}")
            return False
    
    def close(self):
        """Cerrar navegador correctamente"""
        try:
            if self.browser_context:
                self.browser_context.close()
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] 🔒 Contexto cerrado")
            
            if self.playwright:
                self.playwright.stop()
                print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] 🔒 Playwright parado")
                
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PlaywrightEngineV3] ⚠️ Error cerrando: {e}")
        
        finally:
            self.browser_context = None
            self.playwright = None
    
    def __del__(self):
        """Limpiar al destruir objeto"""
        self.close()


# Test
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    
    profile = "auth/test_glotton/profile_pw"
    engine = PlaywrightEngineV3(profile)
    
    print("\n" + "="*60)
    print("TEST: PlaywrightEngineV3 (Glotón)")
    print("="*60)
    
    # Simular múltiples SKUs
    skus = [
        "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add?sku=123&qty=1",
        "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add?sku=456&qty=1",
        "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add?sku=789&qty=1"
    ]
    
    for i, sku in enumerate(skus, 1):
        print(f"\n[TEST] Atacando SKU {i}/3...")
        engine.launch_checkout_mission(sku)

    try:
        input("[TEST] Presiona ENTER para cerrar...")
    except:
        pass
    
    engine.close()

FILE: engines\request_engine.py

import json
import requests
from utils import setup_logger


class RequestEngine:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, cookies_file="cookies_extracted.json", log_level="INFO"):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.session = requests.Session()
        self.log = setup_logger("RequestEngine", log_level)

        # Headers iguales a Chrome real
        self.session.headers.update({
            "User-Agent": (
                "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 "
                "(KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36"
            ),
            "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*",
            "Referer": "https://www.nike.cl/",
            "Origin": "https://www.nike.cl",
        })

        # ==============================
        # 🔥 CARGAR COOKIES REALES
        # ==============================
        try:
            with open(cookies_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                cookies = json.load(f)

            count = 0
            for name, value in cookies.items():
                if value:
                    # añadir cookies para cada dominio relevante
                    for domain in [".nike.cl", "www.nike.cl", ".checkout.vtex.com"]:
                        self.session.cookies.set(name, value, domain=domain)
                    count += 1

            self.log.info(f"Cookies cargadas en Requests: {count} items.")

        except FileNotFoundError:
            self.log.error("No existe cookies_extracted.json. Corre cookie_extractor.py")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"Error cargando cookies: {e}")

    # ==============================
    # FUNCIONES BACKEND VTEX
    # ==============================
    def get_order_form(self):
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm"
        self.log.info(f"GET OrderForm → {url}")
        r = self.session.get(url)
        r.raise_for_status()
        return r.json()

    def add_to_cart(self, sku_id: str, seller: str):
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/items"
        self.log.info(f"POST AddToCart → {url}")

        payload = {
            "items": [
                {"id": sku_id, "quantity": 1, "seller": seller}
            ]
        }

        r = self.session.post(url, json=payload)
        r.raise_for_status()
        return r.json()

    def clear_cart(self):
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/items/removeAll"
        self.log.info(f"DELETE ClearCart → {url}")
        r = self.session.post(url, json={"items": []})
        r.raise_for_status()
        return r.json()

# engines/session_bridge.py
"""
Helpers para extraer cookies desde Playwright y convertirlas a formato de requests.
"""

from typing import Dict

from playwright.async_api import BrowserContext


async def async_extract_cookies(context: BrowserContext) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """
    Versión asíncrona para extraer cookies desde un contexto de Playwright.
    Convierte a dict simple para usar en requests.Session().
    """
    raw_cookies = await context.cookies()
    cookies_dict: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for c in raw_cookies:
        cookies_dict[c["name"]] = c["value"]
    return cookies_dict

# engines/session_validator.py
"""
Validador de sesión VTEX.
Asegura que las cookies estén válidas antes de ejecutar backend.
"""
import logging
from typing import Optional
import requests


class SessionValidator:
    """
    Valida sesión VTEX haciendo una llamada real a OrderForm.
    
    ⚠️ PRINCIPIO CLAVE:
    Una sesión es válida si VTEX permite crear/leer un OrderForm.
    No validamos por nombre de cookies (VTEX las cambia todo el tiempo).
    """

    VTEX_API_BASE = "https://www.nike.cl"
    ORDERFORM_ENDPOINT = "/api/checkout/pub/orderForm"

    def __init__(self, log: Optional[object] = None):
        self.log = log or logging.getLogger("SessionValidator")

    def has_valid_cookies(self, session: requests.Session) -> bool:
        """
        Verifica que una sesión sea válida haciendo una llamada real a VTEX OrderForm.
        
        ✅ VÁLIDA SI:
        - Status 200 + OrderForm response OK
        - OrderForm tiene orderFormId
        
        ❌ INVÁLIDA SI:
        - Status 401/403 (Unauthorized)
        - No hay respuesta JSON
        - No hay orderFormId
        - Timeout / error de conexión
        
        Args:
            session: requests.Session (debe tener cookies del perfil)
        
        Retorna: True si VTEX acepta la sesión, False en caso contrario
        """
        try:
            url = f"{self.VTEX_API_BASE}{self.ORDERFORM_ENDPOINT}"
            
            resp = session.get(url, timeout=5)
            
            # Status 200 es OBLIGATORIO
            if resp.status_code != 200:
                self.log.warning(
                    f"❌ VTEX rechazó sesión (status {resp.status_code}). "
                    f"Cookies inválidas o expiradas."
                )
                return False
            
            # Parsear JSON
            try:
                data = resp.json()
            except Exception as e:
                self.log.warning(f"❌ OrderForm no es JSON válido: {e}")
                return False
            
            # VALIDACIÓN CRÍTICA: orderFormId debe existir
            if not isinstance(data, dict) or "orderFormId" not in data:
                self.log.warning(
                    f"❌ OrderForm válido pero sin orderFormId. "
                    f"Estructura rota o sesión corrupta."
                )
                return False
            
            # ✅ ÉXITO: sesión VTEX válida
            self.log.debug(f"✅ Sesión VTEX válida (orderFormId: {data.get('orderFormId')})")
            return True
            
        except requests.exceptions.Timeout:
            self.log.warning(f"⚠️ Timeout validando cookies (5s). VTEX lento.")
            return False
            
        except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:
            self.log.warning(f"❌ No hay conexión a VTEX. Revisa internet.")
            return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error inesperado validando cookies: {e}")
            return False

    def get_cookie_names(self, session: requests.Session) -> set:
        """Retorna nombres de cookies en la sesión (solo para debug)"""
        try:
            return {c.name for c in session.cookies}
        except:
            return set()

    def assert_valid_cookies(self, session: requests.Session):
        """Lanza excepción si las cookies no son válidas"""
        if not self.has_valid_cookies(session):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "❌ VTEX rechazó la sesión. "
                "Cookies inválidas o expiradas. "
                "Ejecuta LOGIN y luego SESSION_SEED."
            )


======================================================================
FILE: engines\state_machine_orchestrator.py
======================================================================

# engines/state_machine_orchestrator.py
"""
State Machine Orchestrator

Mapeo oficial del flujo ganador:

READY
 ↓
SESSION_SEED
 ↓
ATC
 ↓
PRICING_WAIT
 ↓
PRICING_FROZEN
 ↓
PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
 ↓
BANK_MODAL_OPEN
 ↓
AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION (TERMINAL)

Contratos:
- State Machine decide
- Engines ejecutan
- Señales mandan
- Estados terminales NO salen solos
"""
import logging
import asyncio
from typing import Callable, Optional, Dict, Any
from core.states import AccountState
from engines.orderform_watcher import OrderFormWatcher
from engines.payment_engine import PaymentEngine
from engines.bank_payment_flow import BankPaymentFlow


class StateMachineOrchestrator:
    """
    Orquesta el flujo exacto del mapeo oficial.
    Cada transición es explícita.
    Cada señal es determinista.
    """

    def __init__(self, base_url: str, log_level: str = "INFO"):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.log = logging.getLogger("StateMachineOrchestrator")
        self.log.setLevel(log_level.upper())

        # Engines
        self.watcher = OrderFormWatcher(log_level=log_level)
        self.payment = PaymentEngine(log_level=log_level)
        self.bank_flow = BankPaymentFlow(log_level=log_level)

        # Estado
        self.current_state = AccountState.READY

    def set_state(self, new_state: AccountState, reason: str = ""):
        """Transición de estado explícita"""
        log_msg = f"📊 {self.current_state.name} → {new_state.name}"
        if reason:
            log_msg += f" ({reason})"
        self.log.info(log_msg)
        self.current_state = new_state

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # READY
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def handle_ready(
        self,
        account_name: str,
        sku: str,
        seller: str,
        bank_name: str = "Banco Santander"
    ) -> bool:
        """
        READY → SESSION_SEED
        
        Validaciones:
        - Cuenta cargada
        - Wallet definido
        - Método de pago decidido (banco)
        
        Sale cuando: run() invocado
        """
        self.log.info(f"🟢 READY: {account_name}")
        self.log.info(f"   SKU: {sku}")
        self.log.info(f"   Seller: {seller}")
        self.log.info(f"   Banco: {bank_name}")

        # Validar que esté todo definido
        if not all([sku, seller, bank_name]):
            self.log.error("❌ Faltan parámetros")
            return False

        self.set_state(AccountState.SESSION_SEED, "iniciado")
        return True

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SESSION_SEED
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def handle_session_seed(
        self,
        session_fn: Callable
    ) -> bool:
        """
        SESSION_SEED → ATC
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - Crear sesión válida VTEX
        - Unificar browser + requests
        
        Señal de salida:
        - cookies críticas presentes
        - orderFormId existe
        """
        self.log.info("🔵 SESSION_SEED: creando sesión VTEX...")

        try:
            result = await session_fn() if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(session_fn) else session_fn()
            if not result:
                self.log.error("❌ Sesión fallida")
                return False

            self.log.info("✅ Sesión creada")
            self.set_state(AccountState.ATC, "sesión OK")
            return True

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error: {e}")
            return False

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # ATC
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def handle_atc(
        self,
        atc_fn: Callable,
        sku: str,
        seller: str
    ) -> bool:
        """
        ATC → PRICING_WAIT
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - SKU agregado una sola vez
        - No loops infinitos
        
        Señal de salida:
        - SKU aparece en orderForm.items
        """
        self.log.info(f"🔵 ATC: agregando {sku}...")

        try:
            success = await atc_fn(sku, seller) if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(atc_fn) else atc_fn(sku, seller)
            if not success:
                self.log.error("❌ ATC falló")
                return False

            self.log.info("✅ SKU agregado")
            self.set_state(AccountState.PRICING_WAIT, "ATC OK")
            return True

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error: {e}")
            return False

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # PRICING_WAIT
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def handle_pricing_wait(
        self,
        get_orderform_fn: Callable,
        timeout_sec: int = 30
    ) -> tuple[bool, Optional[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """
        PRICING_WAIT → PRICING_FROZEN
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - Esperar que VTEX termine de pensar
        
        Condiciones que NO permiten salir:
        - item.price == 0
        - totalizers == 0
        - shipping sin SLA
        
        Señal de salida:
        - is_orderform_ready == True
        """
        self.log.info("🟡 PRICING_WAIT: esperando que precio se estabilice...")

        try:
            success, of, reason = await self.watcher.wait_ready(
                get_orderform_fn=get_orderform_fn,
                timeout_sec=timeout_sec
            )

            if not success:
                self.log.error(f"❌ {reason}")
                return False, None

            self.log.info("✅ OrderForm ready")
            self.set_state(AccountState.PRICING_FROZEN, "ready signal")
            return True, of

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error: {e}")
            return False, None

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # PRICING_FROZEN
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def handle_pricing_frozen(
        self,
        get_orderform_fn: Callable,
        freeze_window_ms: int = 400,
        timeout_sec: int = 30
    ) -> tuple[bool, Optional[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """
        PRICING_FROZEN → PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - Confirmar que precio no cambia
        
        CRÍTICO:
        - Solo este estado autoriza pago
        - Nada puede saltarse
        
        Señal de salida:
        - snapshot A == snapshot B (freeze_window_ms)
        """
        self.log.info("🔴 PRICING_FROZEN: esperando congelación...")

        try:
            success, of, reason = await self.watcher.wait_frozen(
                get_orderform_fn=get_orderform_fn,
                freeze_window_ms=freeze_window_ms,
                timeout_sec=timeout_sec
            )

            if not success:
                self.log.error(f"❌ {reason}")
                return False, None

            total = self.watcher.get_total(of)
            display = self.watcher.display_price(total)
            self.log.info(f"✅ Precio congelado: {display}")

            # CONTRATO: Después de esto, NUNCA se valida pricing
            self.set_state(AccountState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED, "congelado")
            return True, of

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error: {e}")
            return False, None

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def handle_payment_bank_selected(
        self,
        vtex_client,
        bank_name: str = "Banco Santander"
    ) -> tuple[bool, Optional[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """
        PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED → BANK_MODAL_OPEN
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - Inyectar método de pago bancario
        - Sin UI, sin clicks
        
        CONTRATO:
        - Banco YA está decidido (READY)
        - NUNCA se decide en runtime
        
        Señal de salida:
        - VTEX acepta paymentData
        - payment activo en orderForm
        """
        self.log.info(f"🔵 PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED: inyectando {bank_name}...")

        try:
            success, reason, of = self.payment.set_bank_payment(
                vtex_client=vtex_client,
                bank_name=bank_name
            )

            if not success:
                self.log.error(f"❌ {reason}")
                return False, None

            # Validar que está activo
            is_active, verification = self.payment.verify_bank_active(of, bank_name)
            if not is_active:
                self.log.error(f"❌ {verification}")
                return False, None

            self.log.info(f"✅ {verification}")
            self.set_state(AccountState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN, "payment inyectado")
            return True, of

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error: {e}")
            return False, None

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # BANK_MODAL_OPEN
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def handle_bank_modal_open(
        self,
        page,
        bank_name: str = "Banco Santander"
    ) -> bool:
        """
        BANK_MODAL_OPEN → AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - Interacción mínima UI
        - Velocidad + determinismo
        
        Acciones:
        - Esperar modal "Selecciona tu banco"
        - Click banco predefinido
        - Esperar botón Finalizar habilitado
        - Click Finalizar (UNA vez)
        
        Señal de salida:
        - Modal Fintoc / confirmación visible
        """
        self.log.info("🔵 BANK_MODAL_OPEN: seleccionando banco...")

        try:
            success, reason = await self.bank_flow.select_bank_and_finalize(
                page=page,
                bank_name=bank_name,
                timeout_sec=10
            )

            if not success:
                self.log.error(f"❌ {reason}")
                return False

            self.log.info(f"✅ {reason}")
            self.set_state(
                AccountState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION,
                "modal Fintoc abierto"
            )
            return True

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error: {e}")
            return False

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION (TERMINAL)
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def handle_awaiting_human_confirmation(
        self,
        total_cents: int,
        bank_name: str = "Banco Santander"
    ) -> bool:
        """
        AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION (TERMINAL)
        
        Responsabilidad:
        - El bot no hace nada más
        
        Acciones:
        - Actualizar UI
        - Notificar Discord
        - Mantener sesión viva (opcional)
        
        ❌ No retries
        ❌ No fallbacks
        ❌ No más clicks
        """
        self.log.info("🟢 AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION")
        self.log.info(f"   🏦 Banco: {bank_name}")
        self.log.info(f"   💰 Total: {self.watcher.display_price(total_cents)}")
        self.log.info(f"   👤 El usuario debe confirmar en su banco")
        self.log.info(f"   🤖 El bot NO toca nada más")

        # Estado final
        self.set_state(AccountState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION)
        return True

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # FLUJO COMPLETO
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def run_full_flow(
        self,
        account_name: str,
        sku: str,
        seller: str,
        bank_name: str,
        session_fn: Callable,
        atc_fn: Callable,
        get_orderform_fn: Callable,
        vtex_client,
        page,
        state_callback: Optional[Callable] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Ejecuta el flujo COMPLETO exactamente como el mapeo oficial.
        
        Retorna: {
            "success": bool,
            "state": AccountState,
            "reason": str
        }
        """
        result = {
            "success": False,
            "state": AccountState.ERROR,
            "reason": ""
        }

        try:
            # READY
            if not self.handle_ready(account_name, sku, seller, bank_name):
                result["reason"] = "READY falló"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # SESSION_SEED
            if not await self.handle_session_seed(session_fn):
                result["reason"] = "SESSION_SEED falló"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # ATC
            if not await self.handle_atc(atc_fn, sku, seller):
                result["reason"] = "ATC falló"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # PRICING_WAIT
            success, of = await self.handle_pricing_wait(get_orderform_fn)
            if not success:
                result["reason"] = "PRICING_WAIT timeout"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # PRICING_FROZEN
            success, of = await self.handle_pricing_frozen(get_orderform_fn)
            if not success:
                result["reason"] = "PRICING_FROZEN timeout"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            total = self.watcher.get_total(of)

            # PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
            success, of = await self.handle_payment_bank_selected(vtex_client, bank_name)
            if not success:
                result["reason"] = "PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED falló"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # BANK_MODAL_OPEN
            if not await self.handle_bank_modal_open(page, bank_name):
                result["reason"] = "BANK_MODAL_OPEN falló"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
            if not self.handle_awaiting_human_confirmation(total, bank_name):
                result["reason"] = "AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION error"
                return result
            if state_callback:
                state_callback(self.current_state)

            # ✅ ÉXITO
            result["success"] = True
            result["state"] = AccountState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
            result["reason"] = "Flujo completado"
            return result

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error inesperado: {e}", exc_info=True)
            result["reason"] = str(e)
            return result
