# runner/state_machine_fixed.py
"""
⚠️ MÁQUINA DE ESTADOS EXPLÍCITA - ALTERNATIVA ARQUITECTÓNICA (no en uso).

Estado: STANDBY (no llamada desde main.py)
Propósito: Máquina de estados explícita para flujo de compra

ORQUESTADOR ACTUAL EN PRODUCCIÓN: runtime/worker.py + DeterministicCheckout
- Si necesitas más control arquitectónico, considera migrar a StateMachine
- StateMachine es más testeable y predecible
- Pero requiere refactor en cómo se llama desde main.py

Cambios A, B, C implementados (defensa anti-cookies-vacías):
- A: Guardia dura de cookies antes de backend
- B: SESSION_SEED real con validación
- C: PAYMENT_FAILED state para errores

Mejoras implementadas:
- 1️⃣ seed_attempts counter (max 3)
- 3️⃣ session_created_at timestamp
- 6️⃣ PRE-CHECK validación antes de pricing

Estados (8 + 1 terminal error):
READY → SESSION_SEED → ATC → PRICING_WAIT → 
PRICING_FROZEN → PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED → 
BANK_MODAL_OPEN → AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
O si falla: PAYMENT_FAILED (terminal)

Nota: Este archivo es válido, sintácticamente correcto, pero NO se llama.
Si necesitas migración futura, usa como referencia.
"""
import logging
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from core.bot_state import BotState
from engines.orderform_watcher import OrderFormWatcher
from engines.payment_engine import PaymentEngine
from engines.bank_payment_flow import BankPaymentFlow
from engines.session_validator import SessionValidator
from playwright.sync_api import Page


class StateMachine:
    """
    Máquina de estados determinista (8 estados + terminal error).
    
    Flow correcto:
    READY → SESSION_SEED → ATC → PRICING_WAIT → 
    PRICING_FROZEN → PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED → 
    BANK_MODAL_OPEN → AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
    
    O si falla:
    (cualquier estado) → PAYMENT_FAILED (🔴 TERMINAL)
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        session,  # requests.Session con cookies VTEX
        page: Optional[Page] = None,  # Playwright Page (opcional al inicio)
        log: Optional[object] = None
    ):
        self.session = session
        self.page = page
        self.log = log or logging.getLogger("StateMachine")
        self.state = BotState.READY
        self.error_reason = None  # Motivo de PAYMENT_FAILED
        
        # Engines
        self.watcher = OrderFormWatcher(session, log=self.log)
        self.payment = PaymentEngine(session, log=self.log)
        self.bank_flow = BankPaymentFlow(log=self.log)
        self.validator = SessionValidator(log=self.log)
        
        # Estado OrderForm
        self.order_form = None
        
        # 1️⃣ MEJORA: Contador anti-loop SESSION_SEED
        self.seed_attempts = 0
        self.max_seed_attempts = 3
        
        # 3️⃣ MEJORA: Timestamp sesión (para validar "frescura")
        import time
        self.session_created_at = None

    def run(self, bank_name: str = "Banco Santander") -> Tuple[BotState, dict]:
        """
        Ejecuta la máquina de estados.
        
        Retorna: (estado_final, último_orderform)
        """
        self.log.info(f"🚀 Máquina iniciada: {self.state.name}")

        try:
            # Loop principal: termina en AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION o PAYMENT_FAILED
            while self.state not in (
                BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION,
                BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
            ):
                self.log.info(f"━━ Estado: {self.state.name}")

                # 🔒 CAMBIO A: GUARDIA GLOBAL DE SESIÓN
                # Bloquea cualquier estado que necesite cookies, salvo SESSION_SEED
                if self.state not in (
                    BotState.SESSION_SEED, 
                    BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED,
                    BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
                ):
                    if not self.validator.has_valid_cookies(self.session):
                        self.log.warning(f"⚠️ Sesión expirada → regenerando identidad VTEX")
                        
                        # 1️⃣ MEJORA: Anti-loop SESSION_SEED
                        self.seed_attempts += 1
                        if self.seed_attempts > self.max_seed_attempts:
                            self.log.error(f"❌ SESSION_SEED falló {self.max_seed_attempts} veces → ABORT")
                            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
                            self.error_reason = f"Session seed loop: {self.seed_attempts} attempts"
                            continue
                        
                        self.state = BotState.SESSION_SEED
                        continue

                # Transiciones explícitas
                if self.state == BotState.READY:
                    self._transition_ready()

                elif self.state == BotState.SESSION_SEED:
                    self._transition_session_seed()

                elif self.state == BotState.ATC:
                    self._transition_atc()

                elif self.state == BotState.PRICING_WAIT:
                    self._transition_pricing_wait()

                elif self.state == BotState.PRICING_FROZEN:
                    self._transition_pricing_frozen()

                elif self.state == BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED:
                    self._transition_payment_bank_selected()

                elif self.state == BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN:
                    self._transition_bank_modal_open(bank_name=bank_name)

                else:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"❌ Estado desconocido: {self.state}")

            # Terminal
            self.log.info(f"✅ Máquina terminada: {self.state.name}")
            if self.state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED:
                self.log.error(f"   Motivo: {self.error_reason}")
            return self.state, self.order_form

        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Excepción en máquina: {e}")
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            return self.state, self.order_form

    # ============================================================================
    # TRANSICIONES (8 estados + error)
    # ============================================================================

    def _transition_ready(self):
        """
        READY → SESSION_SEED
        Validar que sesión está lista
        """
        self.log.info(f"   Validando sesión...")
        if not self.page:
            raise RuntimeError("❌ Page (Playwright) no está asignado")
        
        self.log.info(f"✅ Sesión lista")
        self.state = BotState.SESSION_SEED

    def _transition_session_seed(self):
        """
        SESSION_SEED → ATC
        
        🔧 CAMBIO B: SESSION_SEED REAL
        - Abre browser
        - Navega Nike
        - Extrae cookies
        - Inyecta en requests.Session
        - VALIDA que sean suficientes
        """
        import time
        self.log.info(f"   🔑 Regenerando identidad VTEX...")
        
        if not self.page:
            raise RuntimeError("Page requerida para SESSION_SEED")
        
        try:
            # 1. Navegar a Nike para obtener cookies
            self.log.info(f"   [DEBUG] SESSION_SEED 1: Navegando a Nike...")
            try:
                self.page.goto("https://www.nike.cl", timeout=30000)
                self.log.info(f"   [DEBUG] SESSION_SEED 2: Nike cargado")
            except Exception as goto_error:
                self.log.error(f"   ⚠️ goto Nike timeout/error: {goto_error}")
                self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
                self.error_reason = f"Nike navigation failed: {goto_error}"
                return
            
            # 2. Obtener cookies de Playwright
            self.log.info(f"   [DEBUG] SESSION_SEED 3: Extrayendo cookies...")
            context = self.page.context
            cookies = context.cookies()
            
            if not cookies:
                raise RuntimeError("No se obtuvieron cookies de Playwright")
            
            # 3. Inyectar en requests.Session
            self.log.info(f"   → Inyectando en requests.Session...")
            for cookie in cookies:
                try:
                    self.session.cookies.set(
                        name=cookie.get("name"),
                        value=cookie.get("value"),
                        domain=cookie.get("domain", ".nike.cl"),
                    )
                except:
                    pass  # Algunos cookies pueden fallar, es ok
            
            # 4. VALIDAR que tenemos cookies VTEX válidas (clave!)
            self.log.info(f"   → Validando cookies VTEX...")
            if not self.validator.has_valid_cookies(self.session):
                cookies_names = self.validator.get_cookie_names(self.session)
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"❌ Cookies obtenidas pero incompletas. "
                    f"Tenemos: {cookies_names}, "
                    f"necesitamos: vtex_session"
                )
            
            # 3️⃣ MEJORA: Marcar timestamp de sesión
            self.session_created_at = time.time()
            self.seed_attempts = 0  # Reset counter (SESSION_SEED exitoso)
            
            self.log.info(f"✅ Identidad VTEX lista (sesión válida por próximos 30min)")
            self.state = BotState.ATC
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error en SESSION_SEED: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def _transition_atc(self):
        """
        ATC → PRICING_WAIT
        Obtener OrderForm inicial
        """
        self.log.info(f"   Obtener OrderForm...")
        try:
            self.order_form = self.watcher.fetch()
            self.log.info(f"✅ OrderForm obtenido")
            self.state = BotState.PRICING_WAIT
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error obteniendo OrderForm: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def _transition_pricing_wait(self):
        """
        PRICING_WAIT → PRICING_FROZEN
        Esperar OrderForm "ready"
        
        6️⃣ MEJORA: PRE-CHECK antes de entrar a pricing
        Validar que se puede acceder a checkout API
        """
        self.log.info(f"   🟡 Preparando sesión segura…")
        
        # 6️⃣ MEJORA: PRE-CHECK - validar acceso a checkout
        try:
            # Hacer GET rápido a orderForm para validar sesión
            r = self.payment.session.get(
                f"{self.payment.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm",
                timeout=5
            )
            if r.status_code != 200:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"No se puede acceder a checkout (status {r.status_code}) → "
                    f"sesión probablemente muerta"
                )
            self.log.info(f"   ✅ Acceso a checkout validado")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ PRE-CHECK fallido: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
            return
        
        # Ahora sí, esperar pricing
        self.log.info(f"   Esperando pricing...")
        try:
            self.order_form = self.watcher.wait_until_ready(timeout=8)
            self.log.info(f"✅ Pricing validado")
            self.state = BotState.PRICING_FROZEN
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Timeout esperando pricing: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def _transition_pricing_frozen(self):
        """
        PRICING_FROZEN → PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
        Congelar pricing (400ms)
        
        🔴 PUNTO DE NO RETORNO: Si falla → ABORT
        """
        self.log.info(f"   Congelando pricing (400ms window)...")
        try:
            self.order_form = self.watcher.freeze(window_ms=400)
            total = self.watcher.get_total(self.order_form)
            self.log.info(f"   🔴 PRICING_FROZEN: {self.watcher.display_price(total)}")
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
        except RuntimeError as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Pricing cambió durante freeze: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def _transition_payment_bank_selected(self):
        """
        PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED → BANK_MODAL_OPEN
        Inyectar payment method (backend)
        """
        self.log.info(f"   Inyectando payment...")
        try:
            of_id = self.order_form.get("orderFormId")
            if not of_id:
                raise RuntimeError("orderFormId no encontrado")
            
            self.order_form = self.payment.set_bank_payment(of_id)
            self.log.info(f"✅ Payment inyectado")
            self.state = BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error inyectando payment: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            # 🔧 CAMBIO C: Transicionar a PAYMENT_FAILED en vez de raise
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def _transition_bank_modal_open(self, bank_name: str = "Banco Santander"):
        """
        BANK_MODAL_OPEN → AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION (o PAYMENT_FAILED)
        
        Orquestrador ÚNICO del flujo visual de pago:
        ✅ Navegar a /checkout/#/payment
        ✅ Manejar modal "¡CONFIRMA TUS DATOS!"
        ✅ Seleccionar "Transferencia con tu banco"
        ✅ Finalizar compra → Fintoc
        🛑 BOT SE DETIENE AQUÍ
        """
        self.log.info(f"💳 Orquestrador único de pago iniciado...")
        try:
            if not self.page:
                raise RuntimeError("Page requerida para pago")
            
            # Importar CheckoutOrchestrator dinámicamente para evitar dependencias circulares
            from engines.checkout_orchestrator import CheckoutOrchestrator
            
            orchestrator = CheckoutOrchestrator(self.page, self.log)
            success = orchestrator.run_bank_flow(bank_name=bank_name)
            
            if success:
                self.log.info(f"🟢 Pago iniciado, esperando confirmación humana")
                self.state = BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
            else:
                raise RuntimeError("Flujo de pago no completado")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log.error(f"❌ Error en pago: {e}")
            self.error_reason = str(e)
            # 🔧 CAMBIO C: Transicionar a PAYMENT_FAILED
            self.state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    # ============================================================================
    # HELPERS
    # ============================================================================

    def _has_valid_cookies(self) -> bool:
        """Wrapper para SessionValidator"""
        return self.validator.has_valid_cookies(self.session)

    def get_state(self) -> BotState:
        """Retorna estado actual"""
        return self.state

    def set_page(self, page: Page):
        """Asignar Playwright Page si no se asignó en __init__"""
        self.page = page

    def get_order_form(self) -> dict:
        """Retorna último OrderForm capturado"""
        return self.order_form or {}

    def get_error_reason(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Retorna motivo de fallo si estado es PAYMENT_FAILED"""
        return self.error_reason

    def is_terminal(self) -> bool:
        """Retorna True si estado es terminal"""
        return self.state in (
            BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION,
            BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
        )

    def is_success(self) -> bool:
        """Retorna True si alcanzó AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION"""
        return self.state == BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION


======================================================================
FILE: runtime\backend_vtex.py
======================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
runtime/backend_vtex.py - ATC Backend EXACTO (El único camino)

VERDAD:
- ATC backend es GET /checkout/cart/add con parámetros
- Es el ÚNICO lugar donde ocurre ATC
- No hay ATC browser, no hay session seed, no hay alternativas
- Si redirige (302) → OK
- Si 200 → OK
- Otra cosa → FAIL
- Timeout: 2s máx
- Retries: máx 1 (solo por timeout)

FLUJO:
1. Crear sesión HTTP
2. GET /checkout/cart/add?sku=...&qty=1&seller=1&sc=1
3. Validar price > 0
4. FREEZE (session.close, nada más ocurre)

NO HAY VARIANTES.
"""
import requests
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def extract_total_price(orderform: dict) -> int | None:
    """
    Extrae el precio TOTAL correcto del OrderForm VTEX.
    
    FUENTE CORRECTA: orderForm.totalizers[]
    
    NUNCA uses:
    ❌ item.price
    ❌ item.sellingPrice
    ❌ item.listPrice
    
    SIEMPRE usa:
    ✅ totalizers[id="Items"].value (en centavos)
    
    Returns:
        Valor en centavos, o None si no encuentra
    """
    for t in orderform.get("totalizers", []):
        if t.get("id") == "Items":
            return t.get("value")  # en centavos
    return None


def atc_backend(sku: str, session: requests.Session) -> bool:
    """
    ATC BACKEND EXACTO.
    
    Esto es TODO lo que necesitas para agregar al carrito en Nike.cl (VTEX)
    
    Args:
        sku: SKU del item (ej: "123456789")
        session: requests.Session con cookies Nike válidas
    
    Returns:
        True si ATC OK, False en cualquier error
    
    NOTA: Si redirige (302) = éxito. Si 200 = éxito. Otra cosa = falla.
    """
    url = "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add"
    params = {
        "sku": sku,
        "qty": 1,
        "seller": 1,
        "sc": 1  # subcategory flag
    }
    
    try:
        # GET con timeout corto (máx 2s)
        r = session.get(
            url,
            params=params,
            allow_redirects=False,
            timeout=2
        )
        
        # Éxito: 200 (directo) o 302 (redirige a payment)
        if r.status_code in (200, 302):
            print(f"[ATC] [{sku}] Status {r.status_code} → OK")
            return True
        else:
            print(f"[ATC] [{sku}] Status {r.status_code} → FAIL")
            return False
            
    except requests.Timeout:
        print(f"[ATC] [{sku}] Timeout → FAIL")
        return False
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[ATC] [{sku}] Error: {e} → FAIL")
        return False


def price_check(session: requests.Session) -> bool:
    """
    Price check: Verificar que el item está en el carrito y tiene precio
    
    Usa extract_total_price() para leer el valor CORRECTO desde totalizers.
    
    Args:
        session: requests.Session con cookies Nike válidas
    
    Returns:
        True si hay item con price > 0
    """
    try:
        # Obtener order form actual
        r = session.get(
            "https://www.nike.cl/api/checkout/pub/orderForm",
            timeout=2
        )
        
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"[PRICE] Status {r.status_code} → FAIL")
            return False
        
        data = r.json()
        
        # CORRECTO: Leer desde totalizers
        value = extract_total_price(data)
        
        if not value or value <= 0:
            print(f"[PRICE] Price invalid or zero: {value} → FAIL")
            return False
        
        # Convertir a CLP para display
        price_clp = value // 100
        print(f"[PRICE] Price OK: ${price_clp} CLP (centavos: {value})")
        return True
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[PRICE] Error: {e} → FAIL")
        return False


def backend_run(sku: str, session: requests.Session) -> bool:
    """
    Backend EXACTO: ATC + Price check
    
    FLUJO:
    1. GET /checkout/cart/add?sku=...&qty=1&seller=1&sc=1
    2. Validar price > 0
    3. FREEZE (session.close - backend muere)
    
    Args:
        sku: SKU a comprar
        session: requests.Session con cookies
    
    Returns:
        True si TODO OK, False en cualquier falla
    """
    # PASO 1: ATC
    print(f"\n[BACKEND] ATC: {sku}")
    if not atc_backend(sku, session):
        print(f"[BACKEND] ATC FAILED")
        return False
    
    # PASO 2: Price check
    print(f"[BACKEND] Price check")
    if not price_check(session):
        print(f"[BACKEND] PRICE FAILED")
        return False
    
    # PASO 3: FREEZE
    # Session cierra aquí. Backend muere. Nada más ocurre.
    print(f"[BACKEND] FREEZE: session.close()")
    session.close()
    
    print(f"[BACKEND] OK - Backend finalizado")
    return True


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Test simple (requiere cookie válida)
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Uso: python backend_vtex.py <sku> [cookie_session]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    sku = sys.argv[1]
    
    # Crear sesión de test
    session = requests.Session()
    
    # Si hay cookie, inyectarla
    if len(sys.argv) > 2:
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", sys.argv[2])
    
    result = backend_run(sku, session)
    sys.exit(0 if result else 1)


======================================================================
FILE: runtime\bot_controller.py
======================================================================

"""
BotController - Orquestador Principal (6 Mejoras Implementadas)

Responsabilidades:
✅ Gestionar estados explícitos (no booleanos)
✅ Separación absoluta: LOGIN vs PLAY
✅ Validación real de sesión (no check de archivo)
✅ Price lock persistente
✅ Botones bloqueados según estado
✅ Sin asyncio, sin deadlocks
✅ Master/Slave profile management (ProfileManager)
✅ Rutas ABSOLUTAS desde config.settings
"""
import time
import os
import requests
from pathlib import Path
from core.account_state import AccountState, STATES_LOGIN_BLOCKED, STATES_PLAY_BLOCKED
from engines.playwright_engine_v2 import PlaywrightEngine
from profile_manager import ProfileManager
from runtime.backend_vtex import extract_total_price
from vtex.vtex_client import VTEXClient

# FUENTE DE VERDAD PARA RUTAS ABSOLUTAS
from config.settings import AUTH_ROOT


class BotController:
    """
    Controlador de una cuenta individual.
    
    Estados:
    NO_AUTH → LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS → READY → MONITORING → PRICE_LOCKED → EXECUTING
    
    ARQUITECTURA MASTER/SLAVE:
    - profile_login (MASTER): Usuario loguea manualmente UNA SOLA VEZ
    - profile_run (SLAVE): Clonado de master, descartado después de cada run
    """
    
    def __init__(self, account_name: str, base_path: str = "auth"):
        """
        Args:
            account_name: "cuenta2", "cuenta3", etc
            base_path: Directorio base (default "auth") - IGNORADO, usamos config.py
        """
        self.account_name = account_name
        self.profile_manager = ProfileManager(account_name, base_path)
        
        # Usar profile_run (SLAVE) para Playwright
        profile_run = self.profile_manager.get_run_profile_path()
        self.engine = PlaywrightEngine(profile_run)
        
        # FUENTE DE VERDAD: .login_ok (creado por login_runner.py)
        # Usar ruta ABSOLUTA desde config.settings
        account_path = os.path.join(AUTH_ROOT, account_name)
        self.login_ok_path = os.path.join(account_path, ".login_ok")
        
        if os.path.exists(self.login_ok_path):
            self.state = AccountState.READY
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🎯 BotController inicializado (READY - .login_ok detectado)")
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] login_ok path: {self.login_ok_path}")
        else:
            self.state = AccountState.NO_AUTH
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🎯 BotController inicializado (NO_AUTH - falta .login_ok)")
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] login_ok path: {self.login_ok_path}")
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] Existe?: {os.path.exists(self.login_ok_path)}")
        
        # MEJORA #4: Price lock persistente y estructurado
        self.price_lock = None  # {"sku": "123", "price": 99999, "ts": 1704282000}
        
        # Control de worker
        self.stop_signal = False

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # MEJORA #1: VALIDACIÓN REAL DE SESIÓN (no solo bool)
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    def validate_session_real(self) -> bool:
        """Usa PlaywrightEngine v2 para validación dura"""
        return self.engine.validate_session_real()

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # HANDLER LOGIN (MEJORA #2: LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS bloqueado)
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    def handle_btn_login_click(self) -> bool:
        """
        Responsabilidad ÚNICA: Loguear y validar.
        No compra, no monitorea.
        
        ✅ Se ejecuta en THREAD SEPARADO (no congela UI)
        ✅ Usar MASTER/SLAVE: profile_login (user loguea), clona a profile_run
        
        ARQUITECTURA:
        1. Verificar que profile_login (master) tiene sesión válida
        2. Limpiar profile_run (slave) del run anterior
        3. Clonar profile_login → profile_run
        4. Validate sesión clonada
        5. Marcar como READY
        
        Retorna: True si login fue exitoso
        """
        # MEJORA #2: Bloquear si ya está en LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS
        if self.state == AccountState.LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] ⛔ LOGIN ya en proceso. Espera.")
            return False
        
        # Transicionar a estado intermedio
        self.state = AccountState.LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🔐 LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS")
        
        try:
            # PASO 1: Verificar que el master profile tiene cookies
            if not self.profile_manager.verify_master_profile():
                self.state = AccountState.NO_AUTH
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ❌ No hay sesión guardada en profile_login")
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] 👉 Ejecuta: python login_runner.py {self.account_name}")
                return False
            
            # PASO 2: Limpiar slave del run anterior (elimina SingletonLock)
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🧹 Limpiando profile_run anterior...")
            if not self.profile_manager.cleanup_run_profile(force=True):
                self.state = AccountState.NO_AUTH
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ❌ No pude limpiar profile_run")
                return False
            
            # PASO 3: Clonar master → slave
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] 📋 Clonando profile_login → profile_run...")
            if not self.profile_manager.clone_login_to_run():
                self.state = AccountState.NO_AUTH
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ❌ Error clonando perfil")
                return False
            
            # PASO 4: Validate sesión en el slave clonado
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] ✅ Perfil clonado. Validando sesión...")
            
            if self.validate_session_real():
                self.state = AccountState.READY
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ✅ ESTADO: READY → Puedes presionar PLAY")
                return True
            else:
                self.state = AccountState.NO_AUTH
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ❌ Validación falló. Intenta de nuevo.")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] ❌ Error en login: {e}")
            self.state = AccountState.NO_AUTH
            return False

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # HANDLER PLAY (MEJORA #6: Botón bloqueado en estados inválidos)
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    def handle_btn_play_click(self, target_sku: str) -> None:
        """
        Responsabilidad ÚNICA: Monitor backend → disparo Chrome.
        
        Args:
            target_sku: SKU a monitorear (ej: "12345")
        """
        # MEJORA #6: Bloquear si no estamos en READY
        if self.state != AccountState.READY:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] ⛔ Solo READY puede PLAY")
            print(f"[{self.account_name}]    Estado actual: {self.state.value}")
            return
        
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] ▶ Iniciando PLAY...")
        self.stop_signal = False
        self.worker_loop(target_sku)

    def worker_loop(self, target_sku: str) -> None:
        """
        Loop puro (sin Chrome).
        Solo golpea endpoints → cuando PRICE_LOCKED, lanza browser.
        
        MEJORA #3: Worker solo confía en estado, no en flags débiles
        MEJORA #4: Price lock persistente
        """
        self.state = AccountState.MONITORING
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] 📊 MONITORING iniciado")
        print(f"[{self.account_name}]    Target SKU: {target_sku}")
        
        # 🔴 ATC BACKEND FIRST - Agregar producto al carrito ANTES de monitorear
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🛒 Ejecutando ATC backend...")
        session = requests.Session()
        
        atc_ok = self._atc_backend(session, target_sku)
        if not atc_ok:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] ❌ ATC backend falló - ABORT")
            self.state = AccountState.READY
            return
        
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] ✅ ATC backend OK")
        
        # 🔴 CREAR VTEX CLIENT PARA MONITOREAR PRECIO REAL
        vtex = VTEXClient("https://www.nike.cl", session=session)
        
        # MONITOR PRICE REAL (desde orderForm)
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] ⏳ Esperando precio real del carrito...")
        iteration = 0
        
        while not self.stop_signal:
            iteration += 1
            
            try:
                # Obtener orderForm real
                order_form = vtex.get_order_form()
                
                if not order_form:
                    print(f"[{self.account_name}]    [{iteration}] ⚠️ OrderForm no disponible")
                    time.sleep(0.5)
                    continue
                
                # Extraer precio CORRECTO desde totalizers
                price_clp = vtex.extract_price(order_form)
                
                if price_clp <= 0:
                    print(f"[{self.account_name}]    [{iteration}] ... precio = {price_clp} ...")
                    time.sleep(0.4)
                    continue
                
                # 🎯 PRECIO ENCONTRADO
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] 💰 PRECIO REAL DETECTADO: ${price_clp:,.0f} CLP")
                
                # 🔒 FREEZE - NO MÁS REQUESTS AL BACKEND
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🔒 FREEZE activado - fin de requests al backend")
                self.stop_signal = True
                
                # Price lock
                self.price_lock = {
                    "sku": target_sku,
                    "price_clp": price_clp,
                    "order_form_id": order_form.get("orderFormId"),
                    "ts": time.time()
                }
                print(f"[{self.account_name}]    Price lock: {self.price_lock}")
                
                # Transicionar a PRICE_LOCKED
                self.state = AccountState.PRICE_LOCKED
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] 📌 ESTADO: PRICE_LOCKED")
                
                # Ahora EXECUTING - abrir Chrome con checkout
                self.state = AccountState.EXECUTING
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🚀 ESTADO: EXECUTING")
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ✅ Carrito listo para pago")
                
                # URL correcta: /checkout/#/cart (NO cart/add)
                checkout_url = "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/#/cart"
                self.engine.launch_checkout_mission(checkout_url)
                
                print(f"[{self.account_name}] ✅ Worker finalizado")
                break
                
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[{self.account_name}]    [{iteration}] ⚠️ Error: {e}")
                time.sleep(0.5)
                continue


    def check_price_backend(self, sku: str) -> tuple[bool, int | None]:
        """
        Verifica el precio disponible en el backend VTEX.
        
        Retorna: (precio_disponible: bool, precio: int | None)
            - precio_disponible: True si hay precio > 0
            - precio: valor en centavos (ej: 13599000 = $135.990 CLP)
        
        Args:
            sku: SKU a monitorear
        """
        try:
            # Obtener orderForm desde API
            r = requests.get(
                "https://www.nike.cl/api/checkout/pub/orderForm",
                timeout=2
            )
            
            if r.status_code != 200:
                return False, None
            
            data = r.json()
            
            # CORRECTO: Usar extract_total_price() desde totalizers
            price_centavos = extract_total_price(data)
            
            if not price_centavos or price_centavos <= 0:
                return False, None
            
            # Precio disponible
            return True, price_centavos
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}] ⚠️ Error al verificar precio: {e}")
            return False, None

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # UI HELPERS (MEJORA #6: Saber si botón está habilitado)
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    def is_login_enabled(self) -> bool:
        """Botón LOGIN habilitado SOLO si NO está en LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS"""
        return self.state not in STATES_LOGIN_BLOCKED

    def is_play_enabled(self) -> bool:
        """Botón PLAY habilitado SOLO si estado == READY"""
        return self.state == AccountState.READY

    def get_status_text(self) -> str:
        """Para mostrar en UI el estado actual"""
        status_map = {
            AccountState.NO_AUTH: "❌ No logueado",
            AccountState.LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS: "🔄 Logueando...",
            AccountState.READY: "✅ Listo para PLAY",
            AccountState.MONITORING: "📊 Monitoreando...",
            AccountState.PRICE_LOCKED: "🎯 ¡Precio encontrado!",
            AccountState.EXECUTING: "🚀 Chrome abierto",
        }
        return status_map.get(self.state, "?")

    def _atc_backend(self, session: requests.Session, sku: str) -> bool:
        """
        ATC Backend: Agrega producto al carrito
        
        CRÍTICO: Esto DEBE ejecutarse ANTES de MONITORING
        
        Args:
            session: requests.Session (con cookies)
            sku: SKU a agregar
            
        Returns:
            True si ATC OK (status 200 o 302), False si error
        """
        try:
            url = "https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add"
            params = {
                "sku": sku,
                "qty": 1,
                "seller": 1,
                "sc": 1
            }
            
            r = session.get(
                url,
                params=params,
                allow_redirects=False,
                timeout=2
            )
            
            # Éxito: 200 (OK) o 302 (redirect a checkout)
            if r.status_code in (200, 302):
                print(f"[{self.account_name}]    ATC status {r.status_code} ✅")
                return True
            else:
                print(f"[{self.account_name}]    ATC status {r.status_code} ❌")
                return False
                
        except requests.Timeout:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}]    ATC timeout ❌")
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{self.account_name}]    ATC error: {e} ❌")
            return False

    def stop_worker(self):
        """Detiene el worker_loop gracefully"""
        self.stop_signal = True
        print(f"[{self.account_name}] 🛑 Deteniendo worker...")



======================================================================
FILE: runtime\multiprocessing_runner.py
======================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
runtime/multiprocessing_runner.py - Ejecutor multiprocessing (1 cuenta = 1 proceso)

FLUJO V0.9 — DROP-ONLY (CONGELADO)

Pasos:
1. LOGIN (humano, off-drop)
2. PRE-FLIGHT CHECK (filesystem only)
3. ARMADO_SESION_BACKEND
4. ATC_BACKEND
5. PRICE_CHECK
6. FREEZE_BACKEND
7. HANDOVER_BROWSER
8. MAGIC_LINK
9. WAITING_HUMAN
10. EXIT

Estados: STARTING → PREFLIGHT → BACKEND_SETUP → BACKEND_ATC → BACKEND_PRICE_OK → FREEZE_BACKEND → HANDOVER → WAITING_HUMAN → DONE / ERROR
"""
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

# Imports locales
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))
from profile_manager_v2 import clone_auth_to_run
from runtime.preflight import preflight as do_preflight
from runtime.process_states import ProcState
from runtime.backend_vtex import backend_run
from config.settings import AUTH_ROOT, CHROME_PATH


def emit(bus, user: str, state: ProcState, msg: str = ""):
    """
    Emitir estado al bus
    """
    if bus:
        bus.put({"user": user, "state": state.value, "msg": msg})


def run_account(user: str, sku: str, bus=None):
    """
    Ejecutor por cuenta - FLUJO V1.0 FINAL
    
    1 proceso = 1 cuenta
    1 flujo = 10 pasos
    Sin variantes, sin fallback
    
    Args:
        user: Nombre de la cuenta
        sku: SKU del item (ej: "123456789")
        bus: Queue para estados (opcional)
    """
    try:
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.STARTING)
        
        # Rutas absolutas desde config
        account_path = os.path.join(AUTH_ROOT, user)
        
        # PRE-FLIGHT: Validar filesystem (NO red, NO Chrome, NO cookies)
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.PREFLIGHT)
        ok, reason = do_preflight(account_path, sku, CHROME_PATH)
        if not ok:
            emit(bus, user, ProcState.ERROR, f"preflight_fail:{reason}")
            return
        
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.BACKEND_SETUP)
        
        # Crear sesión HTTP con cookies de profile
        import requests
        session = requests.Session()
        # Aquí se heredarían las cookies de profile_run (en implementación real)
        
        # PASO 4-6: ATC + PRICE_CHECK + FREEZE
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.BACKEND_ATC)
        if not backend_run(sku, session):
            emit(bus, user, ProcState.ERROR, "atc_failed")
            return
        
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.BACKEND_PRICE_OK)
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.FREEZE_BACKEND)
        
        # PASO 7: HANDOVER_BROWSER (inmediato)
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.HANDOVER)
        
        profile_run = os.path.join(account_path, "profile_run")
        with sync_playwright() as p:
            ctx = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=profile_run,
                channel="chrome",
                headless=False,
                viewport=None,
                args=["--start-maximized"]
            )
            
            page = ctx.new_page()
            
            # PASO 8: MAGIC_LINK (EXACTO)
            magic_link = f"https://www.nike.cl/checkout/cart/add?sku={sku}&qty=1&seller=1&sc=1"
            page.goto(magic_link, timeout=30000)
            
            # PASO 9: WAITING_HUMAN (terminal)
            emit(bus, user, ProcState.WAITING_HUMAN)
            input(f"[{user}] ENTER para cerrar")
            
            # PASO 10: EXIT
            ctx.close()
        
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.DONE)
        
    except Exception as e:
        emit(bus, user, ProcState.ERROR, str(e))


def run_accounts_mp(accounts: list, sku: str, bus=None):
    """
    Lanza N procesos (uno por cuenta)
    
    Args:
        accounts: ["cuenta1", "cuenta2", "cuenta3"]
        sku: SKU a comprar (mismo para todas las cuentas)
        bus: Queue para estados (opcional)
    """
    mp.set_start_method("spawn", force=True)
    
    procs = []
    for user in accounts:
        p = mp.Process(target=run_account, args=(user, sku, bus))
        p.start()
        procs.append(p)
    
    # Esperar que terminen todos
    for p in procs:
        p.join()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 3:
        print("Uso: python multiprocessing_runner.py <sku> <cuenta1> [cuenta2] [cuenta3]...")
        print("Ejemplo: python multiprocessing_runner.py 123456789 cuenta1 cuenta2")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    sku = sys.argv[1]
    accounts = sys.argv[2:]
    run_accounts_mp(accounts, sku)


======================================================================
FILE: runtime\preflight.py
======================================================================

"""
runtime/preflight.py - PRE-FLIGHT FINAL v1.0

Valida SOLO y SOLO esto:
✅ .login_ok existe
✅ profile_login/ existe  
✅ Clone profile_login → profile_run funciona
✅ Chrome ejecutable existe
✅ SKU válido

Si cualquiera falla → ABORT. Sin intentos, sin correcciones.

REGLAS:
- PRE-FLIGHT no toca red
- PRE-FLIGHT no abre Chrome
- PRE-FLIGHT no valida cookies
- PRE-FLIGHT no arregla nada
- Si falla → abortas
- Si pasa → ejecutas sin pensar
"""
import os
import shutil


def preflight(account_path: str, sku: str, chrome_path: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    r"""
    Valida precondiciones para PLAY.
    
    Args:
        account_path: Path absoluto a auth/cuenta (ej: C:\Users\...\auth\cuenta2)
        sku: SKU a comprar (debe ser válido/no-vacío)
        chrome_path: Path a chrome.exe
    
    Returns:
        (success, reason)
        - (True, "OK") si todas las validaciones pasan
        - (False, reason) si alguna falla
    
    Raises:
        Nunca. Siempre retorna tupla.
    """
    
    # 1) .login_ok existe
    login_ok = os.path.join(account_path, ".login_ok")
    if not os.path.exists(login_ok):
        return False, "NO_LOGIN_OK"
    
    # 2) profile_login/ existe
    profile_login = os.path.join(account_path, "profile_login")
    if not os.path.isdir(profile_login):
        return False, "PROFILE_LOGIN_MISSING"
    
    # 3) Clone profile_login → profile_run
    profile_run = os.path.join(account_path, "profile_run")
    try:
        if os.path.exists(profile_run):
            shutil.rmtree(profile_run)
        
        shutil.copytree(
            profile_login,
            profile_run,
            ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(
                "Cache", "Code Cache", "Crashpad",
                "ShaderCache", "GrShaderCache",
                "Dictionaries", "hyphen-data"
            )
        )
    except Exception as e:
        return False, f"CLONE_FAIL: {str(e)}"
    
    # 4) Chrome ejecutable existe
    if not os.path.isfile(chrome_path):
        return False, "CHROME_NOT_FOUND"
    
    # 5) SKU válido
    if not sku or not str(sku).strip():
        return False, "SKU_INVALID"
    
    # ✅ PREFLIGHT OK
    return True, "OK"

FILE: runtime\process_states.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
runtime/process_states.py - Estados estandar para procesos
"""
from enum import Enum


class ProcState(str, Enum):
    """Estados unicos de ejecucion"""
    STARTING = "STARTING"
    CLONING = "CLONING"
    PREFLIGHT = "PREFLIGHT"
    BACKEND_SETUP = "BACKEND_SETUP"
    BACKEND_ATC = "BACKEND_ATC"
    BACKEND_PRICE_OK = "BACKEND_PRICE_OK"
    FREEZE_BACKEND = "FREEZE_BACKEND"
    HANDOVER = "HANDOVER"
    WAITING_HUMAN = "WAITING_HUMAN"
    DONE = "DONE"
    ERROR = "ERROR"

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
runtime/state_bus.py - Canal multiprocessing para estados
"""
import multiprocessing as mp


def make_state_bus():
    """
    Crea un Queue multiprocessing para pasar mensajes.
    
    Mensaje format:
    {
        "user": "cuenta2",
        "state": "STARTING",
        "msg": "error message (opcional)"
    }
    """
    return mp.Manager().Queue()

import time

# ⚠️ CONSOLIDACIÓN EN PROGRESO: 30 dic 2025

def matar_zombies_del_perfil(profile_path, dprint):
    """Mata procesos Chrome que bloquean el perfil"""
    try:
        import psutil
    except ImportError:
        dprint("   psutil no instalado (skipping)")
        return
    
    import os
    ruta_perfil = os.path.abspath(profile_path).replace("/", "\\").lower()
    dprint("   Buscando zombies...")
    killed_count = 0
    
    try:
        for proc in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'cmdline']):
            try:
                if proc.info['name'].lower() == 'chrome.exe':
                    cmdline = ' '.join(proc.info['cmdline']).lower()
                    if ruta_perfil in cmdline:
                        proc.terminate()
                        try:
                            proc.wait(timeout=2)
                        except psutil.TimeoutExpired:
                            proc.kill()
                        killed_count += 1
            except (Exception,):
                pass
        
        if killed_count == 0:
            dprint("   No habia zombies (ok)")
        else:
            dprint("   {} zombie(s) eliminado(s)".format(killed_count))
    except Exception as e:
        dprint("   Error: {} (ignorado)".format(e))

def account_worker(account, live: bool = True, max_price_attempts: int | None = None):
    """
    ✅ REFACTORIZADO: Ahora SOLO prepara contexto y llama StateMachine.run()
    
    Responsabilidad (simplificada):
    1. Validar sesión y cookies
    2. Crear requests.Session + Playwright Page
    3. Llamar StateMachine.run() ← ORQUESTADOR ÚNICO
    4. Actualizar estado de account
    5. Fin
    
    NOTA: Toda la lógica de flujo está en runner/state_machine.py::StateMachine
    Este worker es ahora un WRAPPER delgado que:
    - Prepara sesión
    - Abre browser
    - Llama StateMachine
    - Maneja errors
    """
    from runner.state_machine import StateMachine
    from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
    from requests import Session
    
    debug_file = "logs/{}_debug.log".format(account.name)
    Path("logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
    def dprint(*args):
        msg = " ".join(str(a) for a in args)
        print("[{}] {}".format(account.name, msg))
        try:
            with open(debug_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as df:
                df.write("[{}] {}\n".format(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), msg))
        except:
            pass
    
    dprint("🚀 WORKER REFACTORIZADO: Llamando StateMachine.run()")
    log_account = logging.getLogger("worker.{}".format(account.name))
    
    try:
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        # PASO 1: PREPARAR SESIÓN
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        dprint("1️⃣ Preparando sesión...")
        
        # 🔥 VALIDACIÓN: Si el perfil existe = está listo para usar
        # La VERDADERA validación ocurre navegando, no leyendo SQLite
        cookies = get_cookies_for_account(account.account_path)
        
        if cookies:
            dprint(f"   ✅ Cookies encontradas en profile_pw (perfil válido)")
        else:
            dprint("   ℹ️  No hay cookies locales (continuando - validación real al navegar)")
        
        cookies = cookies or {}  # Dict vacío si no hay cookies
        
        dprint("   ✅ Sesión lista")
        
        # Crear requests.Session con cookies VTEX
        user_agent = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36"
        session = Session()
        session.headers.update({"User-Agent": user_agent})
        for key, value in cookies.items():
            session.cookies.set(key, value)
        
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        # PASO 2: ABRIR NAVEGADOR PLAYWRIGHT (ASYNC)
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        dprint("2️⃣ Abriendo navegador...")
        
        profile_dir = os.path.join(account.account_path, "profile_pw")
        os.makedirs(profile_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Matar zombies antes de abrir
        matar_zombies_del_perfil(profile_dir, dprint)
        
        # 🔥 LLAMADA SYNC: sin deadlocks en Windows (sync_playwright)
        dprint("   [DEBUG] 2.0 SYNC: Lanzando navegador...")
        try:
            from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
            
            dprint("   [DEBUG] 2.0a: Iniciando sync_playwright...")
            p = sync_playwright().start()
            dprint("   [DEBUG] 2.0b: sync_playwright iniciado")
            
            browser = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=profile_dir,
                headless=False,
                args=[
                    "--start-maximized",
                    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled"
                ]
            )
            dprint("   [DEBUG] 2.0c: Context abierto")
            
            page = browser.pages[0] if browser.pages else browser.new_page()
            dprint("   [DEBUG] 2.0d SYNC: Navegador abierto ✅")
        except Exception as e:
            dprint(f"   ❌ Error abriendo navegador: {e}")
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            account.last_error = str(e)[:200]
            raise
        
        # ✅ Las cookies ya están en el profile_pw (se cargaron al inicio)
        # No necesitamos sincronizar porque sync_playwright usa el mismo directorio
        dprint(f"   ✅ Sesión lista (perfil: {os.path.basename(profile_dir)})")
        
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        # PASO 3: LLAMAR STATEMACHINE
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        # 📋 CONFIRMACIÓN RUNTIME - VERDAD ABSOLUTA DEL RUN
        print("")
        print("=" * 70)
        print(f"[{account.name}] ⚙️ RUNTIME CONFIG:")
        print(f"[{account.name}]   - SKU: {account.sku or 'SIN SKU'}")
        print(f"[{account.name}]   - Payment: TRANSFERENCIA (BANK)")
        print(f"[{account.name}]   - Auto-Checkout: {account.auto_checkout}")
        print("=" * 70)
        print("")
        
        dprint("3️⃣ Llamando StateMachine.run()...")
        
        sm = StateMachine(
            session=session,
            page=page,
            log=log_account
        )
        
        final_state, final_orderform = sm.run(
            bank_name=getattr(account, 'bank_name', 'Banco Santander')
        )
        
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        # PASO 4: PROCESAR RESULTADO
        # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        dprint("4️⃣ Procesando resultado...")
        
        from core.bot_state import BotState
        
        if final_state == BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION:
            dprint("")
            dprint("=" * 70)
            dprint("✅ FLUJO COMPLETADO - ESPERANDO CONFIRMACIÓN")
            dprint("=" * 70)
            
            if final_orderform and final_orderform.get('items'):
                total = final_orderform['items'][0].get('price', 0)
                dprint("💰 Total: ${:,.0f} CLP".format(total / 100))
            
            dprint("👤 El usuario debe confirmar en su banco")
            dprint("🤖 El bot NO toca nada más")
            dprint("")
            
            account.state = AccountState.AWAITING_BANK_CONFIRMATION
            account.last_status = "Esperando confirmación bancaria"
            if final_orderform and final_orderform.get('items'):
                account.last_price = final_orderform['items'][0].get('price', 0)
        
        elif final_state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED:
            dprint("")
            dprint("❌ FLUJO FALLÓ")
            dprint(f"   Motivo: {sm.error_reason}")
            dprint("")
            
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            account.last_status = "Error en flujo"
            account.last_error = sm.error_reason[:200]
        
        else:
            dprint(f"⚠️ Estado terminal inesperado: {final_state.name}")
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            account.last_status = f"Estado inesperado: {final_state.name}"
        
        dprint("WORKER FINALIZADO")
    
    except Exception as e:
        dprint("❌ ERROR GENERAL: {}".format(e))
        log_account.exception("Worker crashed: {}".format(e))
        import traceback
        dprint(traceback.format_exc())
        account.state = AccountState.ERROR
        account.last_error = str(e)[:200]

def account_worker_deterministic(account, live: bool = True):
    """
    ✅ FLUJO DETERMINISTA PROFESIONAL (nivel Atmos)
    
    GARANTIZADO:
    - Precio congelado (sin $0)
    - Banco seleccionado
    - Modal Fintoc abierto
    - STOP determinista
    - Usuario confirma en su banco
    
    No automatiza:
    - Login bancario
    - 2FA
    - Confirmación final
    """
    
    debug_file = f"logs/{account.name}_deterministic.log"
    Path("logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
    def dprint(*args):
        msg = " ".join(str(a) for a in args)
        print(f"[{account.name}] {msg}")
        try:
            with open(debug_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as df:
                df.write(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}\n")
        except:
            pass
    
    dprint("=" * 70)
    dprint("🔒 FLUJO DETERMINISTA INICIADO")
    dprint("=" * 70)
    
    log_account = logging.getLogger(f"worker.{account.name}.deterministic")
    
    try:
        # ═════════════════════════════════════════════
        # Fase 0: Validar sesión
        # ═════════════════════════════════════════════
        dprint("0️⃣ Verificando sesión Nike...")
        
        cookies = get_cookies_for_account(account.account_path)
        if cookies:
            dprint(f"✅ Cookies encontradas ({len(cookies)} items)")
        else:
            dprint("ℹ️  Sin cookies - navegación iniciará login si es necesario")
        
        user_agent = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36"
        
        dprint("1️⃣ Creando cliente VTEX...")
        client = TlsVTEXClient(
            base_url=BASE_URL,
            cookies=cookies,
            user_agent=user_agent,
            order_form_id=None
        )
        
        # Verificar loggedIn
        dprint("2️⃣ Validando loggedIn...")
        of = client.get_order_form()
        
        if not of.get("loggedIn"):
            dprint("❌ Usuario NO logueado en Nike")
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            return
        
        dprint("✅ Usuario logueado")
        
        # ═════════════════════════════════════════════
        # Fase 1: ATC (Add To Cart)
        # ═════════════════════════════════════════════
        dprint("3️⃣ Agregando al carrito...")
        
        if account.sku and live:
            try:
                success = client.frontend_add_to_cart(
                    sku_id=account.sku,
                    quantity=1,
                    seller=account.seller,
                    sales_channel="1"
                )
                
                if not success:
                    dprint("❌ add_to_cart falló")
                    account.state = AccountState.ERROR
                    return
                
                of = client.get_order_form()
                if not of or not of.get('items'):
                    dprint("❌ Carrito vacío")
                    account.state = AccountState.ERROR
                    return
                
                item_name = of['items'][0].get('name', 'UNKNOWN')
                item_price = of['items'][0].get('price', 0)
                
                dprint(f"✅ ATC OK: {item_name}")
                dprint(f"   Precio: ${item_price/100:,.0f} CLP")
                
                account.state = AccountState.CART_LOCKED
                account.last_status = "Carrito asegurado"
                
            except Exception as e:
                dprint(f"❌ ERROR ATC: {e}")
                account.state = AccountState.ERROR
                return
        
        # ═════════════════════════════════════════════
        # Fase 2: Abrir Playwright y ejecutar flujo determinista
        # DEPRECATED: account_worker_deterministic fue reemplazado
        # Usa: runtime/bot_controller.py::BotController en su lugar
        dprint("❌ ERROR: account_worker_deterministic() está DEPRECATED")
        account.state = AccountState.ERROR
        account.last_error = "Usa BotController en su lugar"
        return
    
    except Exception as e:
        dprint(f"❌ ERROR GENERAL: {e}")
        log_account.exception(f"Worker crashed: {e}")
        account.state = AccountState.ERROR
        account.last_error = str(e)[:200]

FILE: runtime\worker_fixed.py

import time
import logging
import os
import json
import threading
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from core.states import AccountState
from vtex.tls_client_vtex import TlsVTEXClient
from auth.profile_cookies import get_cookies_for_account
from config.settings import BASE_URL
from utils.zombie_killer import matar_chrome_especifico

HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL = 2
STABILITY_CHECK_INTERVAL = 1.2

log = logging.getLogger("worker")


def open_login_browser(account_path, account_name):
    """Abre browser para LOGIN MANUAL (user loguea, cierra Chrome)"""
    from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
    import os
    
    profile_dir = os.path.join(account_path, "profile_pw")
    os.makedirs(profile_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    # 🔫 LIMPIEZA QUIRÚRGICA: Matar procesos fantasma de ESTA cuenta
    print("[LOGIN {}] 🧹 Limpiando procesos viejos...".format(account_name))
    matar_chrome_especifico(profile_dir)
    
    print("[LOGIN {}] Abriendo Nike para loguearse...".format(account_name))
    
    try:
        with sync_playwright() as p:
            browser = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=profile_dir,
                headless=False,
                args=[
                    "--start-maximized",
                    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled"
                ],
                viewport=None
            )
            
            page = browser.new_page()
            page.goto("https://www.nike.cl/login", timeout=0)
            
            print("")
            print("=" * 50)
            print("[LOGIN {}] LOGUÉATE EN NIKE".format(account_name))
            print("=" * 50)
            print("")
            print("1. Ingresa tu email")
            print("2. Ingresa tu contraseña")
            print("3. Completa 2FA si aparece")
            print("4. Cuando veas tu cuenta logueada")
            print("5. CIERRA ESTA VENTANA DE CHROME")
            print("")
            
            page.wait_for_timeout(15 * 60 * 1000)
    
    except Exception as e:
        print("[LOGIN {}] Error: {}".format(account_name, e))


def matar_zombies_del_perfil(profile_path, dprint):
    """Mata procesos Chrome que bloquean el perfil"""
    try:
        import psutil
    except ImportError:
        dprint("   psutil no instalado (skipping)")
        return
    
    import os
    ruta_perfil = os.path.abspath(profile_path).replace("/", "\\").lower()
    dprint("   Buscando zombies...")
    killed_count = 0
    
    try:
        for proc in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'cmdline']):
            try:
                if proc.info['name'].lower() == 'chrome.exe':
                    cmdline = ' '.join(proc.info['cmdline']).lower()
                    if ruta_perfil in cmdline:
                        proc.terminate()
                        try:
                            proc.wait(timeout=2)
                        except psutil.TimeoutExpired:
                            proc.kill()
                        killed_count += 1
            except (Exception,):
                pass
        
        if killed_count == 0:
            dprint("   No habia zombies (ok)")
        else:
            dprint("   {} zombie(s) eliminado(s)".format(killed_count))
    except Exception as e:
        dprint("   Error: {} (ignorado)".format(e))


def _launch_browser_for_payment(account_path, account_name, cookies, dprint):
    """MODE B FINAL: Lanzar payment en proceso separado con perfil real"""
    import subprocess
    import sys
    
    dprint("   Lanzando PAYMENT en proceso separado...")
    
    try:
        subprocess.Popen([
            sys.executable,
            "tools/payment_launcher.py",
            account_path,
            account_name
        ])
        
        dprint("   Chrome abierto - Completa el pago")
        time.sleep(2)
    
    except Exception as e:
        dprint("   ERROR: {}".format(e))
        import traceback
        dprint(traceback.format_exc())


def account_worker(account, live: bool = True, max_price_attempts: int | None = None):
    """Worker VTEX - FLUJO FRONTEND BYPASS"""
    
    debug_file = "logs/{}_debug.log".format(account.name)
    Path("logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
    def dprint(*args):
        msg = " ".join(str(a) for a in args)
        print("[{}] {}".format(account.name, msg))
        try:
            with open(debug_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as df:
                df.write("[{}] {}\n".format(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), msg))
        except:
            pass
    
    dprint("WORKER INICIADO (live={})".format(live))
    log_account = logging.getLogger("worker.{}".format(account.name))
    
    try:
        dprint("0 Verificando sesion Nike...")
        
        # Obtener cookies desde profile_pw (Playwright) o tokens.json (fallback)
        cookies = get_cookies_for_account(account.account_path)
        
        if not cookies or not all(cookies.values()):
            dprint("   ERROR: No hay cookies válidas")
            dprint("   Necesita hacer LOGIN primero (profile_pw vacío)")
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            return
        
        dprint("   Cookies cargadas (3 items)")
        
        user_agent = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36"
        
        dprint("2 Cliente VTEX (TLS spoofed)...")
        client = TlsVTEXClient(
            base_url=BASE_URL,
            cookies=cookies,
            user_agent=user_agent,
            order_form_id=None
        )
        dprint("   Headers sincronizados")
        
        # Verificar que esté logueado en Nike
        dprint("0b Validando loggedIn...")
        of = client.get_order_form()
        
        if not of.get("loggedIn"):
            dprint("   ERROR: Usuario NO logueado en Nike")
            dprint("   Requiere LOGIN antes de PLAY")
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            return
        
        dprint("   ✅ Sesion valida - Usuario logueado")
        
        dprint("4 CAPA 1: Agregando al carrito...")
        
        if account.sku and live:
            dprint("   Enviando: SKU={}, Qty=1, Seller={}".format(account.sku, account.seller))
            
            try:
                success = client.frontend_add_to_cart(
                    sku_id=account.sku,
                    quantity=1,
                    seller=account.seller,
                    sales_channel="1"
                )
                
                if success:
                    dprint("   Verificando carrito...")
                    of = client.get_order_form()
                    
                    if of and of.get('items'):
                        first = of['items'][0]
                        actual_name = first.get('name', 'UNKNOWN')
                        actual_price = first.get('price', 0)
                        
                        dprint("   CARRITO OK")
                        dprint("   {}".format(actual_name))
                        dprint("   ${:,.0f} CLP".format(actual_price/100))
                        
                        account.state = AccountState.CART_LOCKED
                        account.last_status = "Carrito asegurado"
                        dprint("   CART_LOCKED")
                    else:
                        dprint("   ERROR: Carrito vacio")
                        account.state = AccountState.ERROR
                        return
                else:
                    dprint("   ERROR: add_to_cart fallo")
                    account.state = AccountState.ERROR
                    return
                    
            except Exception as e:
                dprint("   ERROR: {}".format(e))
                account.state = AccountState.ERROR
                return
        
        dprint("5 CAPA 2: Esperando precio...")
        account.state = AccountState.RUNNING
        account.last_status = "Esperando precio"
        
        PRICE_POLL_INTERVAL = 0.25
        PRICE_RETRY_MAX = 15
        price_attempt = 0
        price_latched = False
        
        while price_attempt < PRICE_RETRY_MAX and not price_latched:
            price_attempt += 1
            
            try:
                of = client.get_order_form()
                
                if of and of.get('items'):
                    item = of['items'][0]
                    price = item.get('price', 0)
                    
                    if price and price > 0:
                        price_latched = True
                        account.state = AccountState.PRICE_LOCKED
                        account.last_status = "Precio: ${:,.0f} CLP".format(price/100)
                        account.last_price = price
                        
                        dprint("   PRECIO CAPTURADO: ${:,.0f} CLP".format(price/100))
                        dprint("   PRICE_LOCKED - Abriendo navegador...")
                        break
                    else:
                        dprint("   Intento {}/{}: precio aun en 0".format(price_attempt, PRICE_RETRY_MAX))
                
            except Exception as e:
                dprint("   Aviso: {}".format(e))
            
            if not price_latched:
                time.sleep(PRICE_POLL_INTERVAL)
        
        if not price_latched:
            dprint("   ERROR: TIMEOUT - Precio no llego")
            account.state = AccountState.ERROR
            return
        
        dprint("6 CAPA 3: Abriendo navegador para pago...")
        
        dprint("   CONGELANDO CARRITO - NO MAS BACKEND")
        
        try:
            session_cookies = client.session.cookies.get_dict()
            hot_cookies = session_cookies.copy()
            dprint("   Cookies: {} items".format(len(hot_cookies)))
        except Exception as e:
            dprint("   Aviso de cookies: {}".format(e))
            hot_cookies = cookies
        
        _launch_browser_for_payment(account.account_path, account.name, hot_cookies, dprint)
        
        account.state = AccountState.WAITING_HUMAN_READY
        account.last_status = "Esperando pago manual"
        dprint("   PAYMENT ABIERTO - Completa el pago en Chrome")
        return
        
        dprint("WORKER COMPLETADO")
    
    except Exception as e:
        dprint("ERROR WORKER: {}".format(e))
        log_account.exception("Worker crashed: {}".format(e))
        account.state = AccountState.ERROR
        account.last_error = str(e)[:200]

import os
import json
import time
import threading

import pytest

from runner.account_manager import AccountManager
from runner.state_machine import AccountState


def write_tokens(path, valid=True):
    os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
    data = {}
    if valid:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}
    else:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "", "vtex_session": "", "vtex_segment": ""}
    with open(os.path.join(path, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f)


def test_load_accounts(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a1 = base / "cuenta1"
    a2 = base / "cuenta2"
    write_tokens(a1, valid=True)
    write_tokens(a2, valid=False)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    la = mgr.list_accounts()
    states = {x["name"]: x["state"] for x in la}
    # now, accounts with tokens but no target move to NO_TARGET
    assert states["cuenta1"] == "NO_TARGET"
    assert states["cuenta2"] == "NO_AUTH"
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def write_target(path, sku="181815", seller="1"):
    with open(path / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": sku, "seller": seller}, f)


def test_play_pause_resume_stop(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a1 = base / "cuentaA"
    write_tokens(a1, valid=True)
    write_target(a1)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    # play
    r = mgr.play("cuentaA")
    assert r["ok"]
    time.sleep(0.1)
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaA") in (AccountState.RUNNING,)

    # pause
    r = mgr.pause("cuentaA")
    assert r["ok"]
    time.sleep(0.05)
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaA") == AccountState.PAUSED

    # resume
    r = mgr.resume("cuentaA")
    assert r["ok"]
    time.sleep(0.05)
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaA") == AccountState.RUNNING

    # stop
    r = mgr.stop("cuentaA")
    assert r["ok"]
    time.sleep(0.05)
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaA") == AccountState.READY
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def test_supervisor_detects_dead_thread(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a1 = base / "cuentaB"
    write_tokens(a1, valid=True)
    # add target so it becomes READY
    with open(a1 / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "181815", "seller": "1"}, f)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    r = mgr.play("cuentaB")
    assert r["ok"]
    # simulate worker death by killing thread without setting kill_event
    acct = mgr._accounts["cuentaB"]
    if acct.thread:
        # forcibly replace thread with a dummy dead thread (simulate crash)
        acct.thread = threading.Thread(target=lambda: None)
    # give supervisor a tick
    time.sleep(0.05)
    # supervisor should set ERROR
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaB") == AccountState.ERROR
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def test_no_target_and_set_target(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a1 = base / "cuentaNoTarget"
    write_tokens(a1, valid=True)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    # since no target file exists, state must be NO_TARGET
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaNoTarget") == AccountState.NO_TARGET
    # set target
    r = mgr.set_target("cuentaNoTarget", "181815", "seller1")
    assert r["ok"]
    assert mgr.get_account_state("cuentaNoTarget") == AccountState.READY
    mgr.stop_supervisor()

import os
import json
import time
from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from core.states import AccountState


def test_update_config_applies_when_ready(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "u1"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "config.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auto_checkout": False, "wallet": {"primary": "manual"}}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    ok, msg = mgr.update_config("u1")
    assert ok
    acc = mgr.accounts["u1"]
    assert acc.auto_checkout is False
    assert acc.wallet.primary == "manual"


def test_update_config_refused_when_running(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "u2"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "config.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auto_checkout": False, "wallet": {"primary": "manual"}}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()
    # mark as running
    mgr.accounts["u2"].state = AccountState.RUNNING

    ok, msg = mgr.update_config("u2")
    assert not ok
    assert "No se puede" in msg

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_circuit_breaker.py

"""
Tests para Circuit Breaker en orderform_watcher.

Validar que wait_until_ready() falla rápido (no reintentos infinitos) cuando:
1. Backend retorna 5xx (error de servidor)
2. Timeout de red
3. OrderForm vacío (precio = 0)
"""

import pytest
import logging
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch, MagicMock
from engines.orderform_watcher import OrderFormWatcher

log = logging.getLogger("test_circuit_breaker")


class TestCircuitBreaker:
    """Tests para circuit breaker en wait_until_ready()"""

    def setup_method(self):
        """Setup antes de cada test"""
        self.mock_session = Mock()
        self.watcher = OrderFormWatcher(self.mock_session, log=log)

    def test_circuit_breaker_on_500_error(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Circuit breaker dispara en error 5xx
        
        Verificar que wait_until_ready() falla rápido cuando:
        - GET /orderForm retorna 500 Internal Server Error
        - NO reintenta indefinidamente
        """
        # Simular 500 Error
        response = Mock()
        response.status_code = 500
        response.raise_for_status.side_effect = Exception("500 Server Error")
        
        self.mock_session.get.return_value = response
        
        # Ejecutar: debe fallar rápido
        with pytest.raises(Exception) as exc_info:
            self.watcher.wait_until_ready(timeout=5)
        
        # Verificar que fue error de backend
        assert "500" in str(exc_info.value) or "Server Error" in str(exc_info.value)
        
        # Verificar que NO hizo demasiados reintentos
        assert self.mock_session.get.call_count < 10, \
            f"Expected < 10 retries, got {self.mock_session.get.call_count}"

    def test_circuit_breaker_on_503_error(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Circuit breaker dispara en error 503 (Service Unavailable)
        
        Verificar que wait_until_ready() falla rápido cuando VTEX está down
        """
        # Simular 503 Service Unavailable
        response = Mock()
        response.status_code = 503
        response.raise_for_status.side_effect = Exception("503 Service Unavailable")
        
        self.mock_session.get.return_value = response
        
        # Ejecutar: debe fallar rápido
        with pytest.raises(Exception) as exc_info:
            self.watcher.wait_until_ready(timeout=5)
        
        assert "503" in str(exc_info.value) or "Service" in str(exc_info.value)

    def test_no_circuit_breaker_on_200_price_zero(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: SIN circuit breaker para precio = 0
        
        Si el precio está en 0 pero el servidor está up (200 OK),
        wait_until_ready() debe REINTENTUAR (no disparar circuit breaker)
        """
        # Simular 200 OK pero con precio = 0 (aún no salió el drop)
        order_form_zero = {
            "items": [{"price": 0, "name": "Test Item"}],
            "orderFormId": "test123"
        }
        
        order_form_valid = {
            "items": [{"price": 50000, "name": "Test Item"}],
            "orderFormId": "test123"
        }
        
        # Primero retorna precio 0 (3 veces), luego precio válido
        self.mock_session.get.side_effect = [
            Mock(status_code=200, json=lambda: order_form_zero),
            Mock(status_code=200, json=lambda: order_form_zero),
            Mock(status_code=200, json=lambda: order_form_zero),
            Mock(status_code=200, json=lambda: order_form_valid),
        ]
        
        # Ejecutar: debe REINTENTUAR hasta obtener precio válido
        result = self.watcher.wait_until_ready(timeout=10)
        
        # Verificar que obtuvo precio válido
        assert result["items"][0]["price"] == 50000
        assert self.mock_session.get.call_count == 4

    def test_freeze_double_snapshot_validates_price(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: freeze() valida precio con 2 snapshots en 400ms
        
        Verificar que freeze():
        1. Toma snapshot 1
        2. Espera 400ms
        3. Toma snapshot 2
        4. Falla si precio cambió
        """
        snapshot_1 = {
            "items": [{"price": 50000, "name": "Test"}],
            "orderFormId": "test123"
        }
        
        snapshot_2_changed = {
            "items": [{"price": 60000, "name": "Test"}],  # Precio cambió!
            "orderFormId": "test123"
        }
        
        # Simular 2 GETs: precio sube en 400ms
        self.mock_session.get.side_effect = [
            Mock(status_code=200, json=lambda: snapshot_1),
            Mock(status_code=200, json=lambda: snapshot_2_changed),
        ]
        
        # Ejecutar: debe detectar cambio de precio
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError) as exc_info:
            self.watcher.freeze(window_ms=400)
        
        # Verificar que el error es por cambio de precio
        assert "price" in str(exc_info.value).lower() or "change" in str(exc_info.value).lower()

    def test_timeout_after_max_duration(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: wait_until_ready() respeta timeout
        
        Verificar que después de N segundos sin precio válido,
        wait_until_ready() falla (no espera eternamente)
        """
        # Simular precio siempre en 0
        order_form_zero = {
            "items": [{"price": 0, "name": "Test Item"}],
            "orderFormId": "test123"
        }
        
        self.mock_session.get.return_value = Mock(
            status_code=200,
            json=lambda: order_form_zero
        )
        
        # Ejecutar con timeout muy corto (1 segundo)
        with pytest.raises(Exception) as exc_info:
            self.watcher.wait_until_ready(timeout=1)
        
        # Verificar que fue timeout, no error de conexión
        assert "timeout" in str(exc_info.value).lower() or "time" in str(exc_info.value).lower()


class TestOrderFormValidation:
    """Tests para validación de OrderForm"""

    def setup_method(self):
        self.mock_session = Mock()
        self.watcher = OrderFormWatcher(self.mock_session, log=log)

    def test_valid_order_form_structure(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: OrderForm válido tiene estructura correcta
        """
        valid_of = {
            "items": [
                {
                    "name": "Nike Shoe",
                    "price": 120000,
                    "sku": "ABC123"
                }
            ],
            "orderFormId": "xyz789",
            "loggedIn": True
        }
        
        # Verificar que tiene campos requeridos
        assert "items" in valid_of
        assert "orderFormId" in valid_of
        assert len(valid_of["items"]) > 0
        assert "price" in valid_of["items"][0]

    def test_invalid_order_form_no_items(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: OrderForm inválido sin items
        """
        invalid_of = {
            "items": [],
            "orderFormId": "xyz789"
        }
        
        # Verificar estructura
        assert len(invalid_of["items"]) == 0

    def test_display_price_formatting(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Formateo de precio CLP
        """
        watcher = OrderFormWatcher(Mock(), log=log)
        
        # 120000 centavos = $1200.00 CLP
        price_display = watcher.display_price(120000)
        
        assert "1.200" in price_display or "1200" in price_display


if __name__ == "__main__":
    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])


======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_controller.py
