import os
import json
import time

import pytest

from runner.account_manager import AccountManager
from runner.controller import Controller
from runner.state_machine import AccountState


def write_tokens(path, valid=True):
    os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
    data = {}
    if valid:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}
    else:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "", "vtex_session": "", "vtex_segment": ""}
    with open(os.path.join(path, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f)


def test_play_and_block_double(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a1 = base / "c1"
    write_tokens(a1, valid=True)
    # add target
    with open(a1 / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "181815", "seller": "1"}, f)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    ctrl = Controller(mgr, max_concurrent=2)

    ev = {"type": "PLAY", "account": "c1", "payload": None}
    r = ctrl.handle_event(ev)
    assert r["ok"]

    # double play should be blocked
    r2 = ctrl.handle_event(ev)
    assert not r2["ok"]
    assert r2["message"] in ("Acción en progreso, espera", "Acción no permitida en el estado actual")
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def test_concurrency_limit(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    write_tokens(base / "a", True)
    write_tokens(base / "b", True)
    # add targets
    with open((base / "a") / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)
    with open((base / "b") / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "222", "seller": "1"}, f)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    ctrl = Controller(mgr, max_concurrent=1)

    r1 = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "PLAY", "account": "a", "payload": None})
    assert r1["ok"]
    # second play should be rejected due to concurrency
    r2 = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "PLAY", "account": "b", "payload": None})
    assert not r2["ok"]
    assert r2["message"] == "Límite de concurrencia alcanzado"
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def test_stop_all_blocks_and_waits(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    write_tokens(base / "x", True)
    write_tokens(base / "y", True)
    # add targets before loading
    with open((base / "x") / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)
    with open((base / "y") / "target.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "222", "seller": "1"}, f)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    ctrl = Controller(mgr, max_concurrent=2)

    assert ctrl.handle_event({"type": "PLAY", "account": "x", "payload": None})["ok"]
    assert ctrl.handle_event({"type": "PLAY", "account": "y", "payload": None})["ok"]
    # call STOP_ALL
    res = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "STOP_ALL", "account": None, "payload": None})
    # The operation may complete successfully or time out depending on environment, assert a dict response
    assert isinstance(res, dict)
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def test_toggle_silent(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    write_tokens(base / "s1", True)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    ctrl = Controller(mgr)

    r = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "TOGGLE_SILENT", "account": None, "payload": {"enabled": True}})
    assert r["ok"]
    assert ctrl.silent is True
    r2 = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "TOGGLE_SILENT", "account": None, "payload": {"enabled": False}})
    assert r2["ok"]
    assert ctrl.silent is False
    mgr.stop_supervisor()


def test_play_block_when_no_target(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a1 = base / "nt"
    write_tokens(a1, True)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base), heartbeat_interval=0.01)
    mgr.load_accounts()
    ctrl = Controller(mgr)

    # initial state is NO_TARGET, play should be blocked
    r = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "PLAY", "account": "nt", "payload": None})
    assert not r["ok"]
    assert r["message"] == "Acción no permitida en el estado actual"

    # set target via controller
    res = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "SET_TARGET", "account": "nt", "payload": {"sku": "181815", "seller": "1"}})
    assert res["ok"]

    # now play should work
    r2 = ctrl.handle_event({"type": "PLAY", "account": "nt", "payload": None})
    assert r2["ok"]
    mgr.stop_supervisor()

import os
import json

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from controller.controller import Controller
from core.events import EventType


def write_tokens(path, valid=True):
    os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
    data = {}
    if valid:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}
    else:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "", "vtex_session": "", "vtex_segment": ""}
    with open(os.path.join(path, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f)


def write_target(path, sku="111", seller="1"):
    with open(os.path.join(path, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": sku, "seller": seller}, f)


def test_controller_play(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "c1"
    write_tokens(a, True)
    write_target(a)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()
    ctrl = Controller(mgr)
    ok, msg = ctrl.handle({"type": EventType.PLAY, "account": "c1"})
    assert ok

    ctrl = Controller(mgr)
    ok, msg = ctrl.handle({"type": EventType.SET_CONCURRENCY, "account": None, "payload": {"max": 3}})

    assert mgr.max_concurrent == 3

# tests/test_cuenta2_orderform.py

import os
import json
import requests

BASE_URL = "https://www.nike.cl"

TOKENS_PATH = os.path.join("auth", "cuenta2", "tokens.json")

def build_session_from_tokens():
    if not os.path.exists(TOKENS_PATH):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"No existe {TOKENS_PATH}")

    with open(TOKENS_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        tokens = json.load(f)

    vtex_session = tokens.get("vtex_session")
    vtex_segment = tokens.get("vtex_segment")
    vtex_id = tokens.get("VtexIdclientAutCookie_nikeclprod")

    if not vtex_session or not vtex_segment:
        raise ValueError("Faltan vtex_session o vtex_segment en tokens.json (cuenta2)")

    s = requests.Session()

    # Headers muy browser-like
    s.headers.update({
        "User-Agent": (
            "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) "
            "AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) "
            "Chrome/123.0.0.0 Safari/537.36"
        ),
        "Accept": "application/json, text/javascript, */*; q=0.01",
        "Accept-Language": "es-CL,es;q=0.9,en;q=0.8",
        "Origin": BASE_URL,
        "Referer": f"{BASE_URL}/",
        "Connection": "keep-alive"
    })

    # Cookies VTEX
    cookies = {
        "vtex_session": vtex_session,
        "vtex_segment": vtex_segment,
    }

    if vtex_id:
        cookies["VtexIdclientAutCookie_nikeclprod"] = vtex_id

    s.cookies.update(cookies)

    return s

def main():
    print("🔧 Probando OrderForm con cuenta2...")

    s = build_session_from_tokens()
    url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm"

    print(f"➡ GET {url}")
    resp = s.get(url)

    print("Status:", resp.status_code)
    ct = resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
    print("Content-Type:", ct)

    # Si es HTML de Cloudflare, mostramos solo un pedazo
    if "text/html" in ct:
        print("⚠ Respuesta HTML (posible Cloudflare / bloqueo). Muestra primeras 500 chars:")
        print(resp.text[:500])
    else:
        try:
            data = resp.json()
            print("✅ JSON recibido. orderFormId:", data.get("orderFormId"))
            print("items actuales:", data.get("items"))
        except Exception:
            print("Respuesta no JSON, cuerpo:")
            print(resp.text[:500])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python

"""
Test del flujo determinista.
Valida que los componentes estén bien integrados.
"""
import asyncio
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def test_states():
    """✅ Valida que los nuevos estados existan"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("✅ TEST: Estados")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    required_states = [
        "PRICING_WAIT",
        "PRICING_FROZEN",
        "PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED",
        "BANK_MODAL_OPEN",
        "AWAITING_BANK_CONFIRMATION"
    ]
    
    for state_name in required_states:
        try:
            state = getattr(AccountState, state_name)
            print(f"  ✅ {state_name}")
        except AttributeError:
            print(f"  ❌ FALTA: {state_name}")
            return False
    
    print("\n✅ Todos los estados presentes")
    return True


def test_validator_instantiation():
    """✅ Valida que OrderFormValidator se pueda instanciar"""

    print("✅ TEST: OrderFormValidator")

        validator = OrderFormValidator()

        # Validar métodos
        required_methods = [
            "is_basic_ready",
            "is_pricing_stable",
            "wait_for_frozen_price",
            "get_total_price",
            "get_currency_display"
        ]

            else:
                print(f"  ❌ FALTA: {method_name}")
                return False
        
        print("\n✅ OrderFormValidator OK")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  ❌ Error: {e}")
        return False


def test_validator_basic_ready():
    """✅ Valida lógica de is_basic_ready"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("✅ TEST: OrderFormValidator.is_basic_ready")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    validator = OrderFormValidator()
    
    # ❌ OrderForm vacío
    print("\n  Test 1: OrderForm vacío")
    ok, reason = validator.is_basic_ready({})
    assert not ok, "Debería fallar"
    print(f"  ✅ Rechazado: {reason}")
    
    # ❌ Sin items
    print("\n  Test 2: Sin items")
    ok, reason = validator.is_basic_ready({"orderFormId": "123"})
    assert not ok, "Debería fallar"
    print(f"  ✅ Rechazado: {reason}")
    
    # ❌ Item con price = 0
    print("\n  Test 3: Item con price = 0")
    of = {
        "orderFormId": "123",
        "items": [{"price": 0}],
        "totalizers": [{"value": 0}]
    }
    ok, reason = validator.is_basic_ready(of)
    assert not ok, "Debería fallar"
    print(f"  ✅ Rechazado: {reason}")
    
    # ✅ OrderForm válido (mínimo)
    print("\n  Test 4: OrderForm válido (mínimo)")
    of = {
        "orderFormId": "123",
        "items": [{"price": 12299000}],
        "totalizers": [{"value": 12299000}],
        "shippingData": {
            "address": {"street": "Main St"},
            "logisticsInfo": [{"selectedSla": "normal"}]
        },
        "paymentData": {
            "paymentSystems": [{"name": "bank"}]
        }
    }
    ok, reason = validator.is_basic_ready(of)
    assert ok, f"Debería pasar: {reason}"
    print(f"  ✅ Aceptado: {reason}")
    
    print("\n✅ is_basic_ready OK")
    return True


async def test_validator_pricing_stable():
    """✅ Valida lógica de is_pricing_stable"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("✅ TEST: OrderFormValidator.is_pricing_stable")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    validator = OrderFormValidator()
    
    # Primera captura
    of1 = {
        "orderFormId": "123",
        "items": [{"price": 12299000}],
        "totalizers": [{"value": 12299000}],
        "shippingData": {
            "logisticsInfo": [{"selectedSla": "normal"}]
        }
    }
    
    print("\n  Test 1: Primera captura")
    ok, reason = validator.is_pricing_stable(of1, previous_snapshot=None)
    assert not ok, "Primera captura debería retornar False"
    print(f"  ✅ {reason}")
    
    # Segunda captura (igual)
    print("\n  Test 2: Segunda captura (igual)")
    ok, reason = validator.is_pricing_stable(of1, previous_snapshot=of1)
    assert ok, f"Debería indicar estabilidad: {reason}"
    print(f"  ✅ {reason}")
    
    # Segunda captura (precio cambió)
    print("\n  Test 3: Precio cambió")
    of2 = of1.copy()
    of2["totalizers"] = [{"value": 13000000}]
    ok, reason = validator.is_pricing_stable(of2, previous_snapshot=of1)
    assert not ok, "Debería detectar cambio"
    print(f"  ✅ Detectado: {reason}")
    
    print("\n✅ is_pricing_stable OK")
    return True

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("✅ TEST: DeterministicCheckout")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    try:
        checkout = DeterministicCheckout(base_url="https://www.nike.cl")
        print(f"  ✅ Instancia creada: {checkout}")
        
        required_methods = [
            "run_deterministic_flow",
            "validate_state",
            "set_state"
        ]
        
        for method_name in required_methods:
            if hasattr(checkout, method_name):
                print(f"  ✅ Método: {method_name}")

def main():
    """Ejecuta todos los tests"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("🧪 VALIDACIÓN: FLUJO DETERMINISTA")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    tests = [
        ("Estados", test_states),
        ("OrderFormValidator (instancia)", test_validator_instantiation),
        ("OrderFormValidator (is_basic_ready)", test_validator_basic_ready),
        ("BankPaymentFlow", test_bank_payment_flow_instantiation),
        ("DeterministicCheckout", test_deterministic_checkout_instantiation),
    ]
    
    results = {}
    
    for name, test_fn in tests:
        try:
            if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(test_fn):
                result = asyncio.run(test_fn())
            else:
                result = test_fn()
            results[name] = result
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"\n❌ Error en {name}: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            results[name] = False
    
    # Resumen
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("📊 RESUMEN")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    for name, result in results.items():
        status = "✅" if result else "❌"
        print(f"{status} {name}")
    
    total = sum(1 for r in results.values() if r)
    print(f"\n{total}/{len(results)} tests pasaron")
    
    if total == len(results):
        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print("🎉 TODOS LOS TESTS PASARON")
        print("=" * 60)
        print("\nListos para usar:")
        print("  - account_worker_deterministic(account, live=True)")
        print("  - await checkout.run_deterministic_flow(...)")
        return 0
    else:
        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print("❌ ALGUNOS TESTS FALLARON")
        print("=" * 60)
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_dispatch_and_retry.py
======================================================================

import os
import json
import time

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from core.states import AccountState


class DummyVTEX:
    def __init__(self, fail_times=0):
        self.fail_times = fail_times
        self.calls = 0

    def get_order_form(self):
        return {}

    def has_stock(self, sku, seller):
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, sku, seller):
        self.calls += 1
        if self.calls <= self.fail_times:
            raise Exception("temporary error")
        return {"ok": True}


def test_retry_and_requeue(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "c1"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    # monkeypatch worker's VTEXClient to fail twice then succeed
    # simulate persistent failure (more than max_retries)
    dv = DummyVTEX(fail_times=5)
    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: dv)

    ok, msg = mgr.play("c1", live=True)
    assert ok
    # wait for worker to report a transient failure and manager to increment retry_count (timeout 10s)
    deadline = time.time() + 10.0
    acc = mgr.accounts["c1"]
    while time.time() < deadline and acc.retries < 1:
        time.sleep(0.1)
    assert acc.retries >= 1


def test_dispatch_stagger(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    # create three accounts ready
    for i in range(3):
        a = base / f"a{i}"
        os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
        with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.max_concurrent = 2
    mgr.load_accounts()

    starts = []
    # monkeypatch _start_account to record times
    original = mgr._start_account

    def rec_start(a, live=False):
        starts.append(time.time())
        return original(a, live=live)

    monkeypatch.setattr(mgr, "_start_account", rec_start)

    mgr.start_queue(stagger=0.2)
    # check that at least two starts recorded and difference >= 0.2 between them
    assert len(starts) >= 1
    if len(starts) >= 2:
        assert starts[1] - starts[0] >= 0.19

import os

import time

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from core.states import AccountState


def write_tokens(path, valid=True):
    os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
    data = {}
    if valid:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}
    else:
        data = {"auth_cookie": "", "vtex_session": "", "vtex_segment": ""}
    with open(os.path.join(path, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f)


def write_target(path, sku="111", seller="1"):
    with open(os.path.join(path, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": sku, "seller": seller}, f)


def test_load_and_list(tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "cuentaX"
    write_tokens(a, True)
    write_target(a)
    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()
    la = mgr.list_accounts()
    assert la[0]["state"] == AccountState.READY.name

    mgr.load_accounts()
    ok, msg = mgr.play("cuentaY")
    assert ok
    ok2, msg2 = mgr.stop("cuentaY")
    assert ok2

"""
Tests para SessionValidator - Validación de cookies VTEX

Garantizar que:
1. Valida presencia de cookies VTEX obligatorias
2. Rechaza sesiones incompletas
3. No permite backend calls sin cookies válidas
"""

import pytest
import logging
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch
from requests import Session
from engines.session_validator import SessionValidator

log = logging.getLogger("test_session_validator")


class TestSessionValidator:
    """Tests para validación de sesiones VTEX"""

    def setup_method(self):
        """Setup antes de cada test"""
        self.validator = SessionValidator(log=log)

    def test_valid_session_has_required_cookies(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Sesión válida tiene todas las cookies VTEX
        
        Validar que has_valid_cookies() retorna True cuando:
        - vtex_session está presente
        - vtex_segment está presente (contexto)
        - auth_cookie está presente (usuario logueado)
        """
        session = Session()
        
        # Inyectar cookies válidas
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "valid_session_123")
        session.cookies.set("vtex_segment", "valid_segment_456")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "user_logged_in")
        
        # Ejecutar
        is_valid = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # Verificar
        assert is_valid is True, "Session con todas las cookies debe ser válida"

    def test_invalid_session_missing_vtex_session(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Sesión inválida sin vtex_session
        
        Verificar que has_valid_cookies() retorna False cuando falta vtex_session
        """
        session = Session()
        
        # Solo inyectar algunas cookies (faltan otras)
        session.cookies.set("vtex_segment", "valid_segment_456")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "user_logged_in")
        # FALTA: vtex_session
        
        # Ejecutar
        is_valid = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # Verificar
        assert is_valid is False, "Session sin vtex_session debe ser inválida"

    def test_invalid_session_missing_vtex_segment(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Sesión inválida sin vtex_segment
        
        Verificar que has_valid_cookies() retorna False cuando falta vtex_segment
        """
        session = Session()
        
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "valid_session_123")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "user_logged_in")
        # FALTA: vtex_segment
        
        # Ejecutar
        is_valid = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # Verificar
        assert is_valid is False, "Session sin vtex_segment debe ser inválida"

    def test_empty_session_is_invalid(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Sesión vacía es inválida
        
        Verificar que una Session sin ninguna cookie es rechazada
        """
        session = Session()
        # Sin cookies
        
        # Ejecutar
        is_valid = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # Verificar
        assert is_valid is False, "Session vacía debe ser inválida"

    def test_expired_session_token(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Detectar sesión expirada (token vacío)
        
        Verificar que token vacío o None es rechazado
        """
        session = Session()
        
        # Cookie vacío
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "")
        session.cookies.set("vtex_segment", "")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "")
        
        # Ejecutar
        is_valid = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # Verificar
        assert is_valid is False, "Cookies vacías deben ser rechazadas"

    def test_get_cookie_names_returns_actual_cookies(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: get_cookie_names() retorna lista de cookies presentes
        
        Útil para debugging: saber qué cookies tiene la sesión
        """
        session = Session()
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "val1")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "val2")
        # Faltan algunas
        
        # Ejecutar
        cookie_names = self.validator.get_cookie_names(session)
        
        # Verificar
        assert isinstance(cookie_names, (list, set, str))
        assert "vtex_session" in str(cookie_names)
        assert "auth_cookie" in str(cookie_names)

    def test_validate_checkout_context_guard(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Guardia SessionValidator en account_worker
        
        Simular que antes de hacer backend call, el worker verifica sesión.
        Si sesión es inválida → ABORTA sin hacer request.
        """
        validator = SessionValidator(log=log)
        session = Session()
        
        # Sesión inválida
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "")
        
        # En account_worker, hace check:
        if not validator.has_valid_cookies(session):
            # NO hace backend call
            backend_call_made = False
        else:
            # Sí haría backend call
            backend_call_made = True
        
        # Verificar que NO hizo call
        assert backend_call_made is False, "Worker debe abortar con sesión inválida"

    def test_session_validation_before_atc(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Validar sesión ANTES de add_to_cart
        
        Simular flujo: READY → SESSION_SEED → ATC
        Pero si sesión es inválida en ATC → volver a SESSION_SEED
        """
        from core.bot_state import BotState
        
        validator = SessionValidator(log=log)
        session = Session()
        
        # Sesión incompleta
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "abc123")
        # FALTA vtex_segment
        
        is_valid = validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # SimularStateMachine behavior:
        if not is_valid:
            # Volver a SESSION_SEED
            next_state = BotState.SESSION_SEED
        else:
            # Continuar a ATC
            next_state = BotState.ATC
        
        # Verificar
        assert next_state == BotState.SESSION_SEED, \
            "StateMachine debe volver a SESSION_SEED si sesión es inválida"

    def test_multiple_validation_calls_are_idempotent(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Múltiples calls a has_valid_cookies() son idempotentes
        
        Verificar que validar la misma sesión 2 veces da mismo resultado
        """
        session = Session()
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "abc123")
        session.cookies.set("vtex_segment", "def456")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "ghi789")
        
        # Validar 3 veces
        result1 = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        result2 = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        result3 = self.validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        # Todos deben ser iguales
        assert result1 == result2 == result3 == True


class TestSessionValidatorIntegration:
    """Integration tests con flujo completo"""

    def test_session_guard_prevents_backend_calls_without_cookies(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Guard en account_worker previene backend calls
        
        Simular:
        1. account_worker intenta hacer backend call
        2. SessionValidator bloquea (sesión inválida)
        3. account_worker aborta sin hacer request
        """
        validator = SessionValidator(log=log)
        session = Session()
        
        # Sesión vacía (usuario nunca logueó)
        
        # Simulate account_worker check:
        if not validator.has_valid_cookies(session):
            # Worker aborta acá mismo
            account_state = "ERROR"
            backend_requests_made = 0
        else:
            # Nunca llega acá
            account_state = "RUNNING"
            backend_requests_made = 1
        
        # Verificar
        assert account_state == "ERROR"
        assert backend_requests_made == 0

    def test_valid_session_allows_backend_calls(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Sesión válida permite backend calls
        
        Simular:
        1. Sesión tiene todas las cookies
        2. SessionValidator aprueba
        3. account_worker continúa a backend
        """
        validator = SessionValidator(log=log)
        session = Session()
        
        # Sesión VÁLIDA
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "valid_123")
        session.cookies.set("vtex_segment", "valid_456")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "logged_in")
        
        # Simulate account_worker check:
        if not validator.has_valid_cookies(session):
            account_state = "ERROR"
            can_call_backend = False
        else:
            account_state = "RUNNING"
            can_call_backend = True
        
        # Verificar
        assert account_state == "RUNNING"
        assert can_call_backend is True


if __name__ == "__main__":
    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])


======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_state_machine.py
======================================================================

import pytest
import time

from runner.state_machine import StateMachine, AccountState, Event, InvalidTransition, GlobalState


def test_basic_transitions():
    sm = StateMachine(max_concurrent=2, heartbeat_timeout=1.0)
    sm.add_account("a", AccountState.NO_AUTH)
    sm.add_account("b", AccountState.READY)

    # LOGIN moves NO_AUTH -> READY
    st = sm.dispatch("a", Event.LOGIN)
    assert st == AccountState.READY

    # PLAY moves READY -> RUNNING
    st = sm.dispatch("b", Event.PLAY)
    assert st == AccountState.RUNNING

    # PAUSE then RESUME
    st = sm.dispatch("b", Event.PAUSE)
    assert st == AccountState.PAUSED
    st = sm.dispatch("b", Event.RESUME)
    assert st == AccountState.RUNNING

    # STOP moves RUNNING -> READY
    st = sm.dispatch("b", Event.STOP)
    assert st == AccountState.READY


def test_forbidden_transitions():
    sm = StateMachine()
    sm.add_account("x", AccountState.NO_AUTH)
    with pytest.raises(InvalidTransition):
        sm.dispatch("x", Event.PLAY)

    sm.add_account("y", AccountState.ERROR)
    with pytest.raises(InvalidTransition):
        sm.dispatch("y", Event.PLAY)


def test_max_concurrency():
    sm = StateMachine(max_concurrent=1)
    sm.add_account("a", AccountState.READY)
    sm.add_account("b", AccountState.READY)

    sm.dispatch("a", Event.PLAY)
    with pytest.raises(InvalidTransition):
        sm.dispatch("b", Event.PLAY)


def test_heartbeat_timeout_moves_to_error():
    sm = StateMachine(heartbeat_timeout=0.1)
    sm.add_account("a", AccountState.READY)
    sm.dispatch("a", Event.PLAY)
    # wait longer than timeout
    time.sleep(0.15)
    sm.check_heartbeats()
    assert sm.get_account_state("a") == AccountState.ERROR


def test_global_state_derivation():
    sm = StateMachine()
    sm.add_account("a", AccountState.READY)
    sm.add_account("b", AccountState.NO_AUTH)
    assert sm.derive_global_state() == GlobalState.IDLE

    sm.dispatch("a", Event.PLAY)
    assert sm.derive_global_state() == GlobalState.RUNNING

    sm.add_account("c", AccountState.READY)
    # a RUNNING + c READY -> PARTIAL
    assert sm.derive_global_state() == GlobalState.PARTIAL


======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_state_machine_flow.py

"""
Tests para flujo completo: StateMachine.run()

Validar que el flujo de compra funciona end-to-end:
READY → SESSION_SEED → ATC → PRICING_WAIT → PRICING_FROZEN → PAYMENT → AWAITING_HUMAN

Nota: Tests usan mocks, no hace requests reales.
"""

import pytest
import logging
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch, MagicMock
from requests import Session
from core.bot_state import BotState

log = logging.getLogger("test_state_machine")


class TestStateMachineFlow:
    """Tests para flujo completo de StateMachine"""

    def setup_method(self):
        """Setup antes de cada test"""
        # No importamos StateMachine acá para evitar dependencias
        # pero validamos estados y transiciones lógicamente

    def test_state_machine_initial_state_is_ready(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: StateMachine comienza en READY
        
        Verificar que inicial_state = BotState.READY
        """
        # En vez de instanciar StateMachine, validamos el enum
        assert hasattr(BotState, "READY")
        assert BotState.READY is not None

    def test_valid_state_transitions_sequence(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Transiciones de estado son válidas
        
        Verificar que la secuencia de estados tiene sentido:
        READY → SESSION_SEED → ATC → PRICING_WAIT → PRICING_FROZEN → 
        PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED → BANK_MODAL_OPEN → AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
        """
        valid_sequence = [
            BotState.READY,
            BotState.SESSION_SEED,
            BotState.ATC,
            BotState.PRICING_WAIT,
            BotState.PRICING_FROZEN,
            BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED,
            BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN,
            BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION,
        ]
        
        # Verificar que todos los estados existen
        for state in valid_sequence:
            assert hasattr(BotState, state.name), f"State {state.name} debe existir"

    def test_terminal_states_are_defined(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Estados terminales están definidos
        
        Estados donde StateMachine DETIENE:
        - AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION (éxito)
        - PAYMENT_FAILED (error)
        """
        assert hasattr(BotState, "AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION")
        assert hasattr(BotState, "PAYMENT_FAILED")

    def test_error_state_on_session_seed_failure(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Si SESSION_SEED falla → PAYMENT_FAILED
        
        Simular:
        1. StateMachine en SESSION_SEED
        2. Error al obtener cookies (profile_pw vacío)
        3. Transición a PAYMENT_FAILED
        """
        # Simular transición
        current_state = BotState.SESSION_SEED
        error_getting_cookies = True
        
        if error_getting_cookies:
            next_state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
        else:
            next_state = BotState.ATC
        
        # Verificar
        assert next_state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def test_error_state_on_atc_failure(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Si ATC falla → PAYMENT_FAILED
        
        Simular:
        1. StateMachine en ATC
        2. add_to_cart retorna error (SKU no encontrado)
        3. Transición a PAYMENT_FAILED
        """
        current_state = BotState.ATC
        atc_success = False
        
        if not atc_success:
            next_state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
        else:
            next_state = BotState.PRICING_WAIT
        
        assert next_state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def test_error_state_on_pricing_timeout(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Si PRICING_WAIT timeout → PAYMENT_FAILED
        
        Simular:
        1. StateMachine en PRICING_WAIT
        2. wait_until_ready() timeout (precio nunca llegó)
        3. Transición a PAYMENT_FAILED
        """
        current_state = BotState.PRICING_WAIT
        pricing_ready = False
        timeout = True
        
        if timeout or not pricing_ready:
            next_state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
        else:
            next_state = BotState.PRICING_FROZEN
        
        assert next_state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED

    def test_success_path_complete_flow(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Camino feliz: READY → ... → AWAITING_HUMAN
        
        Simular flujo exitoso de principio a fin
        """
        states = [
            BotState.READY,
            BotState.SESSION_SEED,
            BotState.ATC,
            BotState.PRICING_WAIT,
            BotState.PRICING_FROZEN,
            BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED,
            BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN,
            BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION,  # ← Terminal exitoso
        ]
        
        # Simular máquina recorriendo todos los estados
        for i, state in enumerate(states):
            if i == len(states) - 1:
                # Último estado es terminal
                assert state == BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION

    def test_anti_loop_session_seed_max_attempts(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: Anti-loop SESSION_SEED: máx 3 intentos
        
        Si SESSION_SEED falla 3 veces → ABORT con PAYMENT_FAILED
        Prevenir loops infinitos
        """
        max_seed_attempts = 3
        current_attempts = 0
        
        while current_attempts < max_seed_attempts:
            current_attempts += 1
            session_seed_success = False  # Siempre falla
            
            if not session_seed_success:
                if current_attempts >= max_seed_attempts:
                    state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
                    reason = f"SESSION_SEED loop: {current_attempts} attempts"
                    break
        
        # Verificar
        assert state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
        assert current_attempts == max_seed_attempts

    def test_pricing_freeze_snapshot_validation(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: PRICING_FROZEN valida precio con 2 snapshots
        
        Si precio cambia entre snapshot 1 y 2 (400ms) → fallo
        """
        # Snapshot 1
        snapshot_1_price = 50000
        
        # Wait 400ms...
        
        # Snapshot 2
        snapshot_2_price = 60000  # Precio cambió!
        
        # Si precios son diferentes
        if snapshot_1_price != snapshot_2_price:
            next_state = BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
            reason = "Precio cambió durante freeze"
        else:
            next_state = BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
        
        # Verificar
        assert next_state == BotState.PAYMENT_FAILED
        assert "Precio" in reason or "price" in reason.lower()

    def test_checkout_orchestrator_integration(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: CheckoutOrchestrator es llamado desde BANK_MODAL_OPEN
        
        Verificar que StateMachine llama CheckoutOrchestrator.run_bank_flow()
        y NO usa subprocess
        """
        from engines.checkout_orchestrator import CheckoutOrchestrator
        
        # Verificar que CheckoutOrchestrator existe
        assert hasattr(CheckoutOrchestrator, "run_bank_flow")
        
        # Verificar que tiene método run_bank_flow
        mock_page = Mock()
        orchestrator = CheckoutOrchestrator(page=mock_page)
        assert callable(orchestrator.run_bank_flow)

    def test_no_subprocess_in_state_machine(self):
        """
        ✅ TEST: StateMachine NO usa subprocess (refactorizado)
        
        Verificar que _transition_bank_modal_open() usa CheckoutOrchestrator
        y NO llama subprocess.Popen()
        """
        # Leer runtime/worker.py
        try:
            with open("runtime/worker.py", "r") as f:
                worker_code = f.read()
                
                # Verificar que NO hay subprocess en account_worker refactorizado
                # (buscar la parte refactorizada que llama StateMachine)
                assert "StateMachine" in worker_code, \
                    "Worker debe llamar StateMachine"
        except FileNotFoundError:
            # Si no está en workspace, validar en runner/state_machine.py
            with open("runner/state_machine.py", "r") as f:
                sm_code = f.read()
                
                # Verificar que CheckoutOrchestrator es usada
                assert "CheckoutOrchestrator" in sm_code, \
                    "StateMachine debe usar CheckoutOrchestrator"
                
                # Verificar que NO usa subprocess.Popen
                assert "subprocess.Popen" not in sm_code, \
                    "StateMachine debe evitar subprocess.Popen"


class TestStateTransitions:
    """Tests específicos para transiciones entre estados"""

    def test_transition_ready_to_session_seed(self):
        """READY → SESSION_SEED"""
        current = BotState.READY
        
        # Validar página está disponible
        page_available = True
        
        if page_available:
            next_state = BotState.SESSION_SEED
        
        assert next_state == BotState.SESSION_SEED

    def test_transition_session_seed_to_atc(self):
        """SESSION_SEED → ATC (si cookies son válidas)"""
        from engines.session_validator import SessionValidator
        
        current = BotState.SESSION_SEED
        session = Session()
        
        # Cookies válidas
        session.cookies.set("vtex_session", "valid")
        session.cookies.set("vtex_segment", "valid")
        session.cookies.set("auth_cookie", "valid")
        
        validator = SessionValidator()
        cookies_valid = validator.has_valid_cookies(session)
        
        if cookies_valid:
            next_state = BotState.ATC
        
        assert next_state == BotState.ATC

    def test_transition_atc_to_pricing_wait(self):
        """ATC → PRICING_WAIT (si add_to_cart exitoso)"""
        current = BotState.ATC
        atc_success = True
        
        if atc_success:
            next_state = BotState.PRICING_WAIT
        
        assert next_state == BotState.PRICING_WAIT

    def test_transition_pricing_wait_to_frozen(self):
        """PRICING_WAIT → PRICING_FROZEN (si precio > 0)"""
        current = BotState.PRICING_WAIT
        price = 50000  # Válido
        
        if price > 0:
            next_state = BotState.PRICING_FROZEN
        
        assert next_state == BotState.PRICING_FROZEN

    def test_transition_frozen_to_bank_selected(self):
        """PRICING_FROZEN → PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED (si freeze OK)"""
        current = BotState.PRICING_FROZEN
        freeze_success = True
        
        if freeze_success:
            next_state = BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
        
        assert next_state == BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED

    def test_transition_bank_selected_to_modal_open(self):
        """PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED → BANK_MODAL_OPEN (si set_bank_payment OK)"""
        current = BotState.PAYMENT_BANK_SELECTED
        set_payment_success = True
        
        if set_payment_success:
            next_state = BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN
        
        assert next_state == BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN

    def test_transition_modal_open_to_awaiting_human(self):
        """BANK_MODAL_OPEN → AWAITING_HUMAN (si checkout OK)"""
        current = BotState.BANK_MODAL_OPEN
        checkout_success = True
        
        if checkout_success:
            next_state = BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION
        
        assert next_state == BotState.AWAITING_HUMAN_CONFIRMATION


if __name__ == "__main__":
    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])

FILE: tests\test_worker.py

import os
import json
import time

import pytest
from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from vtex.vtex_client import VTEXClient
from core.states import AccountState


class DummyClient:
    def __init__(self):
        self.get_order_form_called = False

    def get_order_form(self):
        self.get_order_form_called = True
        return {}

    def has_stock(self, sku, seller):
        return sku == "111"


def test_worker_dry_run(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "cuentaW"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    # monkeypatch VTEXClient used by worker
    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: DummyClient())

    ok, msg = mgr.play("cuentaW")
    assert ok
    time.sleep(0.2)

    # dry-run should NOT call add_to_cart; verify by checking that thread ran and then stop
    mgr.stop("cuentaW")
    time.sleep(0.1)
    assert mgr.accounts["cuentaW"].state in (AccountState.READY, AccountState.ERROR)
    mgr.accounts["cuentaW"].kill_event.set()


def test_worker_live_add_to_cart(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "cuentaLive"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    class LiveDummy(DummyClient):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__()
            self.added = False

        def add_to_cart(self, sku, seller):
            self.added = True
            return {"ok": True}

    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: LiveDummy())

    ok, msg = mgr.play("cuentaLive", live=True)
    assert ok
    time.sleep(0.3)
    # after a short period, worker should have attempted add_to_cart and exited (we break on success)
    mgr.stop("cuentaLive")
    time.sleep(0.1)
    assert mgr.accounts["cuentaLive"].state in (AccountState.READY, AccountState.ERROR)
    mgr.accounts["cuentaLive"].kill_event.set()


======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_worker_checkout.py

import os
import json
import time

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from core.states import AccountState


class CheckoutClient:
    def __init__(self, price_seq):
        self.price_seq = list(price_seq)
        self.checkout_called = False
        self.added = False

    def get_order_form(self):
        price = self.price_seq[0]
        if len(self.price_seq) > 1:
            self.price_seq.pop(0)
        # include orderFormId and value for checkout
        return {"items": [{"id": "111", "sellingPrice": price}], "orderFormId": "of1", "value": price}

    def has_stock(self, sku, seller):
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, sku, seller):
        self.added = True
        return {"ok": True}

    def get_payment_systems(self, order_form):
        # default: MercadoPago available
        return [{"id": 125, "name": "MercadoPago", "groupName": "MercadoPago"}]

    def find_mercadopago_id(self, order_form):
        systems = self.get_payment_systems(order_form)
        for ps in systems:
            name = (ps.get("name") or "").lower()
            if "mercado" in name:
                return ps.get("id")
        return None

    def checkout_with_payment_system(self, order_form, payment_system_id: int):
        self.checkout_called = True
        return {"ok": True}

def test_manual_checkout_no_auto(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "c1"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    client = CheckoutClient([0, 0, 1000])
    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: client)
    monkeypatch.setattr("vtex.vtex_client.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: client)

    ok, msg = mgr.play("c1", live=True, max_price_attempts=5)
    assert ok
    time.sleep(1.5)

    acc = mgr.accounts["c1"]
    assert acc.last_price == 1000
    assert client.checkout_called is False
    # wait up to 3s for status to be set by worker
    deadline = time.time() + 3.0
    while time.time() < deadline and acc.last_status != "MANUAL_READY":
        time.sleep(0.05)
    assert acc.last_status == "MANUAL_READY"


def test_auto_checkout_mercadopago_success(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "c2"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)
    # config: auto checkout mercadopago
    with open(os.path.join(a, "config.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auto_checkout": True, "payment_provider": "mercadopago"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    client = CheckoutClient([0, 1000])
    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: client)
    monkeypatch.setattr("vtex.vtex_client.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: client)

    ok, msg = mgr.play("c2", live=True, max_price_attempts=5)
    assert ok
    time.sleep(1.5)

    acc = mgr.accounts["c2"]
    assert client.checkout_called is True
    assert acc.last_error is None
    assert acc.last_status == "CHECKOUT_SENT"

        json.dump({"auto_checkout": True, "wallet": {"primary": "mercadopago", "fallback": "creditcard"}}, f)

FILE: tests\test_worker_price.py

import os
import json
import time

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from core.states import AccountState


class PriceSequenceClient:
    def __init__(self, price_seq):
        self.price_seq = list(price_seq)
        self.added = False

    def get_order_form(self):
        # return current price and then advance the sequence (simulate publish delay)
        price = self.price_seq[0]
        # advance sequence if more items exist
        if len(self.price_seq) > 1:
            self.price_seq.pop(0)
        return {"items": [{"id": "111", "sellingPrice": price}]}

    def has_stock(self, sku, seller):
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, sku, seller):
        self.added = True
        return {"ok": True}

    def pop_price(self):
        if len(self.price_seq) > 1:
            self.price_seq.pop(0)


def test_price_wait_success(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "p1"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    # client returns price 0 first, then price 1000
    client = PriceSequenceClient([0, 0, 1000])

    def maker(base_url, session=None):
        return client

    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", maker)

    ok, msg = mgr.play("p1", live=True, max_price_attempts=5)
    assert ok
    # allow worker to run and check orderForm a few times
    time.sleep(2.0)

    acc = mgr.accounts["p1"]
    # price should be observed and stored
    assert acc.last_price == 1000


def test_price_wait_timeout(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    base = tmp_path / "auth"
    a = base / "p2"
    os.makedirs(a, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "tokens.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"auth_cookie": "x", "vtex_session": "y", "vtex_segment": "z"}, f)
    with open(os.path.join(a, "target.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"sku": "111", "seller": "1"}, f)

    mgr = AccountManager(auth_path=str(base))
    mgr.load_accounts()

    # always price 0
    client = PriceSequenceClient([0, 0, 0, 0])

    monkeypatch.setattr("runtime.worker.VTEXClient", lambda base_url, session=None: client)

    ok, msg = mgr.play("p2", live=True, max_price_attempts=2)
    assert ok
    # allow worker to run and timeout
    time.sleep(1.5)

    acc = mgr.accounts["p2"]
    # after timeout, account should be READY with next_retry_at set or ERROR if exceeded
    assert acc.state in (AccountState.READY, AccountState.ERROR)
    assert acc.last_error is not None


======================================================================
FILE: tools\cookie_extractor.py
======================================================================

import os
import json
import base64
import sqlite3
import shutil
import tempfile
import win32crypt
from Crypto.Cipher import AES  # pip install pycryptodome

PROFILE_PATH = r"C:/Users/beriann/Documents/Repos/nike/chrome_profiles/bot_9222"
OUTPUT = "cookies_extracted.json"

TARGET_DOMAINS = [
    "nike.cl",
    ".nike.cl",
    "www.nike.cl",
    "checkout.vtex.com",
    ".checkout.vtex.com",
    "vtex.com",
]


def get_chrome_key():
    """Extract Chrome AES key from Local State."""
    local_state_path = os.path.join(PROFILE_PATH, "Local State")

    with open(local_state_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        local_state = json.load(f)

    encrypted_key = base64.b64decode(local_state["os_crypt"]["encrypted_key"])[5:]
    return win32crypt.CryptUnprotectData(encrypted_key, None, None, None, 0)[1]


def decrypt_cookie(encrypted_value, key):
    """Decrypt Chrome AES-GCM cookie."""
    try:
        if encrypted_value.startswith(b"v10") or encrypted_value.startswith(b"v11"):
            iv = encrypted_value[3:15]
            payload = encrypted_value[15:]
            cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_GCM, iv)
            decrypted = cipher.decrypt(payload)[:-16]
            return decrypted.decode()
        else:
            return win32crypt.CryptUnprotectData(encrypted_value, None, None, None, 0)[1].decode()
    except:
        return None


def find_cookie_db(path):
    for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(path):
        if "Cookies" in filenames:
            return os.path.join(dirpath, "Cookies")
    return None


def extract():
    key = get_chrome_key()

    cookie_path = find_cookie_db(PROFILE_PATH)
    print(f"🟢 AES Key OK")
    print(f"🟢 Cookie DB: {cookie_path}")

    # -------------------------
    # 🔥 FIX: copiar archivo Cookies a temp
    # -------------------------
    temp_cookie = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "Cookies_temp.db")
    shutil.copy2(cookie_path, temp_cookie)

    # abrir copia desbloqueada
    conn = sqlite3.connect(temp_cookie)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT host_key, name, encrypted_value FROM cookies")
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()

    result = {}
    for host, name, enc in rows:
        if not any(domain in host for domain in TARGET_DOMAINS):
            continue
        value = decrypt_cookie(enc, key)
        result[name] = value

    with open(OUTPUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(result, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)

    print("🔥 Cookies desencriptadas → cookies_extracted.json")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    extract()

FILE: tools\payment_launcher.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
MODE B FINAL - Abre Chrome REAL con sesion Nike y entra directo a PAYMENT.
NO toca carrito. NO pierde precio.
"""

import sys
import time
import os
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

def main():
    account_path = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None
    account_name = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "ACCOUNT"
    
    if not account_path:
        print("[LAUNCHER] ERROR: Falta account_path")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    profile_dir = os.path.join(account_path, "profile_pw")
    
    if not os.path.exists(profile_dir):
        print("[LAUNCHER] ERROR: No existe {}".format(profile_dir))
        print("[LAUNCHER] Debes loguearte una vez en Nike antes")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    print("[{}] HANDOVER A PAGO (MODE B REAL)".format(account_name))
    
    try:
        with sync_playwright() as p:
            print("[{}] Abriendo Chrome con perfil REAL...".format(account_name))
            
            # Obtener ruta de la extensión
            repo_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
            extension_path = os.path.join(repo_root, "extension")
            
            browser = p.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=profile_dir,
                headless=False,
                args=[
                    "--start-maximized",
                    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
                    f"--load-extension={extension_path}",
                    "--disable-extensions-except=" + extension_path
                ],
                viewport=None
            )
            
            page = browser.pages[0] if browser.pages else browser.new_page()
            page.bring_to_front()
            
            print("[{}] Abriendo PAYMENT directo (extension activa)...".format(account_name))
            page.goto("https://www.nike.cl/checkout/#/payment", timeout=0)
            
            try:
                page.add_style_tag(content="""
                .ReactModal__Overlay,
                .newsletter-modal,
                iframe[src*="recaptcha"] {
                    display: none !important;
                }
                """)
            except:
                pass
            
            print("")
            print("=" * 50)
            print("[{}] CHECKOUT ABIERTO".format(account_name))
            print("[{}] -> SOLO CONFIRMA Y PAGA".format(account_name))
            print("[{}] -> NO REFRESCAR".format(account_name))
            print("=" * 50)
            print("")
            
            while True:
                time.sleep(1)
    
    except Exception as e:
        print("[{}] ERROR: {}".format(account_name, e))
        import traceback
        print(traceback.format_exc())
        sys.exit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import customtkinter as ctk

class AccountEditor(ctk.CTkToplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, account, manager):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.account = account
        self.manager = manager
        
        self.title(f"Edit: {account.name}")
        self.geometry("500x550")
        self.resizable(False, False)
        
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()
        
        self._build()

    def _build(self):
        main_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self)
        main_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=20, pady=20)

        # 1. SKU
        ctk.CTkLabel(main_frame, text="🗝️ SKU (Crítico en drops)", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w")
        self.sku_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(main_frame, placeholder_text="Ej: 123456")
        self.sku_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(5, 20))
        if self.account.sku:
            self.sku_entry.insert(0, self.account.sku)

        # 2. SELLER ID
        ctk.CTkLabel(main_frame, text="Seller ID", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w")
        self.seller_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(main_frame)
        self.seller_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(5, 20))
        seller_val = getattr(self.account, 'seller_id', self.account.seller or "1")
        self.seller_entry.insert(0, seller_val if seller_val else "1")

        # 3. AUTO CHECKOUT (Checkbox)
        self.auto_buy_var = ctk.BooleanVar(value=self.account.auto_checkout)
        chk = ctk.CTkCheckBox(main_frame, text="Auto Checkout", variable=self.auto_buy_var)
        chk.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 20))

        # 4. MÉTODO DE PAGO (Radio Buttons)
        ctk.CTkLabel(main_frame, text="Default Payment Method", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 10))
        
        payment_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(main_frame)
        payment_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 20))

        payment_method = getattr(self.account, 'payment_method', getattr(self.account, 'payment_mode', 'manual'))
        self.payment_var = ctk.StringVar(value=payment_method)
        
        options = [
            ("🏦 Transferencia banco (RECOMENDADO)", "transfer"),
            ("💳 MercadoPago", "mercadopago"),
            ("💳 Tarjeta", "credit_card"),
            ("🧍 Manual (solo preparar)", "manual")
        ]

        for text, val in options:
            r = ctk.CTkRadioButton(payment_frame, text=text, variable=self.payment_var, value=val)
            r.pack(anchor="w", padx=10, pady=5)

        # ESPACIADOR
        ctk.CTkLabel(main_frame, text="").pack(pady=5)

        # BOTÓN GIGANTE DE GUARDAR
        self.save_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
            main_frame, 
            text="💾 GUARDAR CAMBIOS", 
            fg_color="green", 
            hover_color="darkgreen",
            height=45,
            font=("Arial", 14, "bold"),
            command=self._save_and_close
        )
        self.save_btn.pack(fill="x", pady=10)

    def _save_and_close(self):
        print(f"💾 Guardando datos para {self.account.name}...")

        # 1. Guardar SKU
        new_sku = self.sku_entry.get().strip()
        self.account.sku = new_sku if new_sku else None
        
        # 2. Guardar Seller ID
        seller_val = self.seller_entry.get().strip()
        if hasattr(self.account, 'seller_id'):
            self.account.seller_id = seller_val
        else:
            self.account.seller = seller_val

        # 3. Guardar Método
        payment_val = self.payment_var.get()
        if hasattr(self.account, 'payment_method'):
            self.account.payment_method = payment_val
        else:
            self.account.payment_mode = payment_val

        # 4. Guardar Auto Checkout
        self.account.auto_checkout = self.auto_buy_var.get()

        # 5. PERSISTIR EN DISCO - Usar el manager
        try:
            if self.manager:
                ok, msg = self.manager.save_accounts()
                print(f"✅ {msg}")
            else:
                print("⚠️ Error: No hay manager para guardar.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Error guardando: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()

        print("🔒 Cerrando ventana del editor...")
        self.destroy()
