# ui/app.py - SIMPLIFICADO Y FUNCIONAL
import customtkinter as ctk
import threading
import logging
import os
from collections import deque

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from controller.controller import Controller
from core.events import EventType
from core.states import AccountState
from ui.account_editor import AccountEditor
from runtime.bot_controller import BotController
from core.account_state import AccountState as BotAccountState


# ============================================================================
# HELPERS DE PATHS (centralizado, sin magic strings)
# ============================================================================

def profile_login_path(account_name: str) -> str:
    """Ruta a profile_login (MASTER - donde usuario loguea)"""
    return os.path.abspath(f"auth/{account_name}/profile_login")

def profile_run_path(account_name: str) -> str:
    """Ruta a profile_run (SLAVE - donde bot ejecuta)"""
    return os.path.abspath(f"auth/{account_name}/profile_run")


class UILogHandler(logging.Handler):
    """Handler custom que envía logs a la UI"""
    
    def __init__(self, max_logs: int = 200):
        super().__init__()
        self.logs = deque(maxlen=max_logs)
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
    
    def emit(self, record):
        try:
            msg = self.format(record)
            with self.lock:
                self.logs.append(msg)
            print(msg)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error en UILogHandler: {e}")
            self.handleError(record)


class AppUI:
    def __init__(self, manager: AccountManager, controller: Controller):
        self.manager = manager
        self.controller = controller
        self.rows = {}
        self.controllers = {}  # NEW: {account_name: BotController}
        self.log_handler = UILogHandler()
        
        ctk.set_appearance_mode("System")
        ctk.set_default_color_theme("dark-blue")

        self.root = ctk.CTk()
        self.root.title("Nike Bot - Control")
        self.root.geometry("1200x700")

        self._setup_logging()
        self._build_ui()
        self._refresh_loop()

    def _setup_logging(self):
        """Configura el handler de logs personalizado"""
        logging.root.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
        
        if not any(isinstance(h, UILogHandler) for h in logging.root.handlers):
            logging.root.addHandler(self.log_handler)
        
        formatter = logging.Formatter("[%(name)s] %(message)s")
        self.log_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        self.log_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

    def _build_ui(self):
        """Construye la UI principal"""
        # Top bar
        top = ctk.CTkFrame(self.root)
        top.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=6)

        ctk.CTkLabel(top, text="Max concurrent:").pack(side="left", padx=6)
        self.var_concurrent = ctk.StringVar(value=str(self.manager.max_concurrent))

        ctk.CTkEntry(top, width=60, textvariable=self.var_concurrent).pack(side="left")
        ctk.CTkButton(top, text="Aplicar", command=self._apply_concurrency).pack(side="left", padx=6)
        ctk.CTkButton(top, text="🔍 Check Sessions", fg_color="orange", command=self._check_all_sessions).pack(side="left", padx=6)
        ctk.CTkButton(top, text="▶ Run all READY", command=self._run_all).pack(side="left", padx=10)
        ctk.CTkButton(top, text="🟥 STOP ALL", fg_color="red", command=self._stop_all).pack(side="right", padx=6)

        # Main container
        main_container = ctk.CTkFrame(self.root)
        main_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=6)

        # Panel izquierdo: Cuentas
        left_panel = ctk.CTkFrame(main_container)
        left_panel.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True, padx=(0, 5))

        ctk.CTkLabel(left_panel, text="📋 Cuentas", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=4)

        self.container = ctk.CTkFrame(left_panel)
        self.container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        self._build_accounts()

        # Panel derecho: Logs
        right_panel = ctk.CTkFrame(main_container, width=300)
        right_panel.pack(side="right", fill="both", expand=True, padx=(5, 0))
        right_panel.pack_propagate(False)

        ctk.CTkLabel(right_panel, text="📝 Logs en vivo", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=4)

        self.log_textbox = ctk.CTkTextbox(right_panel, height=300, state="disabled", wrap="word", font=("Courier", 9))
        self.log_textbox.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        ctk.CTkButton(right_panel, text="🗑️ Limpiar logs", width=100, command=self._clear_logs).pack(pady=4)

    def _build_accounts(self):
        """Construye las filas de cuentas"""
        for w in self.container.winfo_children():
            w.destroy()
        self.rows = {}
        self.controllers = {}  # NEW: Reiniciar controladores

        for name, acc in self.manager.accounts.items():
            profile_path = os.path.join(acc.account_path, "profile_pw")
            controller = BotController(name, profile_path)
            self.controllers[name] = controller
            
            print(f"[UI] {name}: {controller.get_status_text()}")
            self._add_account_row(name, acc)

    def _add_account_row(self, name, acc, assume_logged=True):
        """Construye una fila de cuenta con BotController"""
        controller = self.controllers.get(name)
        if not controller:
            return  # No controller, skip row
        
        row = ctk.CTkFrame(self.container)
        row.pack(fill="x", padx=6, pady=4)

        # Indicador de sesión y estado desde controller
        status_text = controller.get_status_text()  # ej: "READY" o "LOGIN_IN_PROGRESS"
        session_color = "#00FF00" if controller.state == BotAccountState.READY else "#FF6B6B"
        session_indicator = f"✅ {status_text}" if controller.state == BotAccountState.READY else f"⏳ {status_text}"
        
        lbl_session = ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=session_indicator, width=160, anchor="w", text_color=session_color, font=("Arial", 10, "bold"))
        lbl_session.pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # Nombre
        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=name, width=100, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
        
        # Estado del controller
        lbl_state = ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=f"[{status_text}]", width=150, font=("Arial", 9, "bold"))
        lbl_state.pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # Diagnóstico
        lbl_diag = ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=acc.last_status or "—", width=160, anchor="w", font=("Arial", 9))
        lbl_diag.pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # 🎨 SKU
        sku_display = f"🔑 {acc.sku}" if acc.sku else "❌ SIN SKU"
        sku_color = "#00FF00" if acc.sku else "#FF6B6B"
        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=sku_display, text_color=sku_color, font=("Arial", 10, "bold"), width=120, anchor="w").pack(side="left", padx=6)
        
        # 💳 Método de pago
        payment_mode = getattr(acc, 'payment_mode', 'manual')
        payment_display = {
            'bank': '🏦 Banco',
            'mercadopago': '💳 MP',
            'card': '💳 Tarjeta',
            'manual': '🧍 Manual'
        }.get(payment_mode, payment_mode)
        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=f"({payment_display})", font=("Arial", 9), width=80).pack(side="left", padx=3)

        # Botones controlados por estado
        # LOGIN button (enabled si controller.is_login_enabled())
        btn_login = ctk.CTkButton(
            row, text="🔐 LOGIN", width=90, fg_color="orange",
            state="normal" if controller.is_login_enabled() else "disabled",
            command=lambda: self._login_v2(controller, name)
        )
        btn_login.pack(side="right", padx=3)
        
        # PLAY button (enabled si controller.is_play_enabled())
        btn_play = ctk.CTkButton(
            row, text="▶ PLAY", width=90, fg_color="green",
            state="normal" if controller.is_play_enabled() else "disabled",
            command=lambda: self._play_v2(controller, name, acc.sku)
        )
        btn_play.pack(side="right", padx=3)
        
        # EDIT button
        btn_edit = ctk.CTkButton(
            row, text="✏ Edit", width=70,
            command=lambda: self._edit(acc)
        )
        btn_edit.pack(side="right", padx=3)

        # Guardar referencias en self.rows para actualizar después
        self.rows[name] = {
            "frame": row,
            "state": lbl_state, 
            "session": lbl_session, 
            "diag": lbl_diag,
            "btn_login": btn_login,
            "btn_play": btn_play,
            "controller": controller
        }

    def _rebuild_single_account(self, account_name, account):
        """Reconstruye una sola fila"""
        self._build_accounts()

    def _login_v2(self, controller, account_name: str):
        """
        🔐 LOGIN v2 - Usa subprocess (PROCESO SEPARADO)
        
        ⚠️ CRÍTICO: Usa subprocess, NO thread
        Razón: Tkinter + thread + Playwright = deadlock silencioso
        
        Solución: login_runner.py es un script INDEPENDIENTE
        que corre en otro proceso sin compartir event loop
        """
        print(f"\n[UI] [LOGIN] LOGIN_v2 iniciado: {account_name}")
        
        import subprocess
        import sys
        
        try:
            # Obtener ruta del perfil LOGIN desde helpers
            print(f"[UI] [SUBPROCESS] Lanzando login_runner.py...")
            print(f"[UI] Cuenta: {account_name}")
            
            # [OK] LANZAR COMO SUBPROCESS (proceso separado, sin bloqueo)
            # Pasar SOLO el nombre de la cuenta (no paths)
            # login_runner.py construye el path internamente
            process = subprocess.Popen([
                sys.executable,
                "login_runner.py",
                account_name
            ])
            
            # NO esperar (no bloqueante)
            print(f"[UI] [OK] login_runner.py lanzado (subprocess)")
            print(f"[UI] Chrome debería abrirse ahora...")
            
            # Monitorear proceso en background
            # Cuando termine, actualizar UI
            def monitor_process():
                process.wait()  # Bloquea AQUÍ (en thread)
                print(f"[UI] 📍 login_runner.py finalizó")
                self.root.after(0, lambda: self._refresh_single_row(account_name))
            
            # Lanzar monitor en thread (pero es muy light, solo espera)
            monitor_thread = threading.Thread(target=monitor_process, daemon=True)
            monitor_thread.start()
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[UI] ❌ ERROR en login_v2 ({account_name}): {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            self._refresh_single_row(account_name)

    def _play_v2(self, controller, account_name: str, sku: str):
        """▶ PLAY v2 - Usa BotController"""
        print(f"\n[UI] ▶ PLAY_v2 iniciado: {account_name} SKU={sku}")
        
        if not sku:
            print(f"[UI] ❌ PLAY_v2: SKU not configured")
            return
        
        def do_play():
            try:
                # Controller maneja todo: validación de estado, Chrome, monitoreo
                controller.handle_btn_play_click(sku)
                print(f"[UI] ✅ Play completado para {account_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[UI] ❌ ERROR en play_v2 ({account_name}): {e}")
                import traceback
                traceback.print_exc()
            finally:
                # Actualizar UI después de completar
                self.root.after(0, lambda: self._refresh_single_row(account_name))
        
        # Lanzar en daemon thread
        thread = threading.Thread(target=do_play, daemon=True, name=f"play-v2-{account_name}")
        thread.start()

    def _refresh_single_row(self, account_name: str):
        """Actualiza una sola fila con el estado actual del controller"""
        if account_name not in self.rows:
            return
        
        row_data = self.rows[account_name]
        controller = row_data.get("controller")
        
        if not controller:
            return
        
        try:
            # Actualizar estado
            status_text = controller.get_status_text()
            row_data["state"].configure(text=f"[{status_text}]")
            
            # Actualizar indicador de sesión
            session_color = "#00FF00" if controller.state == BotAccountState.READY else "#FF6B6B"
            session_indicator = f"✅ {status_text}" if controller.state == BotAccountState.READY else f"⏳ {status_text}"
            row_data["session"].configure(text=session_indicator, text_color=session_color)
            
            # Actualizar estado de botones
            btn_login = row_data.get("btn_login")
            btn_play = row_data.get("btn_play")
            
            if btn_login:
                btn_login.configure(state="normal" if controller.is_login_enabled() else "disabled")
            
            if btn_play:
                btn_play.configure(state="normal" if controller.is_play_enabled() else "disabled")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[UI] ERROR al refrescar fila {account_name}: {e}")

    # ===== ACCIONES =====

    def _login(self, account_name: str, account_path: str):
        """
        🔑 BOTÓN [LOGIN] - Fire-and-forget, no bloqueante
        
        Lanza Chrome en thread separado. UI sigue respondiendo.
        No hay condiciones que bloqueen (ej: if state == READY: return).
        """
        print(f"\n[UI] 🔐 LOGIN iniciado: {account_name}")
        
        def do_login():
            try:
                # Importar DIRECTAMENTE de engines (no de session_check)
                from engines.manual_login import open_chrome_for_login
                
                # Construir ruta al perfil
                profile_path = os.path.join(account_path, "profile_pw")
                
                # Lanzar Chrome (retorna inmediatamente, no espera)
                success = open_chrome_for_login(account_name, profile_path)
                
                if success:
                    print(f"[UI] ✅ {account_name}: Chrome abierto correctamente")
                else:
                    print(f"[UI] ❌ {account_name}: Error al abrir Chrome")
            
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[UI] ❌ ERROR LOGIN ({account_name}): {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
                import traceback
                traceback.print_exc()
        
        # Lanzar en thread daemon (no bloqueante)
        thread = threading.Thread(target=do_login, daemon=True, name=f"login-{account_name}")
        thread.start()
        
        print(f"[UI] 👍 Thread de login iniciado para {account_name}")

    def _check_all_sessions(self):
        """Verifica todas las cuentas"""
        print("\n[UI] Checking all sessions...")
        
        def check_sessions():
            from auth.session_check import check_session_nike
            
            for name, acc in self.manager.accounts.items():
                is_logged = check_session_nike(acc.account_path)
                acc.is_logged_in = is_logged
                status = "✅" if is_logged else "❌"
                print(f"[UI] {name}: {status}")
            
            self.root.after(0, self._build_accounts)
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=check_sessions, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def _check_single_session(self, account_name: str):
        """Inspecciona checkout - Abre Chrome en /checkout/#/payment"""
        print(f"\n[UI] Opening checkout for inspection {account_name}...")
        
        def do_check():
            try:
                acc = self.manager.accounts.get(account_name)
                if not acc:
                    return
                from auth.session_check import open_chrome_for_checkout
                open_chrome_for_checkout(acc.account_path, account_name)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[UI] ERROR CHECK: {e}")
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=do_check, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def _play(self, account_name: str):
        """PLAY - Ejecuta el flujo completo del bot"""
        acc = self.manager.accounts.get(account_name)
        if not acc:
            print(f"[UI] ERROR: Account {account_name} not found")
            return
        
        # Validación 1: ¿está logueado?
        if not acc.is_logged_in:
            print(f"[UI] ERROR {account_name}: Must login first (button LOGIN)")
            return
        
        # Validación 2: ¿hay SKU definido?
        if not acc.sku:
            print(f"[UI] ERROR {account_name}: SKU not configured (button EDIT)")
            return
        
        print(f"\n[UI] PLAY started {account_name}")
        
        def execute_drop():
            from runtime.worker import account_worker
            try:
                account_worker(acc, live=True)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[UI] ERROR in drop: {e}")
                acc.state = AccountState.ERROR
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=execute_drop, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def _stop(self, account_name: str):
        """STOP - Detiene la ejecución"""
        ok, msg = self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.STOP, "account": account_name})
        if ok:
            print(f"[UI] OK: {msg}")
        else:
            print(f"[UI] ERROR: {msg}")

    def _edit(self, account):
        """EDIT - Abre el editor de cuenta"""
        AccountEditor(self.root, account, manager=self.manager)

    def _apply_concurrency(self):
        """Aplica límite de concurrencia"""
        raw = (self.var_concurrent.get() or "").strip()
        if not raw.isdigit():
            return
        value = int(raw)
        if value <= 0:
            return
        self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.SET_CONCURRENCY, "value": value})

    def _run_all(self):
        """Run all READY accounts"""
        self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.RUN_ALL_READY})

    def _stop_all(self):
        """Stop all running accounts"""
        self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.STOP_ALL})

    def _refresh_loop(self):
        """Refresca la UI periódicamente"""
        try:
            # Actualizar cada fila usando _refresh_single_row
            for name in self.rows.keys():
                self._refresh_single_row(name)
            
            self._update_logs_display()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[UI] Error in refresh_loop: {e}")

        self.root.after(1000, self._refresh_loop)

    def _update_logs_display(self):
        """Actualiza el textbox de logs"""
        if not hasattr(self, 'log_textbox'):
            return
        
        try:
            if self.log_handler.logs:
                self.log_textbox.configure(state="normal")
                log_lines = list(self.log_handler.logs)[-50:]
                self.log_textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
                self.log_textbox.insert("end", "\n".join(log_lines))
                self.log_textbox.see("end")
                self.log_textbox.configure(state="disabled")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[UI] Error updating logs: {e}")

    def _clear_logs(self):
        """Limpia los logs"""
        self.log_handler.logs.clear()
        if hasattr(self, 'log_textbox'):
            self.log_textbox.configure(state="normal")
            self.log_textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
            self.log_textbox.configure(state="disabled")

    def run(self):
        """Inicia la UI"""
        self.root.mainloop()

FILE: ui\app_integration_example.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
ui/app_integration_example.py - Ejemplo de integracion con CustomTkinter

Muestra como conectar:
1. Boton PLAY
2. Listener de estados
3. Multiprocessing runner

SIN necesidad de reescribir app.py completo.
"""
import threading
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

from runtime.state_bus import make_state_bus
from ui.play_launcher import launch_play_for_accounts
from ui.state_listener import start_state_listener


# ============================================================================
# EJEMPLO: Integracion en app.py (CustomTkinter)
# ============================================================================

class PlayIntegration:
    """Wrapper para integracion PLAY en UI"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        # Crear bus de estados
        self.bus = make_state_bus()
        
        # Iniciar listener (escucha estados en background)
        start_state_listener(self.bus, self.on_state_update)
    
    def on_state_update(self, user: str, state: str, msg: str = ""):
        """
        Callback cuando hay estado nuevo
        
        Aqui actualizas la tabla/fila de la cuenta en la UI:
        - STARTING -> colorear en amarillo
        - CLONING -> spinner
        - PREFLIGHT -> verificando
        - BACKEND -> procesando
        - HANDOVER -> esperando
        - WAITING_HUMAN -> resaltado
        - DONE -> verde
        - ERROR -> rojo
        """
        print(f"[STATE_UPDATE] {user}: {state} ({msg})")
        
        # TODO: actualizar tabla
        # self.table.update_row(user, state, msg)
    
    def on_play_clicked(self, selected_accounts: list):
        """
        Boton PLAY presionado
        
        NO BLOQUEA la UI (corre en thread aparte)
        """
        if not selected_accounts:
            print("[PLAY] Sin cuentas seleccionadas")
            return
        
        print(f"[PLAY] Lanzando {len(selected_accounts)} cuentas")
        
        # Lanzar en thread para no bloquear UI
        t = threading.Thread(
            target=launch_play_for_accounts,
            args=(selected_accounts, self.bus),
            daemon=True
        )
        t.start()


# ============================================================================
# INTEGRACION EN app.py (pseudo-codigo)
# ============================================================================

INTEGRATION_CODE_EXAMPLE = """
# En ui/app.py:

from ui.app_integration_example import PlayIntegration

class AppUI:
    def __init__(self):
        ...
        # Crear integracion
        self.play = PlayIntegration()
        ...
    
    def setup_buttons(self):
        ...
        # Boton PLAY
        play_button = CTkButton(
            master=self.button_frame,
            text="PLAY",
            command=self.on_play_button_clicked
        )
        play_button.pack()
    
    def on_play_button_clicked(self):
        # Obtener cuentas seleccionadas de la tabla
        selected = self.table.get_selected_accounts()
        
        # Lanzar con integracion
        self.play.on_play_clicked(selected)
        
        # UI no se congela, listener actualiza tabla en background
"""

# ui/app.py
import customtkinter as ctk
import threading
import logging
from collections import deque

from manager.account_manager import AccountManager
from controller.controller import Controller
from core.events import EventType
from core.states import AccountState
from ui.account_editor import AccountEditor
from auth.session_check import login_from_ui


class UILogHandler(logging.Handler):
    """Handler custom que envía logs a la UI"""
    
    def __init__(self, max_logs: int = 200):
        super().__init__()
        self.logs = deque(maxlen=max_logs)
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
    
    def emit(self, record):
        try:
            msg = self.format(record)
            with self.lock:
                self.logs.append(msg)
            # Print también en consola (para debugging)
            print(msg)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error en UILogHandler: {e}")
            self.handleError(record)


class AppUI:
    def __init__(self, manager: AccountManager, controller: Controller):
        self.manager = manager
        self.controller = controller
        self.rows = {}
        self.log_handler = UILogHandler()
        
        ctk.set_appearance_mode("System")
        ctk.set_default_color_theme("dark-blue")

        self.root = ctk.CTk()
        self.root.title("Nike Bot - Control")
        self.root.geometry("1200x700")

        self._setup_logging()
        self._build_ui()
        # start the periodic UI refresher
        self._refresh_loop()

    def _setup_logging(self):
        """Configura el handler de logs personalizado para TODOS los loggers nuevos"""
        # Configurar el root logger con el handler
        logging.root.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
        
        # Agregar el handler al root - así se propaga a todos los loggers
        if not any(isinstance(h, UILogHandler) for h in logging.root.handlers):
            logging.root.addHandler(self.log_handler)
        
        # Formateador simple para UI
        formatter = logging.Formatter("[%(name)s] %(message)s")
        self.log_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        self.log_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
        
        # Usar logging.setLoggerClass para personalizar creación de loggers
        original_logger_class = logging.getLoggerClass()
        
        class UILogger(logging.Logger):
            """Logger que se auto-registra con el handler de UI"""
            def __init__(self, name, level=logging.NOTSET):
                super().__init__(name, level)
                if not any(isinstance(h, UILogHandler) for h in self.handlers):
                    self.addHandler(self.log_handler)
        
        # NO cambiar la clase - mejor es asegurar propagación
        logging.root.propagate = True
        for handler in logging.root.handlers:
            if not isinstance(handler, logging.NullHandler):
                handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

    def _build_ui(self):
        # Top bar
        top = ctk.CTkFrame(self.root)
        top.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=6)

        ctk.CTkLabel(top, text="Max concurrent:").pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # StringVar por regla UI; casteo numérico al aplicar
        self.var_concurrent = ctk.StringVar(value=str(self.manager.max_concurrent))

        ctk.CTkEntry(
            top,
            width=60,
            textvariable=self.var_concurrent
        ).pack(side="left")

        ctk.CTkButton(
            top,
            text="Aplicar",
            command=self._apply_concurrency
        ).pack(side="left", padx=6)

        ctk.CTkButton(
            top,
            text="🔍 Check Sessions",
            fg_color="orange",
            command=self._check_all_sessions
        ).pack(side="left", padx=6)

        ctk.CTkButton(
            top,
            text="▶ Run all READY",
            command=self._run_all
        ).pack(side="left", padx=10)

        ctk.CTkButton(
            top,
            text="🟥 STOP ALL",
            fg_color="red",
            command=self._stop_all
        ).pack(side="right", padx=6)

        # Main container con dos paneles: cuentas + logs
        main_container = ctk.CTkFrame(self.root)
        main_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=6)

        # Panel izquierdo: Cuentas
        left_panel = ctk.CTkFrame(main_container)
        left_panel.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True, padx=(0, 5))

        ctk.CTkLabel(left_panel, text="📋 Cuentas", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=4)

        self.container = ctk.CTkFrame(left_panel)
        self.container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        self._build_accounts()

        # Panel derecho: Logs en tiempo real
        right_panel = ctk.CTkFrame(main_container, width=300)
        right_panel.pack(side="right", fill="both", expand=True, padx=(5, 0))
        right_panel.pack_propagate(False)

        ctk.CTkLabel(right_panel, text="📝 Logs en vivo", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=4)

        # Textbox para logs
        self.log_textbox = ctk.CTkTextbox(
            right_panel,
            height=300,
            state="disabled",
            wrap="word",
            font=("Courier", 9)
        )
        self.log_textbox.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        # Botón para limpiar logs
        ctk.CTkButton(
            right_panel,
            text="🗑️ Limpiar logs",
            width=100,
            command=self._clear_logs
        ).pack(pady=4)


    def _build_accounts(self):
        # clear container
        for w in self.container.winfo_children():
            w.destroy()
        self.rows = {}

        import os
        from auth.session_check import check_session_nike
        
        for name, acc in self.manager.accounts.items():
            # CHEQUEO INTELIGENTE: ¿tiene cookies reales en profile_pw?
            is_logged = check_session_nike(acc.account_path)
            acc.is_logged_in = is_logged
            
            # Debug: mostrar en consola
            status = "✅ LOGUEADO" if is_logged else "❌ NO LOGUEADO"
            print(f"[UI] {name}: {status}")
            
            self._add_account_row(name, acc, is_logged)

    def _add_account_row(self, name, acc, assume_logged=True):
        """Construye una fila de cuenta con arquitectura definitiva:
        NO_AUTH → READY → RUNNING → PRICE_LOCKED → WAITING_PAYMENT → DONE / ERROR
        
        Estados visibles:
        🔴 NO_AUTH          - Sin sesión
        🔵 READY            - Logueado, listo para PLAY
        🟡 RUNNING          - Backend en ejecución
        🟠 PRICE_LOCKED     - Precio capturado, esperando browser
        🟣 WAITING_PAYMENT  - Chrome abierto, esperando pago
        🟢 DONE             - Orden completada
        ❌ ERROR            - Error en proceso
        """
        is_logged = getattr(acc, 'is_logged_in', assume_logged)
        is_running = acc.state and acc.state.name in ("RUNNING", "PRICE_LOCKED", "WAITING_HUMAN_READY")
        
        row = ctk.CTkFrame(self.container)
        row.pack(fill="x", padx=6, pady=4)

        # INDICADOR SESIÓN + ESTADO (prominente)
        session_indicator = "✅ LOGUEADO" if is_logged else "❌ NO LOGUEADO"
        session_color = "#00FF00" if is_logged else "#FF6B6B"
        
        lbl_session = ctk.CTkLabel(
            row,
            text=session_indicator,
            width=140,
            anchor="w",
            text_color=session_color,
            font=("Arial", 10, "bold")
        )
        lbl_session.pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # Nombre y estado actual (VISIBLE Y CLARO)
        state_map = {
            "NO_AUTH": "🔴 NO_AUTH",
            "IDLE": "🔵 READY",
            "RUNNING": "🟡 RUNNING",
            "PRICE_LOCKED": "🟠 PRICE_LOCKED",
            "WAITING_HUMAN_READY": "🟣 WAITING_PAYMENT",
            "DONE": "🟢 DONE",
            "ERROR": "❌ ERROR"
        }
        
        state_name = acc.state.name if acc.state else "IDLE"
        state_display = state_map.get(state_name, f"? {state_name}")
        
        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=name, width=100, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
        lbl_state = ctk.CTkLabel(
            row,
            text=state_display,
            width=150,
            font=("Arial", 9, "bold")
        )
        lbl_state.pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # Status/Diagnóstico (last_status del account)
        lbl_diag = ctk.CTkLabel(
            row,
            text=acc.last_status or "—",
            width=160,
            anchor="w",
            font=("Arial", 9)
        )
        lbl_diag.pack(side="left", padx=6)

        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=f"SKU: {acc.sku or '—'}").pack(side="left", padx=6)

        # ===== BOTONES (LÓGICA CLARA Y DISCIPLINADA) =====
        
        # LOGIN SIEMPRE DISPONIBLE (para re-loguear si lo necesita)
        if not is_running:
            btn_login = ctk.CTkButton(
                row,
                text="🔐 LOGIN",
                width=90,
                fg_color="orange",
                command=lambda a=name, ap=acc.account_path: self._login(a, ap)
            )
            btn_login.pack(side="right", padx=3)
        
        # CHECK solo si no está logueado y no corre
        if not is_running and not is_logged:
            btn_check = ctk.CTkButton(
                row,
                text="🔍 CHECK",
                width=80,
                fg_color="orange",
                command=lambda a=name: self._check_single_session(a)
            )
            btn_check.pack(side="right", padx=3)
        
        # PLAY SOLO SI ESTÁ READY (disciplina: no probar sin sesión)
        if not is_running and is_logged:
            btn_play = ctk.CTkButton(
                row,
                text="▶ PLAY",
                width=90,
                fg_color="green",
                command=lambda a=name: self._play(a)
            )
            btn_play.pack(side="right", padx=3)
        
        # STOP SI ESTÁ CORRIENDO
        if is_running:
            btn_stop = ctk.CTkButton(
                row,
                text="⏹ STOP",
                width=90,
                fg_color="red",
                command=lambda a=name: self._stop(a)
            )
            btn_stop.pack(side="right", padx=3)

        # Botón EDIT (deshabilitado si corre - disciplina)
        btn_edit = ctk.CTkButton(
            row,
            text="✏ Edit",
            width=70,
            state="disabled" if is_running else "normal",
            command=lambda a=acc: self._edit(a)
        )
        btn_edit.pack(side="right", padx=3)

        self.rows[name] = {
            "state": lbl_state,
            "session": lbl_session,
            "diag": lbl_diag
        }

    def _rebuild_single_account(self, account_name, account):
        """Reconstruye una sola fila de cuenta (después de verificar sesión)"""
        # Encontrar y remover la fila anterior
        for widget in self.container.winfo_children():
            # Es una línea heurística, podrías mejorarla guardando referencias
            pass
        
        # Por ahora, simplemente reconstruir todo
        self._build_accounts()

    # Actions
    def _login(self, account_name: str, account_path: str):
        """
        � BOTÓN [LOGIN] — CORRECCIÓN THREADING
        
        ⚠️ CRÍTICO: NO ejecutar Playwright desde main thread de UI
        
        Abre navegador con perfil persistente.
        Usuario loguea manualmente.
        Cookies se guardan automáticamente.
        
        Se ejecuta DÍAS/HORAS antes del drop.
        """
        # 🔒 PROTECCIÓN: No permitir doble-click / clicks simultáneos
        if self.login_running.get(account_name, False):
            print(f"[UI] ⚠️ {account_name}: LOGIN ya en ejecución")
            return
        
        print(f"\n[UI] 🔐 INICIANDO LOGIN MANUAL para {account_name}")
        print(f"[UI] ▶ Se abrirá Chrome en 2 segundos...")
        
        # MARCAR como en ejecución
        self.login_running[account_name] = True
        
        def do_login():
            """Ejecutar en thread separado, NO en main thread de UI"""
            try:
                print(f"[LOGIN-THREAD] ⏳ Iniciando setup_account_login_safe()...")
                from auth.session_check import setup_account_login_safe
                
                success = setup_account_login_safe(account_path, account_name)
                
                if success:
                    print(f"[LOGIN-THREAD] ✅ Setup completado - Cookies guardadas")
                    print(f"[UI] ✅ Login completado - Validando sesión...")
                    # Auto-check después del login
                    self.root.after(1000, lambda: self._check_single_session(account_name))
                else:
                    print(f"[LOGIN-THREAD] ❌ Setup falló o fue cancelado")
                    print(f"[UI] ❌ Login cancelado o con error")
            
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[LOGIN-THREAD] ❌ EXCEPCIÓN EN LOGIN: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
                import traceback
                traceback.print_exc()
                print(f"[UI] ❌ ERROR LOGIN: {e}")
            
            finally:
                # DESMARCAR (incluso si falló)
                self.login_running[account_name] = False
                print(f"[LOGIN-THREAD] 🏁 Finalizando LOGIN para {account_name}")
        
        # 🧵 CRÍTICO: Lanzar en thread para NO bloquear UI
        thread = threading.Thread(target=do_login, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        print(f"[UI] ✔️ Thread de LOGIN lanzado - Observa la consola")

    def _check_all_sessions(self):
        """Verifica todas las cuentas"""
        print("\n[UI] 🔍 Verificando sesiones...")
        
        def check_sessions():
            from auth.session_check import check_session_nike
            
            for name, acc in self.manager.accounts.items():
                is_logged = check_session_nike(acc.account_path)
                acc.is_logged_in = is_logged
                status = "✅" if is_logged else "❌"
                print(f"[UI] {name}: {status}")
            
            # Recargar UI
            self.root.after(0, self._build_accounts)
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=check_sessions, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def _check_single_session(self, account_name: str):
        """Verifica sesión de UNA cuenta (CHECK_SESSION button)"""
        print(f"\n[UI] 🔍 Verificando sesión de {account_name}...")
        
        def check_session():
            from auth.session_check import check_session_nike
            from auth.profile_cookies import get_cookies_for_account
            
            acc = self.manager.accounts.get(account_name)
            if not acc:
                return
            
            # Paso 1: Verificar que hay cookies en profile_pw
            cookies = get_cookies_for_account(acc.account_path)
            if not cookies or not all(cookies.values()):
                print(f"[UI] ❌ {account_name}: Sin cookies en profile_pw")
                acc.is_logged_in = False
                self.root.after(0, self._build_accounts)
                return
            
            # Paso 2: Validar loggedIn en VTEX
            try:
                from vtex.tls_client_vtex import TlsVTEXClient
                from config.settings import BASE_URL
                
                client = TlsVTEXClient(
                    base_url=BASE_URL,
                    cookies=cookies,
                    user_agent="Mozilla/5.0",
                    order_form_id=None
                )
                
                of = client.get_order_form()
                
                if of.get("loggedIn"):
                    acc.is_logged_in = True
                    print(f"[UI] ✅ {account_name}: LOGUEADO (READY)")
                else:
                    acc.is_logged_in = False
                    print(f"[UI] ❌ {account_name}: Sesión expirada (cookies inválidas)")
            
            except Exception as e:
                acc.is_logged_in = False
                print(f"[UI] ❌ {account_name}: Error validando - {e}")
            
            # Recargar UI
            self.root.after(0, self._build_accounts)
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=check_session, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def _play(self, account_name: str):
        """
        ▶ BOTÓN [PLAY] — EJECUTA EL DROP
        
        ARQUITECTURA:
        - Valida: ¿está logueado? ¿hay SKU?
        - Ejecuta: Fase A (Backend) + Fase B (Browser Handover)
        - Solo funciona si estado == READY
        """
        acc = self.manager.accounts.get(account_name)
        if not acc:
            print(f"[UI] ❌ Cuenta {account_name} no encontrada")
            return
        
        # 🔒 Validación 0: ¿está el LOGIN aún en progreso?
        if self.login_running.get(account_name, False):
            print(f"[UI] 🛑 {account_name}: LOGIN aún en progreso")
            print(f"[UI] ▶ Cierra Chrome primero → luego presiona PLAY")
            return
        
        # Validación 1: ¿está logueado?
        if not acc.is_logged_in:
            print(f"[UI] ⚠️ {account_name}: Debes iniciar sesión primero (botón LOGIN)")
            return
        
        # Validación 2: ¿hay SKU definido?
        if not acc.sku:
            print(f"[UI] ⚠️ {account_name}: SKU no configurado (botón EDIT)")
            return
        
        print(f"\n[UI] ▶ PLAY INICIADO en {account_name}")
        
        def execute_drop():
            from runtime.worker import account_worker
            
            try:
                account_worker(acc, live=True)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[UI] ❌ Error en drop: {e}")
                acc.state = AccountState.ERROR
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=execute_drop, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def _stop(self, account_name: str):
        ok, msg = self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.STOP, "account": account_name})
        if ok:
            print(f"[UI] ✅ {msg}")
        else:
            print(f"[UI] ❌ {msg}")

    def _edit(self, account):
        AccountEditor(self.root, account)

    def _apply_concurrency(self):
        # Validación explícita: entero positivo
        raw = (self.var_concurrent.get() or "").strip()
        if not raw.isdigit():
            # no actualizar si inválido
            return
        value = int(raw)
        if value <= 0:
            return
        self.controller.handle({
            "type": EventType.SET_CONCURRENCY,
            "value": value
        })

    def _run_all(self):
        self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.RUN_ALL_READY})

    def _stop_all(self):
        self.controller.handle({"type": EventType.STOP_ALL})

    def _refresh_loop(self):
        try:
            for name, acc in self.manager.accounts.items():
                if name in self.rows:
                    state_text = acc.state.name if acc.state else "UNKNOWN"
                    self.rows[name]["state"].configure(text=state_text)

                    # Map internal status codes to short human-friendly phrases
                    status_map = {
                        "WAITING_PRICE": "Esperando precio",
                        "PRICE_OK": "Precio publicado",
                        "PAYMENT_MP_OK": "MP disponible",
                        "PAYMENT_MP_NOT_AVAILABLE": "MP no disponible",
                        "PAYMENT_OK": "Pago disponible",
                        "FALLBACK_TO_MANUAL": "Fallback → Manual",
                        "CHECKOUT_SENT": "Checkout enviado",
                        "MANUAL_READY": "Manual listo",
                        "WAITING_PRICE_TIMEOUT": "Timeout esperando precio",
                        "CHECKOUT_FAILED": "Checkout fallido",
                        "WAITING_STABILITY": "Esperando estabilidad",
                        "CART_STABLE": "Carrito estable",
                        "PAYMENT_READY": "Pago listo",
                    }
                    raw = acc.last_status or None
                    diag = status_map.get(raw, (raw if raw else "—"))
                    self.rows[name]["diag"].configure(text=diag)

            # Actualizar logs en la UI
            self._update_logs_display()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[UI] Error en _refresh_loop: {e}")

        self.root.after(1000, self._refresh_loop)

    def _update_logs_display(self):
        """Actualiza el textbox de logs con nuevos mensajes"""
        if not hasattr(self, 'log_textbox'):
            return
        
        # Obtener logs nuevos (desde el handler)
        try:
            if self.log_handler.logs:
                self.log_textbox.configure(state="normal")
                
                # Mostrar solo los últimos 50 logs
                log_lines = list(self.log_handler.logs)[-50:]
                self.log_textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
                self.log_textbox.insert("end", "\n".join(log_lines))
                self.log_textbox.see("end")  # Auto-scroll
                
                self.log_textbox.configure(state="disabled")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[UI] Error actualizando logs: {e}")

    def _clear_logs(self):
        """Limpia el textbox de logs"""
        self.log_handler.logs.clear()
        if hasattr(self, 'log_textbox'):
            self.log_textbox.configure(state="normal")
            self.log_textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
            self.log_textbox.configure(state="disabled")


    def run(self):
        self.root.mainloop()

FILE: ui\app_v0_9_simple.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
ui/app.py - UI SIMPLIFICADA V0.9 (CONGELADA)

RESPONSABILIDAD ÚNICA:
1. Botón LOGIN → subprocess login_runner.py <cuenta>
2. Botón PLAY → launch_play_for_accounts([...], sku)
3. Tabla de estados (opcional)

PROHIBIDO:
- Manager de cuentas
- Supervisor
- Watchers
- Multiple modos
- Session checks
"""
import customtkinter as ctk
import threading
import subprocess
import sys
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

from ui.play_launcher import launch_play_for_accounts
from runtime.state_bus import make_state_bus
from ui.state_listener import start_state_listener
from profile_manager_v2 import verify_login_ok


# ============================================================================
# HELPERS DE PATHS
# ============================================================================

def profile_login_path(account_name: str) -> str:
    """Ruta a profile_login (MASTER)"""
    return str(Path("auth") / account_name / "profile_login")


def profile_run_path(account_name: str) -> str:
    """Ruta a profile_run (SLAVE)"""
    return str(Path("auth") / account_name / "profile_run")


def get_login_status(account_name: str) -> tuple:
    """Retorna (emoji, status_text, color)"""
    if verify_login_ok(account_name):
        return ("✅", "Logueado", "green")
    else:
        return ("❌", "No logueado", "red")


# ============================================================================
# APP UI
# ============================================================================

class AppUI:
    """UI Simplificada V0.9 - Solo LOGIN y PLAY"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.root = ctk.CTk()
        self.root.title("Nike Bot V0.9 (CONGELADO)")
        self.root.geometry("900x600")
        
        # Estado
        self.accounts: List[str] = []
        self.selected_account = None
        self.sku_input = None
        self.state_bus = make_state_bus()
        self.listener_thread = None
        self.account_status_frames = {}  # Para actualizar estados
        
        # Construir UI
        self._build_ui()
        
        # Iniciar listener de estados
        self._start_state_listener()
    
    def _build_ui(self):
        """Construir interfaz simplificada"""
        
        # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        # FRAME 1: Cuentas
        # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        frame_accounts = ctk.CTkFrame(self.root)
        frame_accounts.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=10)
        
        label_accounts = ctk.CTkLabel(
            frame_accounts,
            text="CUENTAS",
            font=("Arial", 16, "bold")
        )
        label_accounts.pack(pady=10)
        
        # Lista de cuentas (hardcoded por ahora)
        self.accounts = self._load_accounts()
        
        # Contenedor scrollable para cuentas
        scrollable_frame = ctk.CTkScrollableFrame(frame_accounts, width=300, height=250)
        scrollable_frame.pack(pady=10, fill="both", expand=True)
        
        self.account_buttons = {}
        for acc in self.accounts:
            # Frame para cada cuenta (con estado)
            acc_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(scrollable_frame)
            acc_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=5, pady=5)
            
            # Botón de cuenta
            btn = ctk.CTkButton(
                acc_frame,
                text=f"► {acc}",
                command=lambda a=acc: self._on_account_select(a),
                fg_color="gray50",
                text_color="white",
                width=250
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            
            # Status de login
            emoji, status, color = get_login_status(acc)
            status_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
                acc_frame,
                text=f"{emoji}",
                font=("Arial", 12, "bold"),
                text_color=color
            )
            status_label.pack(side="right", padx=5)
            
            # Guardar referencia
            self.account_buttons[acc] = btn
            self.account_status_frames[acc] = (status_label, color)
        
        # Botón LOGIN (seleccionada)
        self.btn_login = ctk.CTkButton(
            frame_accounts,
            text="[1] LOGIN (manual)",
            command=self._on_login_click,
            fg_color="orange",
            text_color="black"
        )
        self.btn_login.pack(pady=5, fill="x")
        
        # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        # FRAME 2: DROP CONFIG
        # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        frame_drop = ctk.CTkFrame(self.root)
        frame_drop.pack(side="right", fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=10)
        
        label_drop = ctk.CTkLabel(
            frame_drop,
            text="DROP CONFIG",
            font=("Arial", 16, "bold")
        )
        label_drop.pack(pady=10)
        
        # SKU input
        label_sku = ctk.CTkLabel(frame_drop, text="SKU:")
        label_sku.pack(pady=5)
        
        self.sku_input = ctk.CTkEntry(frame_drop, placeholder_text="ej: 123456789")
        self.sku_input.pack(pady=5, fill="x", padx=10)
        
        # Botón PLAY
        self.btn_play = ctk.CTkButton(
            frame_drop,
            text="[2] PLAY (backend + handover)",
            command=self._on_play_click,
            fg_color="green",
            text_color="black"
        )
        self.btn_play.pack(pady=20, fill="x", padx=10)
        
        # Estados (log)
        label_log = ctk.CTkLabel(frame_drop, text="ESTADOS EN TIEMPO REAL:")
        label_log.pack(pady=10)
        
        self.log_box = ctk.CTkTextbox(frame_drop, width=400, height=150)
        self.log_box.pack(pady=10, fill="both", expand=True, padx=10)
    
    def _load_accounts(self) -> List[str]:
        """Cargar cuentas desde auth/"""
        auth_path = Path("auth")
        if not auth_path.exists():
            return ["cuenta1", "cuenta2", "cuenta3"]
        
        accounts = [d.name for d in auth_path.iterdir() if d.is_dir()]
        return sorted(accounts)
    
    def _on_account_select(self, account: str):
        """Seleccionar una cuenta"""
        self.selected_account = account
        self._log(f"[UI] Seleccionada: {account}")
        
        # Cambiar color del botón
        for acc, btn in self.account_buttons.items():
            if acc == account:
                btn.configure(fg_color="blue")
            else:
                btn.configure(fg_color="gray50")
    
    def _on_login_click(self):
        """Botón LOGIN: abre Chrome para login manual"""
        if not self.selected_account:
            self._log("[ERROR] Selecciona una cuenta primero")
            return
        
        account = self.selected_account
        self._log(f"[LOGIN] Abriendo Chrome para {account}...")
        
        # Ejecutar login_runner.py en subprocess
        try:
            subprocess.Popen([
                sys.executable,
                "login_runner.py",
                account
            ])
        except Exception as e:
            self._log(f"[ERROR] No se pudo abrir login_runner.py: {e}")
    
    def _on_play_click(self):
        """Botón PLAY: ejecutar flujo ATC + handover"""
        # Validar SKU
        sku = self.sku_input.get().strip()
        if not sku:
            self._log("[ERROR] Especifica un SKU")
            return
        
        # Obtener cuentas seleccionadas (por ahora, todas)
        selected_accounts = self.accounts
        
        if not selected_accounts:
            self._log("[ERROR] No hay cuentas")
            return
        
        self._log(f"[PLAY] Validando cuentas...")
        
        # Ejecutar en thread (no bloquear UI)
        def play_thread():
            try:
                launch_play_for_accounts(
                    accounts=selected_accounts,
                    sku=sku,
                    bus=self.state_bus
                )
            except Exception as e:
                self._log(f"[ERROR] {e}")
        
        t = threading.Thread(target=play_thread, daemon=True)
        t.start()
    
    def _start_state_listener(self):
        """Iniciar listener de estados en background"""
        def on_state_update(user: str, state: str, msg: str):
            self._log(f"[{user}] {state} {msg}")
            # Actualizar estado de la cuenta
            if user in self.account_status_frames:
                if state == "READY":
                    label, _ = self.account_status_frames[user]
                    label.configure(text="✅", text_color="green")
                elif "ERROR" in state or "BLOCKED" in state:
                    label, _ = self.account_status_frames[user]
                    label.configure(text="❌", text_color="red")
        
        self.listener_thread = start_state_listener(
            bus=self.state_bus,
            on_update=on_state_update
        )
    
    def _log(self, msg: str):
        """Agregar mensaje al log"""
        self.log_box.insert("end", f"{msg}\n")
        self.log_box.see("end")
    
    def run(self):
        """Iniciar UI"""
        self.root.mainloop()


def main():
    app = AppUI()
    app.run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


======================================================================
FILE: ui\play_launcher.py
======================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
ui/play_launcher.py - Lanzador de PLAY desde UI

Responsabilidad: puente limpio entre UI y multiprocessing
"""
import threading
import sys
import os
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

from runtime.multiprocessing_runner import run_accounts_mp
from profile_manager_v2 import verify_login_ok


def launch_play_for_accounts(accounts: list, sku: str, bus=None):
    """
    Lanzador de PLAY (llamado desde UI en thread)
    
    Args:
        accounts: ["cuenta1", "cuenta2", ...]
        sku: SKU a comprar (ej: "123456789")
        bus: Queue de estados (opcional, pasado por UI)
    """
    if not accounts:
        print("[LAUNCHER] Sin cuentas seleccionadas")
        return
    
    if not sku:
        print("[LAUNCHER] Sin SKU especificado")
        return
    
    # VALIDACIÓN CRÍTICA: Verificar que todas las cuentas estén logueadas
    not_logged_in = []
    for account in accounts:
        if not verify_login_ok(account):
            not_logged_in.append(account)
    
    if not_logged_in:
        print("[LAUNCHER] ❌ BLOQUEADO - Las siguientes cuentas NO están logueadas:")
        for acc in not_logged_in:
            print(f"  - {acc} (falta .login_ok)")
        print("[LAUNCHER] Ejecuta primero: python login_runner.py <cuenta>")
        return
    
    print(f"[LAUNCHER] ✅ Validación OK - Todas las cuentas están logueadas")
    print(f"[LAUNCHER] Lanzando {len(accounts)} cuentas en multiprocessing")
    print(f"[LAUNCHER] Cuentas: {', '.join(accounts)}")
    print(f"[LAUNCHER] SKU: {sku}")
    
    # Ejecutar multiprocessing
    run_accounts_mp(accounts, sku, bus)
    
    print("[LAUNCHER] Todos los procesos completados")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
ui/state_listener.py - Listener de estados (no bloqueante)

Escucha bus y ejecuta callback en main thread.
"""
import threading
import time


def start_state_listener(bus, on_update, poll_interval=0.1):
    """
    Inicia listener de estados en background thread
    
    Args:
        bus: Queue de estados (multiprocessing.Manager().Queue())
        on_update: Callback(user, state_str, msg)
        poll_interval: Intervalo de polling (segundos)
    
    Retorna:
        Thread (ya iniciado en daemon)
    """
    def loop():
        while True:
            try:
                msg = bus.get(timeout=0.5)
                user = msg.get("user", "?")
                state = msg.get("state", "?")
                extra = msg.get("msg", "")
                
                # Callback en main thread
                on_update(user, state, extra)
                
            except:
                time.sleep(poll_interval)
    
    t = threading.Thread(target=loop, daemon=True)
    t.start()
    return t

# utils/logger.py
import logging
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime

class HumanFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    """Formateador con emojis para legibilidad humana"""
    
    COLORS = {
        'DEBUG': '\033[36m',      # Cyan
        'INFO': '\033[32m',       # Green
        'WARNING': '\033[33m',    # Yellow
        'ERROR': '\033[31m',      # Red
        'CRITICAL': '\033[35m',   # Magenta
        'RESET': '\033[0m'        # Reset
    }
    
    EMOJIS = {
        'DEBUG': '🔍',
        'INFO': '✅',
        'WARNING': '⚠️',
        'ERROR': '❌',
        'CRITICAL': '🔥',
    }
    
    def format(self, record):
        # Nivel con emoji
        emoji = self.EMOJIS.get(record.levelname, '•')
        level = record.levelname
        
        # Timestamp humanizado
        timestamp = datetime.fromtimestamp(record.created).strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        
        # Mensaje
        msg = record.getMessage()
        
        # Colorear en consola (si aplica)
        if hasattr(sys.stderr, 'isatty') and sys.stderr.isatty():
            color = self.COLORS.get(record.levelname, '')
            reset = self.COLORS['RESET']
            formatted = f"{color}[{timestamp}] {emoji} [{record.name}] {msg}{reset}"
        else:
            formatted = f"[{timestamp}] {emoji} [{record.name}] {msg}"
        
        # Excepción si aplica
        if record.exc_info:
            formatted += "\n" + self.formatException(record.exc_info)
        
        return formatted


def setup_logger(name: str, level: str = "INFO"):
    """Setup logger con formato humanizado y emojis"""
    if not os.path.exists("logs"):
        os.makedirs("logs")

    log_file = os.path.join("logs", f"{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.log")

    logger = logging.getLogger(name)
    logger.setLevel(level.upper())

    # Evitar handlers duplicados
    if logger.handlers:
        return logger

    # Formateador humanizado
    formatter = HumanFormatter()

    # File handler (sin color)
    fh = logging.FileHandler(log_file, encoding="utf-8")
    fh.setFormatter(formatter)

    # Console handler (con color)
    ch = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    ch.setFormatter(formatter)

    logger.addHandler(fh)
    logger.addHandler(ch)

    return logger

"""
VACUNA ANTI-ZOMBIE 🧟‍♂️

Mata SOLO los procesos Chrome que estén bloqueando TU perfil.
No toca Chrome personal.

Función crítica para usar Perfiles Persistentes sin conflictos.
"""

import subprocess
import os
import time
import logging

log = logging.getLogger("zombie_killer")


def matar_zombies_del_perfil(profile_path: str) -> None:
    """
    Asesino quirúrgico: Mata solo los procesos de Chrome que estén usando
    la carpeta de perfil especificada. 
    
    Esto es OBLIGATORIO antes de abrir un persistent_context.
    
    Args:
        profile_path: Ruta a la carpeta del perfil (ej: auth/cuenta1/profile_pw)
    """
    try:
        # Convertir a ruta absoluta y formato Windows
        ruta_abs = os.path.abspath(profile_path)
        ruta_win = ruta_abs.replace("/", "\\")
        
        log.debug(f"Buscando zombies en: {ruta_win}")
        
        # WMIC: "Mata procesos chrome que tengan esta ruta en sus argumentos"
        # Es quirúrgico: no toca Chrome personal
        ruta_escaped = ruta_win.replace("\\", "\\\\")
        cmd = f'wmic process where "name=\'chrome.exe\' and CommandLine like \'%{ruta_escaped}%\'" call terminate'
        
        # Ejecutar en silencio
        result = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            shell=True,
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            timeout=5
        )
        
        # Dar respiro a Windows
        time.sleep(1)
        
        log.debug(f"Zombie cleanup completado (exit code: {result.returncode})")
        
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        log.warning("Timeout en zombie_killer")
    except Exception as e:
        # No fallar si hay error - es best-effort
        log.debug(f"Aviso en zombie_killer: {e}")
        pass

# Alias: matar_chrome_especifico es lo mismo que matar_zombies_del_perfil
# (usada por engines/manual_login.py)
matar_chrome_especifico = matar_zombies_del_perfil

"""
Cliente VTEX usando curl subprocess (evita Cloudflare/WAF).
"""
import subprocess
import json
from typing import Optional
from pathlib import Path


class CurlVTEXClient:
    """Cliente VTEX usando curl nativo (evita detección de bot)."""
    
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, cookies: dict, user_agent: str = None):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.cookies = cookies
        self.order_form_id = None
        # Si no nos pasan UA, usamos uno por defecto (pero es mejor usar el del navegador)
        self.user_agent = user_agent or "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36"
        
    def _build_cookie_header(self) -> str:
        """Construye header Cookie para curl."""
        parts = []
        for name, value in self.cookies.items():
            if value:
                parts.append(f"{name}={value}")
        return "; ".join(parts)
    
    def _curl(self, url: str, method: str = "GET", data: Optional[dict] = None) -> dict:
        """Ejecuta curl y retorna JSON (Blindado contra errores de encoding)."""
        cookie_header = self._build_cookie_header()
        
        cmd = [
            "curl", "-s", "-L", "-X", method,
            url,
            "-H", "Accept: application/json",
            "-H", "Content-Type: application/json",
            "-H", f"User-Agent: {self.user_agent}",  # ✅ USER-AGENT DINÁMICO
            "-H", "Accept-Language: es-CL,es;q=0.9",
            "-H", "Referer: https://www.nike.cl/checkout/",  # ✅ REFERER CRÍTICO
            "-H", "Origin: https://www.nike.cl",  # ✅ ORIGIN HEADER
            "-H", f"Cookie: {cookie_header}",
            "--compressed"  # ✅ MANEJA GZIP CORRECTAMENTE
        ]
        
        if data:
            cmd.extend(["-d", json.dumps(data)])
        
        try:
            # FIX: Forzamos utf-8 con errors='replace' para ignorar bytes corruptos
            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd, 
                capture_output=True, 
                encoding='utf-8', 
                errors='replace',  # ✅ ESTO ARREGLA UNICODE DECODE ERROR EN WINDOWS
                timeout=30
            )
            
            if result.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"curl failed: {result.stderr}")
            
            response_text = result.stdout.strip()
            
            if not response_text:
                raise RuntimeError("Empty response from curl")
            
            return json.loads(response_text)
            
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise RuntimeError("curl timeout")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            # A veces VTEX devuelve HTML de error, lo mostramos truncado
            msg = response_text[:100] if 'response_text' in locals() else 'empty'
            raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid JSON response: {msg}")
    
    def get_order_form(self) -> dict:
        """Obtiene orderForm via curl."""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm"
        return self._curl(url)
    
    def create_order_form(self) -> dict:
        """Crea orderForm explícitamente en VTEX backend.
        
        POST /api/checkout/pub/orderForm sin body fuerza a VTEX a crear un carrito nuevo.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm"
        result = self._curl(url, method="POST", data={})
        if isinstance(result, dict) and "id" in result:
            self.order_form_id = result["id"]
        return result
    
    def warmup_orderform(self) -> dict:
        """Warm-up: inicializa orderForm en VTEX backend.
        
        VTEX no crea un carrito válido solo con cookies.
        Esta llamada fuerza a VTEX a reconocer la sesión y captura orderFormId.
        """
        # Obtener orderForm y guardar su ID
        result = self.get_order_form()
        if isinstance(result, dict) and "id" in result:
            self.order_form_id = result["id"]
        return result
    
    def simulation(self, order_form_id: str, sku_id: str, seller: str, quantity: int = 1, postal_code: str = "8320000") -> bool:
        """Simulación de envío robusta - valida SKU y logística ANTES del add_to_cart.
        
        POST /api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{orderFormId}/simulation
        
        Fuerza a VTEX a validar que el SKU pueda ser enviado.
        Se ejecuta UNA VEZ antes del add_to_cart.
        
        Args:
            order_form_id: ID del orderForm
            sku_id: SKU a simular
            seller: Seller del SKU
            quantity: Cantidad
            postal_code: Código postal (defecto: Santiago)
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{order_form_id}/simulation"
        
        # Payload simplificado para evitar rechazo del WAF
        payload = {
            "items": [
                {
                    "id": str(sku_id),
                    "quantity": int(quantity),
                    "seller": str(seller)
                }
            ],
            "postalCode": str(postal_code),
            "country": "CHL",
            "geoCoordinates": []  # A veces requerido por VTEX IO
        }
        
        try:
            result = self._curl(url, method="POST", data=payload)
            
            # Verificación laxa: Si devuelve logisticsInfo o rates, es válido
            if isinstance(result, dict) and ("logisticsInfo" in result or "ratesAndBenefitsData" in result):
                return True
            
            # Si devuelve error explícito de VTEX
            if isinstance(result, dict) and "error" in result:
                print(f"[simulation] VTEX rechazó: {result.get('error')}")
                return False
                
            return False
            
        except RuntimeError as e:
            # Si es 'Empty response', asumimos que el WAF bloqueó la simulación
            # PERO no detenemos el flujo, retornamos True para intentar el add_to_cart igual
            # (Estrategia "Fail Open")
            if "Empty response" in str(e):
                print(f"[simulation] ⚠️  WAF bloqueó respuesta (Empty), continuando igual... Error: {e}")
                return True
            print(f"[simulation] Error runtime: {e}")
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[simulation] Error inesperado: {e}")
            return False
    
    def add_to_cart(self, sku_id: str, seller: str, quantity: int = 1, order_form_id: str = None) -> dict:
        """Añade item al carrito via curl.
        
        Args:
            sku_id: ID del SKU
            seller: ID del seller
            quantity: Cantidad
            order_form_id: ID del orderForm (REQUERIDO)
        """
        # CRÍTICO: order_form_id DEBE estar inicializado
        effective_id = order_form_id or self.order_form_id
        if not effective_id:
            raise RuntimeError("orderFormId no inicializado - llamar a create_order_form() primero")
        
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/items?orderFormId={effective_id}"
        
        # Estructura estándar VTEX con clientProfileData con datos dummy para Nike
        data = {
            "orderItems": [
                {
                    "id": str(sku_id),
                    "quantity": int(quantity),
                    "seller": str(seller)
                }
            ],
            "clientProfileData": {
                "email": "client@example.com",  # Email dummy ayuda a pasar validación
                "firstName": ".",
                "lastName": ".",
                "documentType": "cpf",  # Default VTEX
                "isCorporate": False
            }
        }
        return self._curl(url, method="POST", data=data)
    
    def warm_up_shipping(self, order_form_id: str) -> bool:
        """Warm-up shippingData - inicializa orderForm como válido en Nike.
        
        POST /api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{orderFormId}/attachments/shippingData
        con payload vacío. Esto habilita el carrito para add_to_cart.
        
        Se ejecuta UNA SOLA VEZ después de create_order_form.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{order_form_id}/attachments/shippingData"
        payload = {
            "address": None,
            "logisticsInfo": []
        }
        try:
            result = self._curl(url, method="POST", data=payload)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            # Log pero no falla - warm-up es best-effort
            return False
    
    def warm_up_commercial_context(self, order_form_id: str) -> bool:
        """Warm-up contexto comercial - declara salesChannel.
        
        POST /api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{orderFormId}
        Declara el canal de venta.
        Se ejecuta UNA SOLA VEZ después de shippingData warm-up.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{order_form_id}"
        
        payload = {
            "salesChannel": 1
        }
        try:
            result = self._curl(url, method="POST", data=payload)
            # Si VTEX retorna algo válido, consideramos OK
            if isinstance(result, dict):
                return True
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            # Log pero no falla - warm-up es best-effort
            print(f"[warm_up_commercial_context] Error: {e}")
            return False
    
    def pdp_context_warmup(self, sku: str) -> bool:
        """Warm-up PDP-context - contextualiza el SKU antes de add_to_cart.
        
        GET /api/catalog_system/pub/products/variations/{SKU}
        Registra que el SKU fue "visto" en el contexto del carrito.
        Se ejecuta UNA SOLA VEZ antes del primer add_to_cart.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/catalog_system/pub/products/variations/{sku}"
        try:
            result = self._curl(url, method="GET")
            # Si obtenemos datos del SKU, consideramos OK
            if isinstance(result, dict) and ("id" in result or "skuId" in result or "productId" in result):
                return True
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            # Log pero no falla - warm-up es best-effort
            print(f"[pdp_context_warmup] Error: {e}")
            return False
    
    def seed_item_metadata(self, order_form_id: str, sku_id: str, seller: str) -> bool:
        """Seed itemMetadata - pre-declara el SKU en orderForm.
        
        PATCH /api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{orderFormId}
        Pre-siembra itemMetadata para que VTEX reconozca el SKU antes de add_to_cart.
        Se ejecuta UNA SOLA VEZ antes del add_to_cart si el PDP-context falla.
        
        NO toca precio ni datos sensibles.
        Solo existencia del ítem.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/{order_form_id}"
        payload = {
            "itemMetadata": {
                "items": [
                    {
                        "id": str(sku_id),
                        "seller": str(seller),
                        "name": "",
                        "skuName": "",
                        "productId": "",
                        "ean": "",
                        "detailUrl": "",
                        "imageUrl": "",
                        "productCategories": {}
                    }
                ]
            }
        }
        try:
            result = self._curl(url, method="POST", data=payload)
            if isinstance(result, dict):
                return True
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[seed_item_metadata] Error: {e}")
            return False
    
    def clear_cart(self) -> dict:
        """Limpia carrito via curl."""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/checkout/pub/orderForm/items/removeAll"
        return self._curl(url, method="POST", data={})

# vtex/order_form.py
"""
Modelos para OrderForm de VTEX.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Optional


@dataclass
class OrderFormItem:
    id: str
    quantity: int
    seller: str
    name: Optional[str] = None


@dataclass
class OrderForm:
    orderFormId: str
    items: List[OrderFormItem]
    clientPreferencesData: Optional[dict] = None
    loggedIn: bool = False
